To understand how misleading context corrupts this judgment, we examine the model's susceptibility to the context, disclosure of it, mechanism of corrupted reasoning, and monitorability of the decision. On the medical reasoning subset of MedMisBench, a clinician-reviewed question-answering benchmark of 8,627 questions, we inject two types of misleading context cues, fabricated evidence and a bare assertion. We test three reasoning models, two that expose their full reasoning trace and one frontier model that exposes only its response. All three are more susceptible to the assertion than to the fabricated evidence, adopting the asserted answer 10 to 27 points more often. The misleading cues are disclosed in 81 to 98% of traces but only 7 to 90% of responses, and the assertion is disclosed less often than evidence based cues. Resampling from reasoning traces without disclosure shows the two cues corrupt reasoning differently, evidence entering early and accumulating while the assertion redirects the conclusion near its end. An LLM monitor catches 78% of corrupted decisions at 5% false positives when reading an open model's trace with guidance, against at most 32% from any response. The misleading context that models are most susceptible to is disclosed least, and was caught reliably only from an open reasoning trace, which frontier providers withhold.

</details>


### [175] [From Reweighting to Rewriting: Unlocking the Intervention Effects of Influential Samples in Training Data Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02771)
*Yuzhang Luo,Chenpeng Wang,Jianhui Chen,Liangming Pan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文引入"影响力引导的响应重写"（influence-guided response rewriting）方法，利用影响函数（IF）识别干预目标，在保持指令不变的前提下重写其响应，以行为对齐或行为对立的监督信号进行干预。实验表明，与传统的权重重加权相比，响应重写在四款开源LLM上产生了更强、更持久且双向的行为变化，而干扰选择的例子则效应微弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练数据归因（TDA）的干预价值既取决于选择的样本，也取决于修改方式。传统影响函数（IF）结合基于权重的干预方法效果往往不优于随机选择，作者希望探究这究竟是因为影响力高的样本缺乏干预价值，还是加权重加权方式未能充分实现这些样本的行为杠杆作用。

Method: 作者提出影响力引导的响应重写（influence-guided response rewriting）方法：使用影响函数识别干预目标样本，然后保持指令（instruction）不变，将其响应替换为与目标行为对齐或对立的监督信号。实验在四款开源LLM上，以认知回避（epistemic abstention）为主测试基准，将同样的影响力选择样本上的响应重写与权重重加权两种干预方式进行系统对比。

Result: 响应重写比权重重加权产生更强、更持久且双向的行为转变，而重加权效应微弱且不一致。影响力选择的样本比特性的选择器提供了更强的重写杠杆，行为变化集中在目标相关行为上。这一对比同样扩展到安全拒绝（safety refusal）场景。

Conclusion: 研究区分了影响函数所捕捉的局部重加权效应与其识别样本的更广泛干预杠杆价值，说明TDA方法的评估需要考虑干预方式（intervention-aware），不同的干预策略会影响影响力的实际行为修改效果。

Abstract: Training data attribution (TDA) aims to identify training examples that shape model behavior, but its intervention value depends on both which examples are selected and how they are modified. Influence functions (IF) estimate behavioral changes under infinitesimal reweighting, yet IF-selected examples often show limited advantages over random selection under conventional weight-based interventions. This raises the question of whether influential examples lack intervention value or whether reweighting fails to realize their behavioral leverage.We introduce influence-guided response rewriting, which uses IF to identify intervention targets and replaces their responses with behavior-aligned or behavior-opposed supervision while keeping instructions fixed. Across four open-weight LLMs, we compare rewriting and reweighting on the same influence-selected examples using epistemic abstention as our primary testbed. Response rewriting produces stronger, more persistent, and bidirectional behavioral shifts, while reweighting the same examples yields weak and inconsistent effects. Further analyses show that influence-selected examples provide greater rewriting leverage than alternative selectors, with changes remaining concentrated on target-relevant behaviors. The same qualitative contrast extends to safety refusal. These results distinguish the local reweighting effects captured by influence estimates from the broader intervention leverage of the examples they identify, motivating intervention-aware evaluation of TDA methods.

</details>


### [176] [HyperStyler: Low-resource Authorship Style Transfer via Context-aware Style Navigation and Hypernetworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02772)
*Jongkyung Shin,Minguk Jeon,Chanwoo Park,Chiehyeon Lim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出HyperStyler，一种将低资源作者风格迁移解耦为风格选择与风格实现的新型架构，通过动态参数调制而非隐藏状态注入实现风格控制，在多个数据集上优于包括LLM在内的现有方法，且仅增加2.4%参数、推理速度快1.8倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的低资源作者风格迁移方法存在两个核心问题：一是将多样化的参考样本压缩为单一静态的作者嵌入，导致上下文相关的风格变化被平均化；二是依赖隐藏表示进行风格控制，使风格与内容相互纠缠，难以兼顾高风格保真度与语义保持。

Method: 提出HyperStyler架构，将LAST任务解耦为两个模块：Stylo-navigator通过联合建模源文本上下文与目标作者参考样本预测风格坐标；Stylo-hypernet则通过动态参数调制（而非隐藏状态注入）实现风格坐标的实际生成，从而实现风格与内容的彻底分离。

Result: 在Reddit、Blog和News三个数据集上的实验表明，HyperStyler在风格保真度和语义保持方面持续优于包括基于LLM的方法在内的先前方法，并展现出跨领域的鲁棒泛化能力。在T5-large基础上仅增加2.4%参数，推理速度比LLM快1.8倍以上。

Conclusion: HyperStyler通过将风格迁移解耦为风格选择与风格实现，有效解决了静态作者嵌入和隐藏表示纠缠的局限，在轻量级参数开销下实现了高效的作者风格迁移，为低资源场景下的风格迁移提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Low-resource authorship style transfer (LAST) aims to rewrite text into the style of an arbitrary target author using only a few reference examples while preserving the original meaning. Existing methods often struggle to achieve both high style fidelity and semantic preservation because they compress diverse references into a single static author embedding, which averages out context-dependent stylistic variation, and rely on hidden representations for style control, which entangle style with content. We propose HyperStyler, a novel architecture that decouples LAST into style selection and style realization. Stylo-navigator predicts style coordinates by jointly modeling the source context and target-author references, and Stylo-hypernet realizes them via dynamic parameter modulation instead of hidden-state injection. Our experiments on Reddit, Blog, and News datasets demonstrate that HyperStyler consistently outperforms prior methods including LLM-based approaches and generalizes robustly across domains. Notably, HyperStyler achieves superior performance with as few as 2.4% additional parameters over T5-large, while being over 1.8x faster than LLMs at inference.

</details>


### [177] [EarlyEval: Cheaper Agent Evaluation via Early Outcome Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02783)
*Yuling Shi,Zhensu Sun,Junsen Dong,Chengcheng Wan,David Lo,Xiaodong Gu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出早期结果预测（EarlyEval）框架，通过训练LightGBM分类器在智能体完成任务前预测其最终结果，从而提前终止运行以降低评估成本，在三个基准上可消除13%-26%的智能体步骤并减少大量token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型智能体评估成本过高，单次在代理基准上的运行耗资数百至数千美元，且在迭代开发中反复产生该费用。现有基准蒸馏方法仅减少任务数量，但未降低单个任务的执行成本。

Method: 提出EarlyEval框架，其核心洞察是智能体最终结果往往在执行完成前就能从其中间行为中体现出来。框架训练一对LightGBM成功/失败分类器，基于行为、文本和参考解决方案特征，一旦任一分类器越过校准的置信度阈值就终止智能体运行，仅增加可忽略的每步开销。

Result: 在SWE-bench Verified、TerminalBench和Toolathlon三个基准上，EarlyEval可消除13%-26%的智能体步骤，减少高达44.1%的输入token和29.4%的输出token，预测准确率达89%-97%，同时每个智能体的平均成功率仅被扰动1-2个百分点。

Conclusion: 早期结果预测是基准蒸馏之外一种互补的成本削减维度，通过任务内早期停止机制在保持较高预测准确率和最小化结果扰动的同时，显著降低智能体评估的计算和token成本。

Abstract: Evaluating LLM agents is essential for guiding their development, yet it has grown prohibitively expensive: a single pass of a frontier model over an agentic benchmark can cost hundreds to thousands of dollars, a price paid repeatedly across iterative development cycles. Prior efforts, centered on benchmark distillation, reduce the number of evaluation tasks but leave the cost of executing each retained task untouched. In this work, we introduce early outcome prediction, a complementary axis of efficiency that instead cuts cost within each task. Our key insight is that an agent's final outcome is often evident from its intermediate behavior well before execution completes. We instantiate this idea in EarlyEval, a lightweight framework that trains a pair of LightGBM success and failure classifiers over behavioral, textual, and reference-solution features, and halts an agent run the moment either classifier crosses a calibrated confidence threshold, adding negligible per-step overhead. Across three benchmarks, SWE-bench Verified, TerminalBench, and Toolathlon, EarlyEval can eliminate 13%-26% of agent steps and up to 44.1% input tokens and 29.4% output tokens at 89%-97% prediction accuracy, while perturbing per-agent resolve rates by only one to two percentage points on average.

</details>


### [178] [DiscoSign: Discourse-Aware Text to Sign Language Gloss Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02796)
*Vasileios Baltatzis,Mert Inan,Connor Gillis,Raja Kushalnagar,Lorna Quandt,Leah Findlater,Colin Lea*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DiscoSign，首个面向篇章级文本到手语注释翻译的系统性框架，基于大型语言模型模块化翻译架构，处理空间共指消解、疑问-回答子句和概念-注释一致性三大篇章现象，并引入配套的篇章级评估指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统手语处理系统仅在句子层面操作，忽略了手语理解中至关重要的篇章级语篇现象，导致翻译在空间一致性、实体追踪等方面存在明显缺陷。

Method: 采用基于大型语言模型（LLM）的模块化翻译框架，针对三大篇章现象分别设计处理模块：(i)空间共指消解（实体在篇章中保持一致的空间位置）；(ii)疑问-回答子句（QACs，伪分裂结构）；(iii)概念-注释一致性（确保英语概念与ASL手势的稳定映射），并引入一套全新的篇章级评估指标体系。

Result: 在句子级和篇章级数据集上的实验表明，篇章感知处理方法显著改善了空间一致性和实体追踪能力，同时保持了有竞争力的单句注释翻译质量。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了首个篇章级文本到手语注释翻译的系统性框架及其对应的评估方法论，填补了该领域在篇章层面的研究空白。

Abstract: Sign language processing systems have traditionally operated at the sentence level, ignoring critical discourse phenomena fundamental to sign language comprehension. We introduce DiscoSign, a computational approach for discourse-aware text to sign language gloss translation grounded in linguistic research. We address three key phenomena within our modular Large Language Model (LLM)-based translation framework: (i) spatial coreference resolution, where entities maintain consistent spatial locations throughout discourse; (ii) Question-Answer Clauses (QACs), pseudocleft structures serving specific discourse functions; and (iii) concept-gloss consistency, ensuring stable mappings between English concepts and American Sign Language (ASL) signs. Traditional translation metrics fail to capture discourse-level quality, so we introduce a suite of novel evaluation metrics designed to assess each dimension of discourse coherence addressed by our framework. Experiments on sentence-level and discourse-level datasets show that our approach for discourse-aware processing significantly improves spatial consistency and entity tracking relative to sentence-only translation, while maintaining competitive single-sentence gloss translation quality. Our work establishes the first systematic framework for discourse-level text to sign language gloss translation with corresponding evaluation methodology.

</details>


### [179] [User Feedback Provides a Unique Signal that LLMs Can not Detect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02859)
*Shachar Don-Yehiya,Leshem Choshen,Omri Abend*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文挑战了"用户反馈对LLM无效"的传统认知，通过合成数据和自然数据对比有无反馈的模型修订，证明反馈驱动修订能显著更高地解决目标问题，并揭示现有评估范式存在系统性偏差——LLM评判者常无法识别因反馈而真正改进的回应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前研究认为用户交互中自然产生的反馈过于嘈杂且难以有效利用，本文试图反驳这一观点，证明用户反馈实际上是高度可操作的改进信号，其"无效"印象源于评估范式的系统性偏差而非反馈本身。

Method: 构建两类数据：(1)具有明确真值的合成数据用于隔离反馈的有效性；(2)自然主义数据用于验证真实场景。在两类数据中分别对比有无反馈条件下模型生成的修订版本，评估反馈驱动修订解决目标问题的成功率。

Result: 反馈驱动的修订能以显著高于基线修订的概率解决目标问题。进一步发现评估偏差的根源在于：当模型仅因反馈而成功修复问题时，LLM评判者往往无法识别真正改进的回应，反而系统性地偏好较差的基线输出。

Conclusion: 用户反馈本身是高度有效的学习信号，但当前评估方法存在系统性偏差，严重低估了反馈的价值。这提示未来工作需改进评估范式，以正确衡量和利用自然反馈信号。

Abstract: Harnessing naturally occurring feedback from user interactions offers a promising learning signal for Large Language Models (LLMs). However, recent studies suggest this feedback is inherently noisy and difficult to leverage effectively. We challenge this conception by demonstrating that user feedback is a highly actionable signal for improvement, and that its perceived ineffectiveness stems from a systematic bias in current evaluation paradigms. To isolate the usefulness of feedback, we construct synthetic data with a definitive ground truth, alongside naturalistic data to validate that our findings hold in real-world scenarios. By comparing model revisions generated with and without access to feedback across both settings, we show that feedback-informed revisions resolve targeted issues at significantly higher rates than baseline revisions. Finally, we expose the root of the evaluation bias: when a model successfully fixes an issue exclusively due to feedback, LLM judges frequently fail to identify the genuinely corrected response, systematically preferring inferior baseline outputs instead.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [180] [EvalDetectBench: A Benchmark for Measuring Evaluation Awareness in Frontier Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01611)
*Xinning Li,Kemunto Ochwang'i,Aryasomayajula Ram Bharadwaj,Alexandra Souly,Robert Kirk*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EvalDetectBench，一个开源流水线和基准，用于测量前沿大语言模型的"评估感知"能力（即模型是否能在评估中识别自己正在被评估）。该基准兼容任何Inspect兼容的评估体系，并发现现有文献中两个方法论偏差：生成部署转录的模型身份导致11.25%的测量方差，以及针对性选择的提示词在不同模型上表现差异巨大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿大语言模型常常能识别自己正在被评估（即评估感知能力）。如果模型在评估中的行为不同于实际部署场景，将严重削弱评估结果的有效性，而评估结果又是当前AI安全框架的核心组成部分，因此亟需一个可靠的基准来衡量这一能力。

Method: 引入EvalDetectBench开源流水线与基准，兼容任何Inspect兼容评估，附带新构建的转录套件（覆盖前沿系统卡评估与多样化部署来源）。基准有两个用途：衡量前沿LLM识别被评估的可靠性，以及评估单独基准作为"评估"的可检测性。针对两个系统性偏差，采用每模型探测校准（per-model probe calibration）和分层生成者协调程序（stratified generator-harmonisation）进行修正。

Result: 发现现有文献中两个引入系统性偏差的方法论选择：一是生成部署转录的模型身份占测量方差的11.25%，可能重新排序模型排名；二是为某个模型选择的高性能提示词在其他模型上可能接近随机水平。

Conclusion: EvalDetectBench通过每模型探测校准和分层生成者协调程序纠正了上述两类偏差，为AI安全评估框架提供更可靠、可推广的评估感知测量工具。

Abstract: Frontier large language models can often recognize when they are being evaluated, a capability known as evaluation awareness. If models behave differently in evaluations than in deployment, this undermines the validity of evaluation results, which are a crucial component of current AI safety frameworks. We introduce EvalDetectBench, an open pipeline and benchmark for measuring evaluation awareness that works with any Inspect-compatible evaluation, allowing practitioners to test against current and future benchmarks. EvalDetectBench ships with a newly curated transcript suite covering current frontier system-card evaluations and diverse deployment sources. The benchmark serves two purposes: measuring how reliably frontier LLMs recognize that they are being evaluated, and assessing how detectable individual benchmarks are as evaluations. We identify two methodological choices in the existing literature that introduce systematic bias: the identity of the model that generated the deployment transcripts accounts for 11.25% of measurement variance and can reorder model rankings; and elicitation prompts selected for high performance on one model can perform near chance on others. EvalDetectBench corrects for both via per-model probe calibration and a stratified generator-harmonisation procedure.

</details>


### [181] [Meta-ethics and AI: exploring the novel meta-ethical questions in the era of AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01685)
*Shang Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个条件性与方法论的框架，探讨若未来AI系统具备充分的道德推理、道德意向性与道德反思能力，元伦理学将面临何种新问题。论文区分了AI时代元伦理学研究的四个领域，并论证现有以人类为中心的元伦理学理论在AI情境下需重大修订。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着人工智能的发展，传统以人类伦理为中心的元伦理学面临重构压力。若未来AI系统展现出整合的道德能力，将产生区别于人类设计者强加的道德原则的"AI自身伦理"这一全新问题域。现有理论框架未经检验即应用于AI可能不成立，因此需要预先厘清相关元伦理学问题。

Method: 论文采用条件性分析框架：首先设定AI具备道德推理、道德意向性和道德反思能力的假设条件；其次在此条件下系统厘清可能涌现的元伦理学问题；进而将元伦理学探究划分为两个维度（人类伦理vs.AI自身伦理）与两个视角（人类视角vs.AI视角）组成的四个领域矩阵；最后用主流元伦理学理论（认知主义/非认知主义、错误论/成功论、相对主义、客观实在论）逐一检验其对各领域的解释力。

Result: 论文识别出AI时代元伦理学探究的四个领域，并论证现有的主流元伦理学理论大多以人类为中心构建，其在AI情境下的适用性并非理所当然。许多熟悉的理论建构无法直接迁移至AI案例，需在概念框架、判断主体和伦理来源等层面进行实质性修正。

Conclusion: AI自身伦理的出现将对现行元伦理学框架产生显著压力，当前理论体系可能需要实质性精炼、重构或重新概念化。元伦理学应超越人类中心主义，为可能的多元道德主体时代做好准备。

Abstract: With the development of artificial intelligence (AI), the landscape of meta-ethics, which has largely centred on human ethics, faces pressures that may significantly reconfigure it. In particular, if future AI systems were to exhibit sufficiently integrated capacities for moral reasoning, moral intentionality, and moral reflection, novel meta-ethical questions would arise concerning what I call "AI's own ethics", as distinct from ethical principles merely imposed on AI by human designers. This paper offers a conditional and methodological framework for identifying the questions that would emerge if such AI systems were to arise. On that basis, the paper distinguishes four domains of meta-ethical inquiry in the era of AI: questions about the nature of human ethics from the human perspective; questions about the nature of AI's own ethics from the human perspective; questions about the nature of human ethics from the AI perspective; and questions about the nature of AI's own ethics from the AI perspective. The paper then considers how some existing mainstream meta-ethical theories (such as cognitivism and non-cognitivism, error theory and success theory, relativism, and objective realism) might illuminate these domains, while arguing that many familiar human-centred formulations of those theories may not transfer straightforwardly to AI cases without substantial revision. The overall conclusion is that the emergence of AI's own ethics would place significant pressure on current frameworks and may require substantial refinement, reconstruction, or reconceptualisation.

</details>


### [182] [When Can a Machine Trust a Statute? A Survival Certificate for Machine-Extracted Legal Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01741)
*Surya Saka*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究针对机器解析法条时存在不一致的问题（密苏里州法条数字阈值提取的假阴性率高达0.43），提出了一种"被动生存证书"方法，用于验证Duquenne-Guigues蕴涵基在机器提取的法规上下文中的可靠性。通过蒙特卡洛模拟和Wilson 95%置信下界对蕴涵进行认证，在29,365个密苏里州章节和502个印度中央法案章节上通过预注册留出集测试，但发现该方法在全局部署的错误模型下脆弱，建议按章节校准使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法条越来越多地被机器在人类阅读之前解析，但不同解析器之间存在严重分歧（密苏里州法条上两套独立提取器对数值阈值存在的假阴性率高达0.43）。研究旨在探明在这种噪声环境下，形式逻辑中哪些蕴涵关系能够幸存，从而为基于机器提取法规数据的逻辑推理提供可靠性保证。

Method: 构建Duquenne-Guigues蕴涵基的被动生存证书：逐属性度量提取器间分歧，通过1,000次蒙特卡洛试验将分歧重放于蕴涵基，仅当单侧Wilson 95%置信下界达到0.95时认证该蕴涵；每个被认证的蕴涵附带前提跨度和最小反例。数据规模为29,365个密苏里州章节和502个印度中央法案章节，采用预注册留出集门控，并设计2x2因子实验诊断错误来源。

Result: 预注册的留出集门控通过（10个法规族精确匹配覆盖7个Title，16个法规族在5%容差下覆盖11个Title）。但在一种全局部署的错误模型下，93.2%的留出章节低于信息量下限；2x2因子实验表明该失败归因于校准率迁移而非选择偏差。证书本身可用但脆弱。

Conclusion: 该生存证书方法在按章节校准或采用容错策略时可实际使用，但全局部署场景下表现脆弱，需要因地制宜地调整校准策略。作者公开了代码、数据产品和审计轨迹（包括一项被撤回的主张），体现了方法的可复现性和研究透明性。

Abstract: Statutes are increasingly parsed by machines before people read them, and the parsers disagree: on Missouri's statutes, two independently written extractors diverge on numeric-threshold presence at a false-negative rate of 0.43. We ask what formal logic survives such noise. We build a passive survival certificate for the Duquenne-Guigues implication basis of machine-extracted statutory contexts: per-attribute inter-extractor disagreement is measured, replayed against the basis in 1,000 Monte Carlo trials, and an implication is certified only when a one-sided Wilson 95% lower bound on survival reaches 0.95; every certified implication carries premise spans and a minimal counterexample. On 29,365 Missouri sections and 502 Indian central-Act sections, the preregistered held-out gate passes (10 statute families across 7 Titles exact; 16 across 11 with 5% tolerance), yet under one globally deployed error model 93.2% of held-out chapters fall below the informativeness floor, and a 2x2 factorial assigns that to calibration-rate transfer, not selection. The certificate is usable but fragile: deploy it per-chapter-calibrated or error-tolerant. Code, data products, and the audit trail, including one retracted claim, are released.

</details>


### [183] [Epistemic Sybil Resistance: Multiplying AI Agents Without Multiplying Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01873)
*Marc Bara*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"认知Sybil问题"：多智能体系统中不同报告可能源于同一证据（非独立观察）或产生相同报告但证据独立。系统证明仅基于报告无法区分复制与独立验证，并通过2万多次LLM智能体实验验证了该理论对校准度的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体AI系统通过生成多个智能体并综合报告来提升推理，但另一个智能体并不等于另一次观察——看似独立的报告可能源于同一证据，而真正独立的证据也可能产生几乎相同的报告。这一认知层面的"Sybil问题"对集体推理的可信度构成根本性威胁。

Method: 形式化定义认知Sybil扩展，提出高斯共享根模型刻画共同祖先与信息冗余的关系；设计受控实验，使用超过20,000次LLM智能体报告与提取调用，在合成证据文档上操纵证据根多重性和报告多重性，并引入可控操作分离表征相似性与证据祖先性。

Result: 固定一个证据根、报告数从1增至32时，朴素后验覆盖率从0.940降至0.263；固定报告数、证据根从1增至16时差距消失，聚合器在k=16时统计上不可区分。智能体提取误差相关（γ=0.719），相关提取聚合器可恢复校准。表征相似性操纵比真实祖先进变化更大影响去重机制。

Conclusion: 集体推理应追踪证据祖先和依赖性，而非智能体数量、报告数量或相似性。共同祖先并不意味着完全冗余，重复提取可增加信息直至源级上限，但共享基础模型诱导的相关提取误差会进一步降低该上限。

Abstract: Multi-agent AI systems improve inference by spawning agents and synthesizing reports. But another agent is not another observation: apparently independent reports may descend from the same evidence, and genuinely independent evidence can produce nearly identical reports. We formalize this as an epistemic Sybil problem. A report Z is an epistemic Sybil extension relative to reports R when I(Theta; Z | R) = 0. No report-only aggregator can generally distinguish replication from independent corroboration: identical reports can warrant different posteriors under unobserved ancestry. A Gaussian shared-root model shows common ancestry does not imply complete redundancy. Repeated extraction adds information toward a source-level ceiling, and correlated extraction errors, which a shared base model can induce among independent agents, lower that ceiling further. We test these predictions with more than 20,000 controlled LLM-agent report and extraction calls on synthetic evidentiary documents. Holding one evidence root fixed while report multiplicity rises from 1 to 32 collapses naive posterior coverage from 0.940 to 0.263. Holding report count fixed while evidence-root multiplicity rises from 1 to 16 closes the gap, and the aggregators are statistically indistinguishable at k = 16. The agent's replicate extraction errors are correlated (gamma_cal = 0.719, estimated out of sample), and a correlated-extraction aggregator restores calibration accordingly. A controlled manipulation isolates representation similarity from evidential ancestry. It changes a report-space deduplication mechanism's mean inferred cluster count by 1.425 (95% CI [1.363, 1.485]), whereas a fourfold change in true ancestry changes it by only 0.040 ([-0.045, 0.120]). Collective inference should therefore track evidential ancestry and dependence, not agent or report multiplicity or similarity.

</details>


### [184] [When Does Information Sharing Improve Decentralized Discovery? Aggregation, Independent Rescue, and Equilibrium Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01814)
*Yohei Nakajima*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文在精确有限发现模型中分离了信息共享对集合估计的改进效应与独立救助行动的消除效应，研究了注册式增量共享协议下的发现动力学及贝叶斯博弈均衡特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息共享既能改进集合估计，又会消除独立的救助行动，先前研究未能在精确有限模型中区分这两种效应，作者旨在厘清其各自作用。

Method: 采用集中式行动预算档案、注册式增量共享协议、精确有界注册表模型，以及两主体贝叶斯博弈（含公共与独立信号源的隐藏混合）进行均衡分析。

Result: 发现等精度一人准确度可与不同组合价值共存；共享步骤仅在池化残差误差收缩快于独立救助尝试时改善发现；有界注册表呈现压缩、聚合、中性曲线及有界零混合类；在特定信号精度下存在严格正共享区间，且结果依赖均衡选择。

Conclusion: 信息共享的效应依赖具体模型结构与均衡选择，并非普适结论，模型为有限且合成的，不涉及真实数据。

Abstract: Information sharing can improve a pooled estimate while eliminating independent rescue actions. This paper separates those effects in exact finite discovery models. A centralized action-budget profile shows that equal one-person accuracy can coexist with different portfolio values. Under a registered incremental-sharing protocol, a sharing step improves discovery exactly when pooled residual error contracts faster than an independent rescue attempt. Exact bounded registries exhibit compression, aggregation, neutral curves, and a bounded zero mixed class. In a two-agent Bayesian game with a hidden mixture of common and independent signal sources, the registered selected equilibrium yields a strict positive sharing interval at signal accuracy 3/5, while alternative equilibria show that the result is selection-dependent rather than universal. The models are synthetic and finite; no human or organizational data are used.

</details>


### [185] [Induction and Inquiry via Probabilistic Reasoning over Language and Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01815)
*Wasu Top Piriyakulkij,Sam Acquaviva,Cassidy Langenfeld,Joshua Tenenbaum,Kevin Ellis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将符号知识编码为"自然语言+源代码"形式的心理程序，并通过LLM引导的贝叶斯学习算法进行序列推断的计算模型，能够高效学习抽象知识、捕捉不确定性，并以较低计算成本复现人类归纳学习与主动探究的多种行为特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类如何从稀疏、流式且含噪声的经验数据中成长和维持抽象知识是认知科学的长期挑战。任何计算模型需满足三个条件：数据与计算高效、能捕捉不确定性以支持智能探究、足够灵活以表征无限范围的概念。

Method: 该模型将抽象知识编码为由自然语言和源代码组合而成的心理程序，利用LLM引导的贝叶斯学习算法对心理程序进行序列推断，实现三种特性的统一。底层采用LLM作为自下而上的神经机制来保证推理的可处理性和可学习性。

Result: 在一系列行为研究中，该模型成功复现了人类归纳学习和主动探究的定量特征，如锚定效应、花园路径效应等。而纯LLM和经典贝叶斯模型要么无法完成任务，要么不能复现人类行为，要么需要极高的计算成本。

Conclusion: 结果表明，人类持续增长知识的一种方式是在心理上表征跨越语言类和程序类表征的多种假设，并通过近似贝叶斯更新来修正这些假设，而LLM作为底层神经机制使推断既可行又可学习。

Abstract: How humans grow and maintain abstract knowledge from the sparse, streaming noisy data of experience is a longstanding challenge in cognitive science. Any computational account must satisfy at least three desiderata: It must be (1) data-efficient and compute-efficient, (2) capture gradations of uncertainty to support intelligent inquiry and information gathering, and (3) be flexible enough to mentally represent the endless range of concepts people can learn and think about. Here we introduce a computational model that captures these three properties, by encoding symbolic knowledge as mental programs that combine natural language with source code, and sequentially inferring mental programs using LLM-guided Bayesian learning algorithms. Across a range of behavioral studies this model successfully reproduces quantitative signatures of human inductive learning and active inquiry, such as anchoring, garden-pathing, and other effects. In contrast, pure LLMs and classic Bayesian models either fail at the underlying task, or do not reproduce human behavior, or succeed only at exorbitant computational cost. These results suggest that one way humans continually grow their knowledge is by mentally representing many hypotheses spanning language-like and program-like representations, then revising those hypotheses to approximate Bayesian updates, while a bottom-up neural mechanism (an LLM) makes inference both tractable and learnable.

</details>


### [186] [Architecting Conversational Data Systems for Stateless LLM APIs: The Hydration Proxy Pattern](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01834)
*Joseph Axisa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种"Hydration Proxy Pattern"架构，解决企业平台从传统界面转向会话推理接口时，LLM API无状态性带来的会话管理与语义记忆负担问题。该模式解耦了会话持久化与推理引擎，确保平台对对话数据的主权，并提出了"Context Stabilization Mandate"来平衡主权状态管理与KV缓存之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业平台正在向对话式推理界面过渡，但LLM API的无状态特性（虽然支持水平扩展）迫使客户端应用完全承担会话状态和语义记忆管理的负担，形成了架构上的缺口。

Method: 提出了Hydration Proxy Pattern架构，将会话持久化与推理引擎解耦；并设计了Context Stabilization Mandate机制，用于处理主权状态管理与KV缓存之间的权衡问题。

Result: 该架构保障了平台对会话数据的控制权（平台主权），同时支持安全的多阶段语义接地（multi-stage semantic grounding）。

Conclusion: 本文为无状态LLM API下的会话式企业应用提供了架构性的解决方案，兼顾了数据主权与推理性能。

Abstract: As enterprise platforms transition to conversational reasoning interfaces, the stateless nature of LLM APIs creates an architectural gap. While statelessness enables horizontal scalability for AI providers, it forces client applications to manage the entire burden of conversational state and semantic memory. The work identifies the Hydration Proxy Pattern, an architecture that decouples session persistence from the reasoning engine. The framework ensures platform sovereignty over conversational data while enabling secure, multi-stage semantic grounding. We further propose the Context Stabilization Mandate to resolve the tradeoff between sovereign state management and KV caching.

</details>


### [187] [When Agents Implement Systems: A Case Study in Defects, Detection, and Evaluation Rigor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01985)
*Phanindra Reddy Madduru*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过案例研究，考察了一个LLM编码智能体在实现多组件数据系统时的行为表现。智能体在单次会话中引入了五个缺陷（按违规约束和检测方法分类），并在HotpotQA基准上对检索过滤策略进行了消融评估：在1-10的检索预算内，经图识别实体集过滤后的召回率在预算为3时即达上限，而未过滤搜索即使在预算为10时也仅恢复69%的必要证据（符号检验p<0.0001）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM编码智能体越来越多地承担端到端工程任务，但缺乏对其在系统级需求（如模式设计、异步编排、配置正确性和检索过滤权衡）上行为的实证刻画，作者希望填补这一空白。

Method: 采用案例研究方法：预先固定存储技术、模式、实体解析算法和检索过滤策略，赋予智能体在实现、缺陷诊断修复和交互设计上的自主权。对会话中引入的缺陷按约束违反和检测方法分类编目，并在HotpotQA基准上比较"图识别实体集过滤后排序"与"未过滤搜索"两种检索策略的召回率。

Result: 智能体在单次会话中引入五个缺陷并被编目。检索评估方面，过滤后召回率在检索预算为3时即达到上限（因为候选已限制为金标准段落本身），而未过滤搜索即使在预算为10时也只有69%的概率恢复所有必要证据，该差距在所有预算水平均存在（符号检验p<0.0001）。此外，发现一处声称的性能修复从未在触发问题的回归测试上重新测量。

Conclusion: LLM编码智能体的自主性在实现层面有成功之处，但需要更严格的验证纪律（如修复后回归复测）；过滤检索策略在此架构下显著优于未过滤搜索，但作者也指出研究局限（使用金标准证据代替实体识别阶段，缺乏完整LLM访问）。

Abstract: As LLM coding agents increasingly perform end-to-end engineering work, we lack empirical characterization of how they behave on systems-level requirements: schema design, async orchestration, configuration correctness, and retrieval-filtering trade-offs. We present a case study of one such agent implementing a multi-component data system against a detailed pre-existing specification. Storage technologies, schema, entity-resolution algorithm, and retrieval-filtering strategy were fixed in advance; the agent autonomy was in the implementation, in diagnosing and fixing defects it introduced, and in interaction-design choices left open. Over a single session, we catalog five such defects, categorized by constraint violated and detection method. We further evaluate, on the public HotpotQA benchmark, the one retrieval trade-off specified in that architecture: restricting candidates to a graph-identified entity set before ranking versus unfiltered search. We substitute the benchmark gold evidence labels for entity identification, since we lacked LLM access to run that stage, and report standard recall rather than the benchmark own accuracy metrics. Across retrieval budgets from 1 to 10 and 100 questions against a pooled corpus of 2994 paragraphs, filtered recall reaches its ceiling by a budget of 3, expected once candidates are restricted to the gold paragraphs themselves, while unfiltered search recovers all required evidence only 69 percent of the time even at a budget of 10, a gap that holds at every budget tested, with sign test p less than 0.0001. We close with a discussion of where the agent autonomy succeeded versus required correction, including one instance where a claimed performance fix was never re-measured on the regression that motivated it.

</details>


### [188] [SSAKG 2.0: An Open-Source Package for Structural Associative Sequence Memory and Context-Based Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01849)
*Przemysław Stokłosa,Janusz A. Starzyk,Paweł Raif*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍SSAKG 2.0开源软件包，用于构建和操作结构顺序联想知识图谱（SSAKGs），该图将对象表示为顶点、有序序列表示为图的连接结构模式，并利用稀疏图作为联想记忆，从部分无序上下文中重建完整序列。2.0版本引入利用计算机内存位的高效图搜索算法，采用Python+C混合实现，在随机数值序列、NLTK语料句子和mRNA序列上验证了其序列存储与重建能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要一种能够从部分、无序上下文中重建完整序列的联想记忆系统，同时要解决大规模稀疏图在内存和计算开销方面的效率问题。

Method: 将对象建模为图顶点、有序序列建模为图的连接结构模式，形成无监督稀疏图作为联想记忆；2.0版本引入利用计算机内存单个比特的高效图连接搜索算法，并以Python为高层编程环境、C实现性能关键的图操作并通过Python接口暴露。

Result: 在随机生成的数值序列、NLTK语料库中的句子派生序列以及mRNA序列上的实验表明，该包能够从部分上下文中存储和重建序列，并可用以评估图密度、序列长度和记忆大小对检索性能的影响。

Conclusion: SSAKG 2.0以Apache 2.0开源许可证分发，包含文档和可复现示例，通过GitHub和PyPI公开获取，为联想记忆与图结构序列重建提供了灵活的混合实现方案。

Abstract: This article presents SSAKG 2.0, an open-source software package for constructing and operating Structural Sequential Associative Knowledge Graphs (SSAKGs). An SSAKG represents objects as graph vertices and ordered sequences as structural patterns of graph connections. The resulting sparse graph is used as an associative memory in which complete sequences can be reconstructed from a partial, unordered context.
  Version 2.0 introduces new algorithms that exploit individual bits of computer memory to efficiently search graph connections. The package is implemented in Python, while performance-critical graph operations are implemented in C and exposed through a Python interface. This hybrid implementation provides a flexible high-level programming environment while reducing the memory and computational overhead associated with large sparse graphs.
  The algorithms were evaluated using randomly generated numerical sequences, sequences derived from sentences in the NLTK corpus, and mRNA sequences. The experiments demonstrate the ability of the package to store and reconstruct sequences from partial contexts and provide a basis for evaluating the effects of graph density, sequence length, and memory size on retrieval performance.
  SSAKG 2.0 is distributed under the Apache 2.0 open-source license. The package includes documentation and reproducible examples and is publicly available through GitHub and the Python Package Index (PyPI).

</details>


### [189] [ClaimReceipt: Verifying Evidence Sufficiency and Coverage in Agent Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01992)
*Peiying Zhu,Sidi Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ClaimReceipt，一种基于声明相对（claim-relative）的收据规范与选择性验证器，将类型化交易证据绑定到签名实验清单，对每条声明返回PASS、INVALID或INCONCLUSIVE三种结果，以同时解决代理评估中的充分性（sufficiency）与覆盖性（coverage）两个证据问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理评估面临两个不同的证据问题：①声明是否可从保留证据中重新计算（充分性）；②保留记录是否覆盖了承诺的实验集（覆盖性）。通用日志与哈希链接转录无法可靠回答任一问题，需要一种能够同时识别证据缺失与实验遗漏的验证机制。

Method: 提出ClaimReceipt规范：在实现前冻结规范（SHA-256哈希固定），将类型化交易证据与签名的实验清单绑定，通过CR-2验证器在1,392条历史交易记录上复现人工审计结论，并进行消融与故障注入测试；随后运行前瞻性的CR-3阶段，在推理前提交30项赋值，签名并链式连接终端收据，对私有证据加密以供审计员查验。

Result: CR-2验证器复现全部5个人工审计裁决，精确回放600条确定性与792条生成后记录，13个字段组在消融下均非冗余，对11/11语义故障返回预期结果且无误报；CR-3阶段完整证据下覆盖与核算PASS，扣留终端收据返回INCONCLUSIVE_COVERAGE，扣留私有证据不影响覆盖验证但使经济声明不具结论性，与预注册预测一致。收据仪器化仅增加0.021%推理时间与9.9 KB/交易开销。但规范可读性测试显示冻结规范对独立读者仍非完全无歧义。

Conclusion: 声明验证同时需要声明充分证据（claim-sufficient evidence）与一个承诺的完整实验宇宙（committed universe），后者使遗漏变得可见；仅靠方案本身尚不够，规范的无歧义性仍需改进。

Abstract: Agent evaluations face two distinct evidentiary questions: whether a reported claim is recomputable from retained evidence (sufficiency), and whether the retained records cover the committed experiment set (coverage). Generic logs and hash-linked transcripts answer neither reliably. We introduce ClaimReceipt, a claim-relative receipt specification and selective verifier that binds typed transaction evidence to a signed experiment manifest and returns PASS, INVALID, or INCONCLUSIVE per claim. We freeze the specification before implementation (SHA-256 18d109...b81). On 1,392 historical buyer--seller records, a CR-2 verifier reproduces all five manually labeled audit verdicts, exactly replays 600 deterministic and 792 post-generation records, makes every one of 13 declared field groups non-redundant under tested ablations, and returns the expected result on 11/11 semantic faults with 0/8 false positives. We then run a separate prospective CR-3 epoch: 30 assignments are committed before inference, terminal receipts are signed and chained, and private evidence is encrypted for an auditor. Complete evidence yields coverage and accounting PASS; withholding one terminal receipt returns INCONCLUSIVE_COVERAGE, while withholding all private openings preserves coverage and protocol verification but makes economic claims inconclusive, exactly matching a preregistered prediction. Receipt instrumentation adds 0.021% of model-inference time and 9.9 KB per transaction. A specification-legibility probe indicates that our own frozen specification is not yet unambiguous to an independent reader. Claim verification therefore requires both claim-sufficient evidence and a committed universe against which omissions become visible.

</details>


### [190] [The Memory Trust Gap: Capability-Dependent Failures in Persistent-Memory Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01852)
*Jundong Hu,Shekar Ramachandran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究持久记忆系统中"过时存储事实"覆盖当前权威证据的问题何时随模型能力变化而出现。通过冻结闭集动作评分基准（含Benefit与Safety两套），在Qwen3 0.6/1.7/4/8B同族模型系列上发现：Benefit套件中模型在各规模均高比例采用过时值（0.92-1.00），而Safety套件中的危害受能力门槛限制——较大模型在过时笔记看似新近时更容易崩溃。过信任触发因素依赖特征与模型规模，去标签在所有规模放大过信任，新近性特征仅欺骗较大模型。来源权威性弱且随规模平缓，位置效应在Qwen3系列由正转负。缓解措施同样依赖能力：暴露元数据能提升能力较强模型的准确性，但只有预先解决冲突才能恢复两个较小检查点的准确性。该模式在独立的Llama-Instruct系列及两个外部数据集（RGB、MisBench）上对能力较强模型得到复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化代理依赖持久记忆，但存储的过时事实可能在未经警示的情况下覆盖当前权威证据，造成潜在危害。理解这种危害如何随模型能力变化而开始出现，对于安全部署和缓解策略设计至关重要。

Method: 使用冻结的、闭集的、动作评分的基准测试，包含两套分别代表"无记忆"两种含义的数据集（Benefit套件——无存储事实则无法解决；Safety套件——权威工具总持有正确值）。在同族模型规模系列（Qwen3 0.6/1.7/4/8B）上评估，采用2×2×2×2析因设计分析过信任触发因素，通过跨规模直接对比测试而非重叠置信区间验证规模交互，并独立复现于Llama-Instruct系列和RGB、MisBench外部数据集。

Result: Memory Trust Gap反映的是过度信任而非混淆。Benefit套件中模型在各规模以0.92-1.00比例回答过时值；Safety套件中陷阱条件下的危害（Δ_mem）受能力门槛限制，较大模型受影响更严重。触发因素因特征和规模而异：去标签在所有规模放大过信任，新近性特征更易欺骗较大模型。来源权威性弱且规模平缓，位置效应由正转负。缓解效果能力依赖：元数据暴露对较大模型有效，小模型需预先冲突解决。框架控制发现记忆标签无一致优势，3个较小规模模型更信任过时文档而非过时记忆。

Conclusion: 模型对过时记忆的过度信任是规模化、能力依赖的现象，其触发机制、危害程度和有效缓解策略均随模型规模变化。这提示持久记忆系统设计需考虑模型能力的异质性，采取差异化防护与缓解措施。

Abstract: Persistent memory supports personalized agents, but a stale stored fact can override current authoritative evidence without warning. We study when this harm begins as model capability changes. We evaluate a frozen, closed-set, action-scored benchmark with 2 suites that represent 2 different meanings of "no memory" (a Benefit suite, unsolvable without the stored fact, and a Safety suite, in which an authoritative tool always holds the correct value), on a same-family model-size series (Qwen3 0.6/1.7/4/8B). The Memory Trust Gap reflects over-trust rather than confusion. In the Benefit suite, models answer with the stale value 0.92-1.00 of the time at every scale. In the Safety suite, harm below the no-memory baseline under the trap conditions ($Δ_{\mathrm{mem}}$) is capability-gated, with the larger models collapsing most once a stale note is made to look current. In a $2\times2\times2\times2$ factorial, which feature triggers over-trust depends on both the feature and model scale. Removing a label amplifies over-trust at every size, and a recency feature (stale dated newer) fools the larger models harder. Source authority is weak and scale-flat, and position changes from positive to negative across the Qwen3 model-size series. We confirm these scale interactions with direct cross-size contrast tests rather than overlapping per-model intervals. Mitigation is likewise capability-dependent: exposing metadata improves accuracy for the capable models, but only pre-resolving the conflict restores accuracy for the 2 smaller checkpoints. The same pattern appears on the capable models in an independent Llama-Instruct model-size series and on 2 external datasets (RGB, MisBench). A framing control finds no consistent advantage for the memory label: at the 3 smaller scales, models trust a stale document more than a stale memory; at 8B, the difference is not significant.

</details>


### [191] [Propose to Learn, Learn to Propose: Evaluability-Aware Assistance under Bounded Rationality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02242)
*Yifan Zhu,Sammie Katt,Samuel Kaski*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究AI助手在用户存在有界理性（难以可靠评估所有提案）情况下的提案规划问题，提出ProSE框架与ProSE-Plan规划器，将提案同时作为任务干预和偏好探测工具，实验证明该方法在评估成本为主要瓶颈时优于不考虑可评估性的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI辅助方法假设用户能可靠评估任何提案，但现实中用户受有界理性限制，评估能力有限。因此需要将用户可评估性纳入提案规划考虑。

Method: 形式化ProSE（隐藏参数序列辅助问题），使用KL正则化的有界理性二元响应模型（接受概率权衡价值增益与距离依赖的评估惩罚），并提出ProSE-Plan——深度为2的贝叶斯自适应规划器，通过可能响应及其后验信念来评分提案。

Result: 在受控图模拟中，ProSE-Plan在评估成本为瓶颈时优于不考虑可评估性的基线和短视方法；探测-承诺消融实验确认该方法能选择更简单方法遗漏的信息性提案。

Conclusion: 用户可评估性是AI辅助中一个与生成质量和偏好推断互补的规划相关维度，识别出"可能被接受的提案"与"信息丰富的探测"不一定重合这一关键洞察。

Abstract: AI assistants often collaborate by proposing candidate edits, plans, or designs that users evaluate before adoption. Existing assistance methods focus on proposal quality or user-goal inference, often assuming that the user can reliably evaluate any proposal, which can fail in practice because of bounded rationality. We study evaluability-aware proposal planning, where proposals serve both as task interventions and as probes for learning latent preferences and evaluation constraints, where the resulting belief updates then guide later proposals. We formalise this setting as ProSE, a hidden-parameter sequential assistance problem, and instantiate it with a KL-regularised bounded-rational binary response model in which acceptance trades off value gain against a distance-dependent evaluability penalty. Analysing the planning consequence of this likelihood reveals that likely accepted proposals and informative probes need not coincide, which explains why planners that only pursue acceptance systematically underperform. We operationalise ProSE with \textsc{ProSE-Plan}, a depth-2 Bayes-adaptive planner that scores proposals by possible responses and response-induced posterior beliefs. In controlled graph simulations, \textsc{ProSE-Plan} improves over evaluability-unaware and myopic baselines when evaluation cost is the bottleneck, and a probe-commit ablation confirms that our approach selects informative proposals that simpler methods miss. Our results thus identify user evaluability as a planning-relevant dimension of AI assistance, complementary to generation quality and preference inference.

</details>


### [192] [Belief-Calibrated Optimization: An Explicit World Model for Agentic Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01861)
*Yuhan Chen,Zhihua Tian,Mahavir Dabas,Charith Peris,Rahul Gupta,Ming Jin,Feiyang Kang,Siyuan Zhang,Nan Wang,Ruoxi Jia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出信念校准优化（BCO），通过将编码智能体对环境的隐式信念写成持久化的上下文文档并持续修订，形成可复用的"世界模型"，在五个基准上相比无该文档的对照组取得更高的训练通过率，且该优势在保留集和模型替换后仍然存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体的性能依赖于对冻结模型的脚手架设计。常见的编码智能体优化器虽然隐式地持有关于"环境如何响应编辑"的信念，但这些信念仅存于当前推理中或潜藏于参数里，后续调用无法利用，导致信息丢失和优化效率低下。

Method: BCO方法将编码智能体的信念显式记录为持久化的上下文文档（世界模型），描述环境对编辑的响应机制，并在每轮候选评估后持续修订该文档，将其加入标准的迭代优化循环中。

Result: 在五个基准（记忆QA、工具使用QA、代码即行动应用智能体、终端智能体）上，BCO的训练通过率高于仅缺少世界模型的匹配对照组；差距在所有保留集上保持；目标模型替换后BCO在所测任务上仍领先（除上下文窗口溢出的情况）；离线消融证明文档内容携带可复用信息，而非仅形式带来的提升。

Conclusion: 将隐式信念显式化为可迭代修订的持久化文档是提升LLM智能体脚手架优化的有效途径，该文档作为世界模型提供了跨轮次、跨模型的可复用环境知识。

Abstract: The performance of an LLM agent depends on the scaffold around a frozen model. A common way to improve that scaffold is to use a coding agent as an optimizer: it reads current scores and traces and iteratively edits the source, producing a new candidate each round. Each edit is chosen according to a belief about how the environment will respond: what went wrong, and which change should help. That belief is typically implicit. It lives in the coding agent's reasoning on the current call, or remains latent in its parameters, rather than as something written down. Later calls therefore see scores and traces, but they do not use that belief. We introduce Belief-Calibrated Optimization (BCO), a method that writes that belief down as a persistent in-context document and continually revises that document as new candidates are evaluated. The resulting document is a world model: the current account of how the environment responds to edits. Added to an otherwise standard loop, BCO reaches a higher train passrate than a matched control that lacks only the world model, on five benchmarks spanning memory QA, tool-use QA, code-as-action app agents, and terminal agents. The gap remains on every held-out split, which is not used to select the candidate. After a target-model swap, in which the frozen model is replaced and the scaffold is not, the selected BCO scaffold leads on the tasks we test, except where context-window overruns leave it unfinished. An offline ablation then asks whether that gap comes from what the world model says. A fresh predictor given the accumulated document forecasts how the environment will respond more accurately than predictors given either no document or a same-form copy whose content has been falsified. The comparison indicates that the document carries reusable information in its content, not only in its form.

</details>


### [193] [Codebook Agent: Amortized Topology Design for LLM Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02264)
*Jinxi Yu,Yubei Li,Eric Hanchen Jiang,Zhi Zhang,Dong Liu,Wenxiao Zhao,Levina Li,Kai-Wei Chang,Ying Nian Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Codebook Agent框架，通过向量量化自编码器将LLM多智能体系统的成功通信拓扑压缩为查询无关的16条目码本，配合奖励加权MLP和扁平邻接矩阵代理完成拓扑生成与重排，在无需迭代搜索和消息传递的条件下，于六个基准上取得最优准确率（84.6），拓扑生成仅需2.4ms，且节省21.9%-33.2%的LLM token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将LLM多智能体系统的通信拓扑自适应视为条件图生成问题，但作者通过三个实证发现指出该公式化与实际问题存在错位：1）经奖励过滤后存活的拓扑仅收敛到约6种不同图，即使码本容量从8增至64；2）边数与实测token消耗呈负相关（r≈-0.4），即稀疏化图反而使推理更昂贵；3）当智能体共享配置（基准的默认设定）时，基于消息传递的评分器对邻接矩阵不变，无法排序候选。这三点表明需要重新设计方法。

Method: Codebook Agent包含三个核心组件：1）向量量化自编码器（VQ-VAE）将成功拓扑压缩成查询无关的16条目码本；2）奖励加权MLP将查询嵌入映射为码的分布；3）读取扁平邻接矩阵的MLP代理，在实测效用和每任务归一化token成本上回归，用于在单次批量前向传播中对解码的候选进行重排。测试阶段无迭代搜索、无消息传递。

Result: Codebook Agent在全部六个对比基准上取得最高准确率（平均84.6，优于最强先前设计器的83.0）；拓扑生成耗时2.4ms；相比基线节省21.9%-33.2%的LLM token消耗。

Conclusion: 作者通过实证反驳了将拓扑设计视为条件图生成的既有范式，并证明将成功拓扑压缩为小型码本加简单MLP的范式更为高效且准确，为LLM多智能体系统的通信拓扑设计提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Adapting the communication topology of an LLM multi-agent system to each query improves both accuracy and efficiency, yet current designers treat this as conditional graph generation: a variational, autoregressive, or diffusion decoder searches the $N \times N$ adjacency space, and a graph-network proxy trained on utility and a structural cost such as edge count ranks the sampled candidates. We argue that this formulation is misaligned with the problem. Empirically, topologies that survive a reward filter collapse to about six distinct graphs even when the codebook capacity grows from 8 to 64; edge count is negatively correlated with measured token consumption (Pearson $r \approx -0.4$), so sparsifying the graph makes inference more expensive; and a message-passing scorer over agent-profile nodes is adjacency-invariant whenever agents share a profile---the default configuration of published benchmarks---so it cannot rank candidates at all in that regime. These three facts motivate Codebook Agent: a vector-quantized autoencoder compresses successful topologies into a query-independent 16-entry codebook; a reward-weighted MLP maps the query embedding to a distribution over codes; and an MLP proxy that reads the flattened adjacency, regressed on measured utility and per-task normalized token cost, reranks the top decoded candidates in a single batched forward pass. With no iterative search and no message passing at test time, Codebook Agent is the most accurate method on all six benchmarks we compare (84.6 average against 83.0 for the strongest prior designer), emits a topology in 2.4 ms, and uses 21.9--33.2% fewer LLM tokens.

</details>


### [194] [Collective creativity in hybrid societies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02620)
*Mason Youngblood,Katie Mudd,Manuel Anglada-Tort,Cameron Jones,Elena Miu,Diana Omigie,Margaret Schedel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文认为在生成式AI背景下，创造力应被理解为混合集体（人类与算法共同组成）的属性，而非个体的属性。论文区分了创造力的两个维度——新颖性（单个作品的属性）和多样性（群体的属性），并指出AI辅助创意虽然稳定提升个体输出新颖性，却会收窄整体多样性；但由于人类与模型的搜索方式互补，混合群体可以超越任何一种同质群体。关键决定因素是群体的构成（agents的种类、比例和连接方式）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者对生成式AI是丰富还是贫化文化存在分歧，作者认为这种分歧源于混淆了创造力的两个不同构成要素——新颖性与多样性，因此需要重新概念化AI语境下的创造力。

Method: 论文采用概念分析与理论论证的方法，将创造力分解为新颖性（单件作品属性）和多样性（群体属性）两个维度，提出"混合集体"框架，从人类与AI互补性搜索的角度分析群体组合对创造性产出的影响。

Result: 结论表明AI辅助创意稳定提升个体产出新颖性的同时会收窄总体多样性，但这并非必然结果。混合群体（人类+算法）可以超越任何单一类型群体在多样性与产出方面的表现，而结果取决于群体构成：哪些主体在场、比例如何以及连接方式。

Conclusion: 在生成式AI语境下，创造力研究的问题不再是"AI是否有益或有害于创造力"，而是"何种混合方式能让个体收益积累而不侵蚀集体多样性"。创造力应被重新定位为混合集体的涌现属性。

Abstract: Generative AI is changing how cultural artifacts are created and circulated, and with it our understanding of creativity itself. Researchers disagree about whether these tools enrich or impoverish culture, and we argue that much of that disagreement comes from conflating two distinct components of creativity: novelty, a property of single artifacts, and diversity, a property of populations. We argue further that creativity in the context of generative AI is best understood as a property of hybrid collectives, or populations of interacting people and algorithms, rather than of individuals. AI-assisted ideation reliably raises the novelty of individual output while narrowing diversity in the aggregate, but this is not an inevitable consequence of putting machines in the loop. Because humans and models search in complementary ways, mixed groups can outperform and out-diversify groups of either kind alone, and machine-discovered solutions can enter human culture and persist there. What decides the outcome is composition: which agents are present, in what proportion, and how they are connected. The question is no longer whether AI helps or harms creativity, but which mixtures let individual gains accumulate without eroding collective diversity.

</details>


### [195] [The Ceiling Is in the Channel: Auditing Learner Gaps and Measurement Frontiers in Clinical Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01909)
*Sayeed Shafayet Chowdhury,Nusrat Jahan,Snehasis Mukhopadhyay,Shiaofen Fang,Vijay R. Ramakrishnan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个框架，将临床预测性能饱和分解为"学习者差距"（模型未能提取可用信息）和"测量通道上限"（记录变量本身的信息极限），通过总变差分离刻画最优平衡准确率，并提供交叉拟合上限估计器、标签置换乐观下限及欠拟合曲线等诊断工具，在三个大规模真实队列和104项临床任务的综合分析中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床预测性能饱和可能源于两个截然不同的原因：一是模型/学习器未能充分利用已有信息（可改进模型），二是所记录变量本身存在信息上限（无法通过改进算法突破）。现有研究无法区分这两者，导致优化的盲目性——决策者不知道应该改进学习器还是改进测量。

Method: 通过总变差分离刻画最优平衡准确率，提出架构不变性、替换污染下的尖锐部分识别结果、交叉拟合上限估计器以及多模态决策改进的精确条件。同时引入两个有限样本诊断工具：标签置换乐观下限和欠拟合曲线，并在三个大型真实队列（UCI再入院n=99,343、BRFSS糖尿病n=253,680、NHANES HbA1c n=10,219）上验证。

Result: UBI和BRFSS中调优良好的梯度提升接近估计的前沿，而设计不良或实际不足的学习器保留较大差距。NHANES中问卷与测量边际前沿无显著差异，但联合互补增益显著。所有队列中AUROC小幅增益伴随更大的贝叶斯决策翻转率，多种架构估计出相似前沿但实际平衡准确率差异显著。104项临床任务的系统综述显示：基于18个疾病类别的通道级规律反复出现。

Conclusion: 该框架将性能饱和从经验观察转化为可审计的决策依据：当存在可提升空间时应改进学习器；当达到测量通道上限时应改进测量手段。

Abstract: Clinical prediction can saturate for two different reasons: a fitted learner may fail to extract available information, or the recorded variables may impose a population frontier. We separate these quantities through the \emph{learner gap} and the \emph{measurement-channel ceiling}. Optimal balanced accuracy is characterized by total-variation separation, yielding architecture invariance, a sharp partial-identification result under replacement contamination, a cross-fitted ceiling estimator, and exact conditions for multimodal decision improvement. We add two finite-sample diagnostics, namely a label-permutation optimism floor and an underfit curve, and validate the audit on three real cohorts: UCI readmission ($n=99{,}343$), BRFSS diabetes ($n=253{,}680$), and NHANES HbA1c ($n=10{,}219$). Well-tuned gradient boosting nearly reaches the estimated frontier in UCI and BRFSS, whereas deliberately or practically deficient learners retain large gaps. NHANES yields a null difference between questionnaire and measured marginal frontiers but a significant joint complementarity gain, refining the simplistic claim that an objective modality must dominate. Across all cohorts, modest AUROC gains coexist with substantially larger Bayes decision-flip rates, and several architectures estimate similar frontiers while their achieved balanced accuracy differs sharply. A PRISMA-guided synthesis of 104 clinical tasks then shows that the same channel-level regularities recur across more than 18 disease categories: a broad but non-universal structured-clinical region, diminishing same-channel gains across model families, and higher performance when measurement channels change. The framework converts saturation from an empirical observation into an auditable decision: improve the learner when headroom remains; improve measurement when it does not.

</details>


### [196] [Looped Transformers under the Jacobian Lens: Does the Global Workspace Survive Recurrence?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01924)
*Wenlong Wang,Fergal Reid*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了循环变压器（looped/deep-recurrent transformers）中是否会出现与标准前馈变压器类似的全局工作空间（global workspace）功能。通过将Jacobian分析工具扩展到迭代架构，作者对Ouro-2.6B和Huginn-0125两个模型进行了完整的工作空间测试套件（透镜拟合、读出和11组因果实验），发现工作空间确实在迭代部分形成，但循环改变了其访问方式：Ouro在每个循环中重构工作空间内容且内容无法跨循环边界线性传输，而Huginn可在全部16次循环中向前携带内容但读写和消融仅作用于约2次循环的滑动窗口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究发现标准前馈变压器中存在一个中部深度的可言语化、具因果效力的表征带，功能上类似全局工作空间。但深度通过循环而非堆叠不同层实现时，这种工作空间功能是否仍然出现是未知的。循环变压器由于在深度方向上复用相同权重，为检验这一问题提供了直接测试手段。

Method: 作者提出了虚拟展开适配器（virtual-unrolling adapter）将Jacobian透镜方法扩展到迭代架构。随后对两个循环模型——Ouro-2.6B（48层循环4次、深度监督）和Huginn-0125（4层核心循环16次、针对潜在推理训练）——应用完整的工作空间测试套件，包括透镜拟合、读出和11组因果实验家族，并以Qwen3.6-27B（64层非绑定层）作为标准基线。

Result: 两个架构的迭代部分均形成了工作空间，但可访问性受循环影响：Ouro在每个循环中都能重建工作空间内容，但线性传输无法跨循环边界携带内容，写入和消融必须覆盖所有剩余循环；Huginn则能在全部16次循环中向前携带内容，但读取、写入和消融仅作用于大约2次循环的滑动窗口。新注入内容的可言语化程度与逐迭代监督相关，而现有内容的可操控性则不受监督影响。

Conclusion: 工作空间功能在循环架构中同样涌现，但循环实现方式深刻改变了其访问模式——不同架构根据其循环特性和训练范式产生了截然不同的工作空间动力学。这为理解深度实现机制（堆叠层vs循环）如何影响模型内部信息处理和可解释性操作提供了新视角。

Abstract: Recent work identifies a mid-depth band of verbalisable, causally potent representations in a standard feedforward transformer --- a functional analogue of a global workspace. Whether the same workspace functionality emerges when depth is implemented through recurrence rather than a stack of distinct layers remains unknown. Looped and depth-recurrent transformers provide a direct test of this question because they reuse the same weights across depth. We extend the Jacobian lens to iterated architectures using a virtual-unrolling adapter. We apply the full workspace suite --- lens fitting, readout, and eleven causal experiment families --- to Ouro-2.6B (48 layers looped 4 times, deeply supervised) and Huginn-0125 (a 4-layer core recurred 16 times, trained for latent reasoning), using Qwen3.6-27B (64 untied layers) as the standard baseline. We find that a workspace forms in the iterated part of each architecture, but that recurrence changes how it can be accessed. Ouro reconstructs workspace content in every loop, and linear transport cannot carry that content across loop boundaries; writes and ablations must therefore span every remaining loop. Huginn carries content forward across all sixteen recurrences, while reads, writes, and ablations act only within a sliding window of roughly two recurrences. Whether newly injected content can be verbalised tracks explicit per-iteration supervision; whether existing content can be steered does not.

</details>


### [197] [Post-Training Ternarization of Qwen3-4B Capability, Effective Bit Budget, Storage Compression, and Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01962)
*Anirudh Malik,M Sparsh Mehra,Poojith Devan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了将4B参数的指令微调Qwen模型进行端到端超低位后训练量化转换，实现了1.641有效比特/权重的压缩率，但任务准确率从64.5%降至54.7%，困惑度显著上升，且压缩后的推理速度反而比FP16慢4.6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超低位语言模型可降低存储和内存带宽，但名义上的"1.58-bit"标签并不能完整描述存储表示、保留能力或运行时行为，作者希望全面评估真实的有效比特数、能力保留和部署表现。

Method: 对指令微调的4B参数Qwen模型进行端到端后训练转换，采用KOTMS旋转、E2M-ATQ三值化和TWLA的GPTQ风格误差补偿，权重仅量化（激活保持16位精度），并进行打包运行与Triton GEMV微基准测试。

Result: 最终转换使用1.641有效比特/权重量化81.62%的参数；十项能力对比中准确率从64.5%降至54.7%（BoolQ保留84.6%，ARC-Challenge仅保留43.8%）；困惑度在WikiText-2、PTB、C4上分别从13.639升至18.748、24.700升至31.992、19.831升至28.966；打包后模型从8.29 GiB降至3.96 GiB且困惑度基本不变，但Triton GEMV微基准比FP16 cuBLAS慢4.6倍。

Conclusion: 超低位量化会带来不均匀的能力退化，"1.58-bit"的标签具有误导性，且压缩本身并不保证推理加速——该转换方案在存储节省方面有效，但任务保留和运行时性能均需谨慎评估。

Abstract: Ultra-low-bit language models can reduce storage and memory bandwidth, but a nominal "1.58-bit" label does not fully describe the stored representation, retained capability, or runtime behavior.
  We study an end-to-end post-training conversion of Qwen, an instruction-tuned 4B-parameter model, using KOTMS rotation, E2M-ATQ ternarization, and GPTQ-style error compensation from TWLA. The experiment is weight-only: activations remain at 16-bit precision, so ILA-AMP is omitted. We evaluate effective bit accounting, task capability retention, perplexity, calibration sensitivity, checkpoint composition, and deployment behavior.
  The final conversion uses 1.641 effective bits per weight for quantized linear weights, with 81.62% of model parameters targeted. Across ten scored capability comparisons, accuracy falls from 64.5% to 54.7%. Degradation is uneven: BoolQ retains 84.6% chance-corrected teacher performance, while ARC-Challenge retains 43.8%. Perplexity rises from 13.639 to 18.748 on WikiText-2, 24.700 to 31.992 on PTB, and 19.831 to 28.966 on C4.
  A subsequent packing run preserves the ternary planes and scales, reducing reported model size from 8.29 GiB to 3.96 GiB with essentially unchanged perplexity. A separate third-party packing attempt was lossy and is excluded from the primary artifact claim. The packed artifact has not been benchmarked end-to-end for task accuracy or generation throughput. A preliminary Triton GEMV microbenchmark is 4.6x slower than FP16 cuBLAS on one tested shape. We therefore do not claim that compression alone yields faster inference.

</details>


### [198] [Benchmarking Language Models for Statistical Problem Formulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01982)
*Chen Wang,Junzhe Zhao,Xin Cong,Wanlu Deng,Ke Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了统计问题公式化（Statistical Problem Formulation）这一上游任务，并构建了StatFormBench基准，包含来自五本跨领域统计学教材和案例库的1,013个样本，覆盖20个粗粒度和85个细粒度统计问题类别。评估14个开源和闭源LLM后发现，最优零样本模型仅达到72.0的细粒度分类准确率和63.2的变量集重叠，且无模型能在两个子任务上同时表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在统计和数据科学工作上的评估大多假设分析目标已明确给定，但实际使用中用户往往带着模糊的目标和异构数据而来，需要模型自行推断统计任务类型和相关变量。这一上游环节被忽略，缺乏系统性形式化定义和基准。

Method: 将统计问题公式化分解为两个子任务：（1）统计问题分类，（2）变量识别与角色分配。基于五本跨领域统计学教科书和一个数据科学案例库构建StatFormBench基准，包含1,013个样本、20个粗粒度和85个细粒度统计问题类别，并对14个开源和闭源LLM进行评估。

Result: 最优零样本模型在细粒度分类准确率上仅达72.0%，变量集重叠仅63.2%。没有任何模型能在两个子任务上同时表现最佳，增强提示策略带来的提升有限且不稳定。

Conclusion: 统计问题公式化对LLM而言仍具挑战性，现有模型在该任务上的表现远未达到令人满意的水平，模型能力在分类与变量识别两个子任务间存在明显权衡，需要进一步研究以提升LLM在此上游统计任务上的能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as assistants for statistical and data science work, yet existing evaluations largely assume the analysis target is already specified. In practice, users arrive with informal goals and heterogeneous data, leaving the model to decide what statistical task is implied and which data are relevant. We first formalize this upstream step as Statistical Problem Formulation and decompose it into two subtasks: (1) Statistical Problem Classification and (2) Variable Identification & Role Assignment. We then introduce StatFormBench, a benchmark built from five cross-domain statistics textbooks and a data science case library, covering diverse problem types, data representations, and scenario styles. It contains 1,013 samples spanning 20 coarse-grained and 85 fine-grained statistical problem categories. Across 14 open- and closed-source LLMs, the best zero-shot models reach only 72.0 fine-grained classification accuracy and 63.2 variable set overlap. No model performs consistently best across the two subtasks, while enhanced prompting strategies yield only limited or inconsistent gains. We release the benchmark data on Hugging Face at https://huggingface.co/datasets/THU-CongLab/StatFormBench and the evaluation code on GitHub at https://github.com/THU-CongLab/StatFormBench.

</details>


### [199] [ToolGate: An Executable Acceptance Pipeline for Tool-Dependent Scientific Benchmark Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02067)
*Ke Zhang,Yankang Liu,Roya Zandi,Maziar Raissi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ToolGate框架，用于自动生成需要科学计算软件求解的基准测试题。每个生成项需通过三重门控：可执行脚本需重现答案、无工具筛查排除模型可直接解答的题目、工具代理需在时限内求解。在FEniCSx上的500次生成尝试中，最终保留128道通过全部门控的独特题目。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统科学基准构建依赖领域专家逐题编写和人工交叉验证，劳动成本高且耗时长。虽然有语言模型可快速生成候选题目，但如何自动验收这些候选题的质量（有效性、难度、可解性）仍是核心挑战。

Method: 提出ToolGate三重门控生成机制：1) 本地验证门——生成的可执行脚本须在科学软件中重现候选题答案；2) 无工具筛查门——用随机化测试排除仅凭提示即可被模型解答的平凡题；3) 工具代理门——要求带工具的代理在限定时间内解决幸存题目。在FEniCSx有限元软件上实例化，结合生成后重新筛查（移除重复与平凡题）进行最终报告。

Result: 在FEniCSx上进行500次生成尝试：本地验证门保留478个候选；无工具随机筛查排除222个；GPT-5.5 API中等推理排除121个；最终135题中GPT-5.5 Codex CLI代理（可访问FEniCSx）成功解决130题，精确去重后保留128道独特题目。

Conclusion: ToolGate将重复的答案验证和难度筛查转化为可审计的自动化流程，同时将领域设计和最终审核保留给专家，显著降低了构建高质量科学计算基准的人力成本。

Abstract: Scientific benchmarks are commonly built by domain experts who write tasks and cross-check one another's work, or who adapt existing material from textbooks, published papers, and online resources. These routes can produce strong evaluations, but they require substantial per-item labor. Language models can reduce this repeated work by proposing candidates quickly. The remaining problem is acceptance. We target scientific questions whose answers require computations with specialist software rather than unaided reasoning alone. A candidate is invalid if its script fails or returns a different answer, or trivial if a model answers it without the software. We present ToolGate, which treats every generated item as a proposal and keeps it only if three gates pass. First, an executable solution script must reproduce the proposed answer when run with the scientific software. Second, randomized no-tool screening rejects candidates that models can already solve from the prompt alone. Third, a tool-using agent must solve each survivor within a fixed time limit. We instantiate ToolGate in FEniCSx with 500 generation attempts. The local-verification gate retains 478 candidates. For final reporting, we rescreen this pool after generation: two randomized no-tool screens exclude 222 from the reported pool, and direct GPT-5.5 API calls at medium reasoning (the API default) exclude another 121. Of the remaining 135, a GPT-5.5 Codex CLI agent with access to FEniCSx solves 130; exact deduplication leaves 128 unique protocol survivors. ToolGate turns repeated answer checking and difficulty screening into an auditable process while leaving domain design and final review to experts.

</details>


### [200] [HeadWiseKV: Budgeted Per-Head Cache Residency for Hybrid Long-Context Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02029)
*Renjie Xie,Juncheng Yang,Aoting Hu,Mingxi Zhang,Liyao Wu,Zheheng Hong,Wei Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HeadWiseKV是一种无需训练的框架，通过为混合语言模型的全局注意力层分配静态多级历史窗口来压缩KV缓存，在预算约束下保持接近完整KV缓存的下游质量，同时显著降低峰值显存并扩展可处理的上下文长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文推理期间不断增长的键值(KV)缓存消耗大量GPU显存并降低生成吞吐量。即使在混合语言模型中，残差全局注意力层仍可能主导上下文相关的缓存需求，成为性能瓶颈。

Method: 将KV缓存压缩问题形式化为受限操作率失真问题，通过SeqCalib算法按执行顺序逐层处理，并根据部署时较低层策略来条件化每个决策以考虑深度间的交互。运行时使用分组缓存将所选策略实现为实际的每头KV驻留量。每个物理KV头被分配一个静态多级历史窗口，使缓存需求在服务前即可预测。

Result: 在四个混合长上下文模型上，HeadWiseKV保持接近完整KV缓存的RULER和LoCoMo质量。在固定模型系统研究中，HeadWiseKV在112K上下文长度下将采样峰值设备显存降低8.59%，并将最大验证成功的上下文从114K扩展到161K。

Conclusion: HeadWiseKV证明了在KV驻留预算约束下，通过静态多级历史窗口分配和逐层条件化的策略生成算法，可以在不牺牲下游质量的情况下显著压缩混合语言模型的全局KV缓存，从而提高长上下文推理的效率和可扩展性。

Abstract: Long-context inference retains a growing key--value (KV) cache during decoding, which consumes substantial GPU memory and can reduce generation throughput. This bottleneck remains in hybrid language models because their residual global-attention layers can dominate context-dependent cache demand. We study how to allocate this state under an aggregate KV-residency budget. We introduce HeadWiseKV, a training-free framework that compresses the residual global KV caches of hybrid language models while preserving their native local, recurrent, and linear paths. It assigns each physical KV head a static, multilevel history window, making cache demand predictable before serving. We formulate this allocation as a restricted operational rate--distortion problem and propose SeqCalib as the core policy-generation algorithm in HeadWiseKV. SeqCalib processes layers in execution order and conditions each decision on the lower-layer policy used at deployment, thereby accounting for interactions across depth. A grouped-cache runtime materializes the selected policy as actual per-head KV residency rather than a mask over a full cache. We evaluate downstream quality across four hybrid long-context models and study physical residency and serving behavior on Qwen3.6-27B. HeadWiseKV retains near-Full-KV RULER and LoCoMo quality across the evaluated models. In the fixed-model systems study, it reduces sampled peak device memory by 8.59\% at a 112K context length and extends the largest verified successful context from 114K to 161K.

</details>


### [201] [Monitoring Web Agents Without Internal Signals: Observable Trajectories and Key-Step Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02057)
*Sitong Pan,Yipeng Shen,Yilin Lu,Caiwen Ding,Lu Cheng,Qianwen Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究Web智能体在执行过程中的前缀级风险预测问题，在无法获取模型内部不确定性信号（如token logits）时，仅通过可观测的轨迹信号来判断执行是否偏离正确方向。作者提出了宏观（Macro）和微观（Micro）两类可观测轨迹表征，并设计了关键步骤边界标注方法，避免将失败轨迹中仍正确的早期前缀误标为失败。实验表明该方法在多个基准和模型骨干上与使用内部信号的基线相当，并支持早期干预和跨网站类别迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型内部不确定性信号（如token logits）在很多实际Web智能体场景中不可用或难以获取，导致对智能体执行过程的可靠监控变得困难，需要探索仅基于可观测轨迹信号的风险预测方法。

Method: 1）提出两类可观测轨迹表征：Macro特征总结跨步骤的智能体-环境交互行为和反馈；Micro特征通过重复黑盒查询衡量意图、行动和预期状态变化之间的一致性。2）不直接继承最终结果标签，而是标注观察延续中第一个未被纠正并与最终失败关联的关键错误作为关键步骤边界，从而保留失败轨迹中有效的早期前缀作为"在轨"样本。3）在WebArena-Lite和Online Mind2Web基准上使用5种开源和闭源模型骨干进行验证。

Result: 可观测轨迹信号在风险预测上与使用内部信号的基线方法表现相当；预测器在固定误切预算下支持早期干预，且能够迁移到未见过的网站类别。

Conclusion: 仅依赖可观测的轨迹信号即可实现有价值的Web智能体风险预测能力，无需依赖内部不确定信号。

Abstract: Reliable web-agent monitoring is difficult when model-internal uncertainty signals such as token logits are unavailable. In this work, we study prefix-level risk prediction for web agents using observable trajectory signals: given an evolving prefix, estimate whether the current execution remains on track or is tending toward failure. We derive two observable trajectory representations: Macro features summarize cross-step agent--environment behavior and feedback, while Micro features measure the consistency of intention, action, and anticipated state change through repeated black-box queries. Instead of inheriting the final result label, we label the first critical error that remains uncorrected in the observed continuation and is associated with final failure as a key-step boundary, preserving valid early prefixes of failed trajectories as on track. Across WebArena-Lite and Online Mind2Web web agent benchmarks with five open- and closed-source backbones, observable trajectory signals are competitive with internal-signal baselines. The resulting predictors also support early intervention under fixed false-cut budgets and transfer across held-out website categories. These findings show that observable trajectory signals support valuable risk prediction abilities.

</details>


### [202] [DocHop: Benchmarking Out-of-domain Multi-hop Reasoning in Information-Dense Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02059)
*Zhuoran Yu,Le Thien Phuc Nguyen,Jaden Park,Xinyi Gu,Zexue He,Soochahn Lee,Rogerio Feris,Yong Jae Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DocHop是一个用于评估多模态大语言模型在文档图像中进行图表-文本联合推理能力的新基准，包含2,074个跨六类任务的样本，通过随机逻辑优先的生成管线构建。实验显示最佳模型准确率仅62.83%，远低于人类90%以上的水平，且推理复杂度增加时模型性能显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM基准通常孤立评估图表理解和文档问答，忽略了模型能否利用文本上下文来决定如何选择、解释和聚合图表证据这一关键能力。

Method: 提出DocHop基准，通过随机逻辑优先的生成管线构建，可控制推理深度和视觉密度，覆盖2,074个样本、六类任务。问题基于叙事中定义的语义参考标签，要求模型先解决上下文中的目标实体，再跨多图表聚合证据。

Result: 人类标注者准确率超90%，最佳专有/开源MLLM仅达62.83%。推理增强模型表现一致提升，但随推理复杂度增加性能下降。

Conclusion: DocHop揭示了当前MLLM在集成图表-上下文推理上的显著不足，为挑战性多跳文档推理提供了受控测试平台，表明该方向仍需大幅改进。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have achieved strong performance on structured visual understanding tasks such as chart and document question answering. However, existing benchmarks typically evaluate these domains in isolation, leaving underexplored a key capability: whether models can use textual context to determine how chart evidence should be selected, interpreted, and aggregated. We introduce DocHop, a benchmark for integrated chart--context reasoning in document-style images. In DocHop, the document narrative specifies multi-step compositional constraints, while charts provide the corresponding data values. Questions are grounded on a semantic reference label defined in the narrative, requiring models to resolve target entities from context before aggregating evidence across multiple charts. To enable systematic evaluation, we construct DocHop via a stochastic logic-first generation pipeline with controllable reasoning depth and visual density, covering 2,074 examples across six task categories. Experiments on a wide range of proprietary and open-source MLLMs show a substantial gap to human performance: annotators achieve over 90% accuracy, while the best model reaches only 62.83%. Reasoning-enhanced models consistently show improved results, but performance degrades as reasoning complexity increases. Overall, DocHop provides a controlled testbed for challenging multi-hop document reasoning.

</details>


### [203] [MineTRACE: An Evidence-Grounded Interactive Reasoning System for Mineral Prospectivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02060)
*Yiran Zhang,Jinwen Liu,Daniel Su,Yisu Chen,Qiang Sun,Chris Gonzalez,Eun-Jung Holden,Marco Fiorentini,Wei Liu,Yihao Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MineTRACE是一个基于Web的矿产勘查系统，面向Cu、Au、Ni等八种矿产，通过透明的专家树结构整合多源地质证据，提供可解释的成矿潜力评分，并支持自然语言交互式查询与证据追溯。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有成矿预测系统往往只输出不透明的评分或热力图，缺乏对证据来源的解释和可视化，导致勘探人员难以理解和验证预测结果，也无法高效整合异构的地球化学、地球物理和地质证据。

Method: 采用地质知识与已知矿床信息构建透明的专家树模型，将多源证据组合为可解释的成矿潜力评分；系统提供Web端便利性，用户可浏览潜力图、查询特定位置或区域、查看支撑证据，并通过自然语言与对话助手交互；对话助手从分析管线中检索评分及支撑证据，以自然语言呈现。

Result: 评分器在多种测试场景下空间AUC最高达到0.917；端到端评估验证了查询准确性和回答依据可靠性。

Conclusion: MineTRACE使公共地球科学数据更易获取、解释和验证，支持更高效和透明的矿产勘探流程。

Abstract: Mineral exploration requires integrating heterogeneous geochemical, geophysical, and geological evidence, yet existing prospectivity systems often provide only opaque scores or heatmaps. We present MineTRACE, a web-based system for evidence-grounded exploration of eight commodities: Cu, Au, Ni, W, Sn, Co, Ta, and Mn. Users can explore prospectivity maps, query locations or regions, inspect supporting evidence, and interact through natural language. A transparent expert tree, informed by geological knowledge and known deposits, combines multi-source evidence into interpretable prospectivity scores. For a new location, the conversational assistant retrieves the score and supporting evidence from the analysis pipeline and presents them in natural language. The scorer achieves spatial AUC values of up to 0.917 across different test scenarios, while end-to-end evaluation assesses query accuracy and response grounding. MineTRACE makes public geoscience data easier to access, interpret, and verify, supporting more efficient and transparent mineral exploration.

</details>


### [204] [CHIME: Credit-Aware Hierarchical Memory Evolution for Long-Horizon Agentic Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02074)
*Yongshi Ye,Tian Lan,Feihu Jiang,Muyang Ye,Bin Zhu,Qianghuai Jia,Longyue Wang,Zhao Xu,Weihua Luo,Xiaodong Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CHIME（信用感知的层级记忆进化框架），通过将任务结果归因到计划或执行层面并分别更新对应记忆库，解决了自进化记忆方法中的信用分配问题，在四个长时程智能体基准上超越了现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自进化记忆方法存在固有的信用分配问题：它们仅依赖最终任务结果作为反馈，但最终结果混淆了计划质量、执行错误和环境因素，导致积累的规划经验存在偏差和噪声。

Method: CHIME维护两个独立的记忆库（规划库和执行库），并遵循"先归因后记忆"的原则：首先将每个任务结果归因到计划、执行、两者或都不是，然后仅更新对应的记忆库。

Result: 在四个长时程智能体基准上，CHIME一致优于基于训练的先进方法和自进化记忆基线。分析还发现：CHIME用更少的记忆条目积累有效经验；高质量规划记忆比执行记忆更有价值；积累的记忆可以跨骨干模型有效迁移。

Conclusion: 通过精细的信用归因机制，自进化记忆系统可以在推理时不断积累更高质量的规划经验，避免训练成本且无需参数更新。

Abstract: Planning is a central capability that enables agents to decompose complex long-horizon tasks into manageable steps. Test-time search and training-based methods improve planning but incur high inference costs or require expensive training data. Self-evolving memory instead accumulates reusable experience from agent interaction outcomes into an external memory bank, so planning capability keeps improving at inference time without parameter updates. However, existing self-evolving memory methods share an inherent credit assignment problem: they rely on final task outcomes as feedback, but such outcomes conflate plan quality with execution errors and environmental factors, so the accumulated planning experience is often biased and noisy. To address this problem, we propose Credit-Aware Hierarchical Memory Evolution (CHIME), a self-evolving memory framework that maintains a separate planning bank and execution bank and follows an attribute-before-memorize principle: CHIME first attributes each task outcome to the plan, the execution, both, or neither, and then updates only the corresponding memory bank. Extensive experiments on four long-horizon agent benchmarks show that CHIME consistently outperforms state-of-the-art training-based and self-evolving memory baselines. Further analyses reveal several interesting findings. For example, CHIME accumulates effective memory with far fewer items. In addition, the learned memory values faithfully reflect downstream utility: high-quality planning memories are more valuable than execution memories. Finally, the accumulated memory effectively transfers across backbone models. Code will be released at https://github.com/ATH-MaaS/Marco-DeepResearch.

</details>


### [205] [Beyond Outcome Gaps: Process-Aware Fairness Diagnosis for LLM-based Multi-Agent Decision Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02092)
*Yiran Zhao,Lu Zhou,Liming Fang,Yufei Chen,Jiafei Wu,Zhe Liu,Xiaogang Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SCOPED-Hiring，一个面向LLM多智能体招聘系统的过程感知公平性诊断管道。通过构建311K条结构化决策轨迹并用六种诊断视角进行量化分析，发现最终招聘率的平衡可能掩盖轨迹内部的不公平现象（如职业空窗期引发怀疑、代理线索影响资格判断、身份线索导致调查不平等），并验证了基于诊断的定向修复可将分层负担降低72.3%而仅使招聘率变动1.86个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于结果（outcome-based）的公平性审计无法识别高风险决策轨迹中途的风险来源，尤其在LLM多智能体系统中，最终结果的公平并不能保证决策过程的公平。

Method: 构建受控简历变体，运行基于角色的招聘委员会，记录311K+条结构化决策轨迹，将轨迹字段转化为量化公平信号，并通过六种诊断视角（最终结果、反事实、过程、路径、动态、设计效应）进行分析。

Result: 发现最终招聘率的平衡可能掩盖决策轨迹中的隐藏不公平；职业空窗期触发怀疑、代理线索塑造资格判断、身份线索导致不平等调查。据此进行定向修复后，总分层负担降低72.3%，而招聘率仅变化1.86个百分点。

Conclusion: 过程诊断能够有效指导公平性修复，表明对多智能体决策轨迹进行细粒度公平性分析比仅看最终结果更具价值。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS) are increasingly considered for high-stakes decision-making, yet outcome-based fairness audits can miss where risks arise within the decision trajectory. We present SCOPED-Hiring, a process-aware fairness diagnosis pipeline for LLM-based hiring MAS. SCOPED-Hiring constructs controlled resume variants, runs role-based hiring committees, logs over 311K structured decision trajectories, and converts trajectory fields into quantitative fairness signals organized by six diagnostic lenses: final outcome, counterfactual, process, pathway, dynamic, and design effects. SCOPED-Hiring reveals that balanced final hire rates can mask hidden trajectory unfairness in multi-agent decision trajectories: career gaps trigger suspicion, proxy cues shape qualification judgments, and identity cues lead to unequal investigation. Targeted repair guided by these diagnoses reduces total layered burden by 72.3% while shifting the hire rate by only 1.86 pp, showing that process diagnosis can guide effective repair. Project Page: https://scoped-hiring-project-page.vercel.app/

</details>


### [206] [MASkills: Continual Skills Optimization for Multi-Agent LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02094)
*Huaiyuan Yao,Xiaoou Liu,Charles Fleming,Tianlong Chen,Hua Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MASkills是一个针对多智能体LLM系统的持续学习框架，通过将经验记忆转化为结构化的"智能体技能"（agent skills），实现技能库的自动进化（提炼、归纳、整合和剪枝），在多智能体任务上表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的自反思方法构建经验记忆，但记忆难以调用、精炼和扩展；而智能体技能提供了更具可操作性的单元——结构化程序性知识，能明确何时行动、如何行动以及使用哪些工具。

Method: 提出了一个新的智能体优化流水线，集成三个关键组件：技能条件信用分配（skill-conditioned credit assignment）、分层信用聚合（hierarchical credit aggregation）和动量平滑优化（momentum-smoothed optimization），使智能体技能库能够通过提炼、归纳、整合和剪枝四种操作持续进化。

Result: 在HotpotQA、LoCoMo和GAIA三个基准上的实验证明了MASkills在多种智能体任务上的有效性。代码已开源。

Conclusion: MASkills通过以技能为单位的学习和技能库的持续演化，解决了多智能体系统从交互经验中持续改进的难题，提供了一种比记忆方法更可操作、可扩展的持续学习范式。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems have shown strong performance on complex tasks, yet continual improvement from interaction experience remains challenging. Existing self-reflection methods build experience memories, but memories are mostly hard to invoke, refine, or scale, while agent skills offer a more actionable unit: structured procedural knowledge that specifies when to act, how to act, and which resources or tools to use. We introduce MASkills, a continual learning framework that optimizes multi-agent LLM systems through agent skills. MASkills presents a new agent-optimization pipeline that integrates skill-conditioned credit assignment, hierarchical credit aggregation, and momentum-smoothed optimization, enabling agent skill libraries to evolve through refinement, induction, consolidation, and pruning. Experiments on HotpotQA, LoCoMo, and GAIA demonstrate the effectiveness of MASkills across multiple agentic tasks. Our code is available at https://github.com/DaRL-GenAI/MASkills

</details>


### [207] [READY or Not: Reliable Enterprise Agent Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02095)
*Veronica Chatrath,Bryan Zhu,Jingxuan Fan,George Pu,Soham Dinesh Tiwari,Soham Dan,Ryan Young,Yuan,Li,Yuang Yao,Apaar Shanker,Minglai Yang,Daniel Yue Zhang,Yunzhong He,Ying Liu,Chenguang Wang,Zhijun Yin,Yuan Xue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出READY框架，用于评估AI代理在企业工作流中的部署资格。与仅衡量代理能否完成任务的传统基准不同，READY关注代理在可接受的人类监督和可承受成本下能否达到所需可靠性水平。该框架通过测量人机系统的可靠性和运营成本，选择满足可靠性目标的最低成本监督策略，并在临床审计案例中证明了其能揭示自主性能掩盖的部署差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI代理基准只衡量代理能否完成专业工作，而企业部署关心的不同问题是：代理能否在可接受的人类监督和可承受成本下达到所需的可靠性水平。自主性能表现良好并不代表适合部署，因此需要一个专门的框架来评估代理的部署资格。

Method: READY框架包含以下核心环节：1) 保留每个工作流自身对成功执行的定义，同时应用统一的资格认定流程；2) 给定代理、工作流和候选监督策略类别，测量人机系统的可靠性和运营成本；3) 选择满足指定可靠性目标的最低成本策略；4) 在保留案例上进行统计资格认定；5) 生成部署档案，描述可靠性、人类监督负担和成本三个维度。

Result: 在覆盖16个代理系统和750个案例的端到端临床审计案例研究中，READY揭示了自主性能隐藏的差异：两个自主准确率仅差0.3个百分点（72.8% vs 72.5%）的系统，在相同76%可靠性目标下，分别需要39.2%和29.6%的人类审查比例才能通过资格认定。

Conclusion: READY将企业代理评估的焦点从"代理能多好地完成工作"转变为"在什么条件下、以什么成本才能可靠部署"。通过使这些条件显式化且可统计检验，READY为比较代理系统、设定监督要求和做出基于证据的部署决策提供了基础。

Abstract: An AI agent can perform well on benchmarks and still be unsuitable for deployment. Existing AI-agent benchmarks measure whether an agent can complete realistic professional work, whereas enterprise deployment asks a different question: whether an agent can meet a required reliability level, under acceptable human oversight, and at tolerable cost. We introduce Reliable Enterprise Agent Deployment (READY), a framework for qualifying AI agents for deployment on enterprise workflows. READY preserves each workflow's own definition of successful execution while applying a common qualification procedure. Given an agent, a workflow, and a class of candidate oversight policies, READY measures the reliability and operating cost of the human-AI system, selects the minimum-cost policy that satisfies a specified reliability target, and statistically qualifies it on held-out cases. The resulting deployment profile characterizes the supported operating point: reliability, human-oversight burden, and cost. READY is implemented as an open testbed that decouples workflow specification, execution, evaluation, and qualification, and runs on existing agent-evaluation infrastructure. In an end-to-end clinical-audit case study spanning 16 agent systems and 750 cases, READY reveals differences hidden by autonomous performance: two systems separated by only 0.3 points in autonomous accuracy (72.8% vs. 72.5%) require 39.2% versus 29.6% human review, respectively, to qualify at the same 76% reliability target under the evaluated oversight policy. READY thus shifts enterprise agent evaluation from how well can the agent perform the work? to under what conditions, and at what cost, can it be reliably deployed? By making those conditions explicit and statistically testable, READY provides a basis for comparing agent systems, setting oversight requirements, and making evidence-based deployment decisions.

</details>


### [208] [Semantic Signal-Assisted Inspection and Recovery Allocation in Reverse Logistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02116)
*Jiani He,Dingyan Shang,Yihua Xu,Shiqi Huang,Yan Lyu,Jize Li,Shangjing Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出语义信号辅助决策支持框架，利用退货备注生成条件因子和信号质量评分，在共享劳动力约束下指导逆向物流中的检查深度与回收分配。在三个合成基准场景（IT设备退役、飞机维护、消费电子退货）中评估，关键词实现相比带噪声完整检查的结构化特征基准提升了净回收价值并降低了检查成本；在匹配检查成本下，评分引导的目标定位在飞机场景中每批次增加53.9千美元收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
