Motivation: 逆向物流运营者在退回资产条件尚未完全观测时就需要决定如何检查和分配路径，而全面检查消耗稀缺劳动力。现有方法缺乏在决策前利用非结构化叙述性证据（如退货备注）来优化检查深度和恢复资源配置的手段。

Method: 提出Semantic Signal-Assisted Decision Support框架，将退货备注转化为条件因子和信号质量评分，以指导检查深度和回收分配；在共享劳动力容量约束下评估关键字、短语和大语言模型三种提取器。

Result: 在30对配对模拟种子下，关键字实现在三个场景中均比带噪声完整检查的结构化特征基准提升净回收价值并降低检查成本；匹配检查成本下，评分引导目标定位在飞机场景增加53.9千美元/批次；短语和大语言模型提取器在飞机场景提供额外增益。

Conclusion: 叙述性证据（退货备注）可在回收决策做出前有效支持检查分配，语义信号辅助框架具有实际部署价值，但在纯经济目标下风险盲基准仍可能获得更高价值，提示需要综合考虑风险与收益。

Abstract: Reverse-logistics operators often decide how to inspect and route returned assets before their condition is fully observed, while full inspection consumes scarce labor. Semantic Signal-Assisted Decision Support converts return notes into a condition factor and a signal-quality score that guide inspection depth and recovery allocation under shared labor capacity. We evaluate the framework in three synthetic benchmark scenarios spanning information technology decommissioning, aircraft maintenance, and consumer-electronics returns. Across 30 paired simulation seeds, the keyword implementation improves net recovery value relative to a structured-feature comparator with noisy full inspection while reducing inspection cost in all three scenarios. A risk-blind comparator that skips inspection altogether still records higher value under the benchmark's purely economic objective. At matched inspection cost, score-guided targeting adds 53.9 thousand United States dollars per batch in the aircraft scenario but has little economic effect in the other two configurations; phrase and large language model extractors provide further gains in the aircraft scenario. These results show how narrative evidence can support inspection allocation before recovery decisions are made.

</details>


### [209] [Beyond Context Windows: Persistent Discovery Context for Data-Centric Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02129)
*Jalal Mahmud*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出持久发现上下文（Persistent Discovery Context），一种轻量级记忆层，用于存储和复用数据代理在发现步骤中产生的"意图到对象"映射，以增强后续检索质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据中心的代理在执行任务前通常会进行发现步骤以识别相关数据对象，但这些成功的发现结果通常被丢弃而非复用，造成了有价值信息的浪费。

Method: 引入持久发现上下文作为一种轻量级记忆层，存储先前的意图到对象映射，并在未来检索时加以复用，以增强元数据搜索的效果。

Result: 在三个结构化数据环境中，持久发现上下文一致性地优于仅基于元数据的搜索；自动生成的记忆同样有效；发现了可复现的干扰失败模式；在词汇稀疏的领域中，仅凭记忆检索甚至优于基于元数据的检索。

Conclusion: 发现结果构成了数据中心代理可复用的重要上下文形式，为提升代理检索能力提供了新思路。

Abstract: Data-centric agents repeatedly perform a discovery step before planning or execution: identifying the data objects relevant to a task. Yet successful discovery outcomes are typically discarded rather than reused. We introduce persistent discovery context, a lightweight memory layer that stores prior intent-to-object mappings and reuses them to augment future retrieval. Across three structured data environments, persistent discovery context consistently improves retrieval quality over metadata-only search, remains effective with automatically generated memories, and exposes a reproducible interference failure mode. In lexically sparse domains, memory-only retrieval can even outperform metadata-based retrieval. These findings suggest that discovery outcomes constitute a useful form of reusable context for data-centric agents.

</details>


### [210] [EmoStance: Response-Side Affective-Orientation Control for Empathetic Response Generation via Emoji Weak Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02133)
*Ziyuan Jin,Yuxuan Ge,Zheng Tian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EmoStance框架，利用多注释者emoji分布作为弱情感证据诱导潜在控制空间，近似听众立场，通过连续前缀嵌入引导冻结的指令微调LLM生成具有响应侧情感取向的共情回复。构建了EmojiDialogue数据集（带emoji投票和置信度的逐句共情对话扩展），在800次盲审配对评估中达到62.2%的决定性胜率，在上下文特异性和感知响应性上提升最明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共情回复生成不仅需要决定说什么，还需要决定如何针对说话者的情感状态进行回应，即响应侧情感取向控制。现有方法缺乏对响应者情感态度立场的建模与引导机制。

Method: 1) 将共情回复形式化为响应侧情感取向控制问题；2) 使用多注释者emoji分布作为弱情感-态度证据（而非输出符号或硬标签），诱导潜在控制空间近似听众立场；3) 构建EmojiDialogue数据集（EmphatheticDialogues的逐句扩展，含emoji投票与置信度）；4) 提出EmoStance模型：建模源侧情感表达，从对话上下文和说话者角色预测软响应侧取向，通过连续前缀嵌入引导冻结的指令微调LLM。

Result: 20名注释者、800次判断的盲配对评估中，EmoStance实现62.2%的决定性胜率，在上下文特异性和感知响应性方面提升最为明显，并与外部知识方法互补。

Conclusion: 将emoji分布作为弱证据来诱导响应侧情感取向的潜在空间是可行且有效的路径，能显著提升共情回复质量，且可与外部知识方法结合使用。代码、注释元数据和重建脚本已开源。

Abstract: Empathetic response generation requires models to decide not only what to say, but also how to respond to the previous speaker's affective situation. We formulate this as response-side affective-orientation control and use multi-annotator emoji distributions as weak affective--attitudinal evidence, rather than as output symbols or gold labels, to induce a latent control space that operationally approximates listener stance. We construct EmojiDialogue, an utterance-level extension of EmpatheticDialogues with emoji votes and confidence scores, and propose EmoStance, which models source-side affective expression, predicts a soft response-side orientation from dialogue context and speaker roles, and steers a frozen instruction-tuned LLM through continuous prefix embeddings. In blind pairwise evaluation with 20 annotators and 800 judgments, EmoStance achieves a 62.2% decisive win rate, with the clearest gains in contextual specificity and perceived responsiveness, while remaining complementary to external-knowledge methods. Code, annotation metadata, and reconstruction scripts are available in our GitHub repository: https://github.com/18277390221/EmoStance.

</details>


### [211] [FUSE: An Evaluating Framework for Dangerous Capabilities of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02168)
*Zhengyi Jin,Ru Zhang,Xiao Chen,Xinbo Liu,Jiaxuan Lin,Jia Huang,Jianyi Liu,Zhen Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个模块化框架，通过知识(K)、防御(D)、危害(H)三条正交评估管线对AI模型进行标准化危险能力评估，生成危险能力画像φ。在化学-生物领域对12个商用大语言模型进行测试，发现不同模型和家族在三维度上呈现显著差异，且危险能力随时间并未单调下降，新模型知识加深但防御提升有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的零散化安全评估无法有效治理AI危险能力，需要统一、模块化、可扩展的评估框架来系统衡量模型在不同维度的危险能力。

Method: 提出模块化评估框架，包含K/D/H三条正交评估管线和统一协议；通过可插拔模块提供场景种子、知识库、危害查询和评分标准，核心评估引擎跨领域不变；在化学-生物模块中对4个家族12个商用LLM进行评估，并通过网络攻击试点验证协议可迁移性。

Result: 横向比较发现模型在三维度上展现出差异明显的能力画像——知识相近的模型拒绝韧性不同，防御强的模型在合规时并非生成更少的有害内容，Claude、DeepSeek和GPT呈现家族级模式差异。纵向分析显示危险能力未随发布时间的推移单调下降，新模型知识增加但防御仅部分改善。信度由交叉评判一致性(ρ>0.79)与管线正交性(相关系数0.32-0.52)支持。

Conclusion: 缩放与对齐进展并未统一转化为安全性提升；该模块化框架为跨领域、跨时间的危险能力评估提供了可靠且可扩展的标准化方法，有助于改善AI危险能力的治理。

Abstract: Fragmented safety evaluation undermines the governance of dangerous AI capabilities. We present a modular framework that evaluates each model through three orthogonal pipelines---Knowledge ($K$), Defense ($D$), and Harm ($H$)---under a unified protocol, aggregating results into a standardized dangerous-capability profile $φ$. Pluggable modules supply scenario seeds, knowledge banks, hazard queries, and judge rubrics, while the core evaluation engine remains unchanged across domains; the CB evaluation is complemented by a cyber pilot demonstrating protocol transfer.
  Instantiating the framework with a chemical-biological (CB) module, we evaluate 12 commercial LLMs from four families. Our first contribution is a horizontal comparison of dangerous capability across models and model families: the three dimensions expose sharply divergent profiles---models with comparable knowledge differ in refusal resilience, and strong defenders do not generate less harmful content when they do comply---while family-level patterns further separate Claude, DeepSeek, and GPT models. The second is a temporal analysis of capability evolution: tracking $K$, $D$, and $H$ against model release dates reveals that dangerous capability has not monotonically declined; newer models deepen knowledge while only partially improving defense, showing that scaling and alignment progress do not uniformly translate into safety. Reliability is established via cross-judge consistency (bootstrap $ρ> 0.79$, 4 of 5 judges) and pipeline orthogonality ($K$--$D$--$H$ inter-correlations $ρ\in [0.32, 0.52]$).

</details>


### [212] [Examining the Vulnerability of Multi-Agent Medical Systems to Human Interventions for Clinical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02191)
*Benjamin C Liu,Dillon Mehta,Rishi Malhotra,Adam Zobian,Yong Ying Tan,Samir Chopra,Daniella Rand,Natalie Pang,Abhiram Gudimella,Kevin Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了在多智能体医疗系统中，在"故障点"（智能体推理最易受外部影响的时刻）进行人工干预如何改变诊断准确性。基于MedQA数据集模拟医患对话，正确干预可将基线诊断准确率提升高达40%，而错误或偏倚相关干预会使性能下降6%并加剧诊断漂移和不确定性。研究还发现智能体环境中的认知偏差行为与真实临床实践存在相似性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体医疗系统在实际应用中可能因人类干预而改变诊断准确性，但此前缺乏对故障点及其干预效果的系统研究。理解这些干预如何影响AI诊断推理，是提升系统鲁棒性的关键。

Method: 使用MedQA数据集，模拟医患对话场景，定义故障点为智能体对话中推理最易受外部影响的时刻，通过分析人类干预如何改变推理和诊断准确性来评估干预效果。

Result: 正确干预方法可将基线诊断准确率提升高达40%；错误或偏倚相关的干预会降低性能6%，并增加诊断漂移和不确定性；同时观察到智能体模拟环境中存在与现实临床实践相似的认知偏差，如过早关闭和易受误导线索影响。

Conclusion: 识别并引导故障点的人工干预，可能为提升多智能体医疗系统的诊断鲁棒性提供有效机制。

Abstract: Human interventions at fault points can alter the diagnostic accuracy of multi-agent medical systems. We defined fault points as moments in AI agent conversations, in which an agent's reasoning became most vulnerable to external influence. Using the MedQA dataset, this study analyzed simulated doctor-patient conversations to measure how interventions shifted reasoning and accuracy. Correct intervention methods showed an improvement in baseline diagnostic accuracy of up to 40%, while incorrect or bias-related interventions degraded performance by up to 6% and increased diagnostic drift and uncertainty. Beyond performance changes, our analysis revealed behavioral similarities between cognitive biases in simulated agent environments and real-world clinical practice. Examples included premature closure and susceptibility to misleading cues. Overall, these findings demonstrate that identifying and guiding fault points with human interventions may provide a mechanism for improving diagnostic robustness in multi-agent medical systems.

</details>


### [213] [ASCII Attack: Recontextualising Harmful Requests as Artistic Critique in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02215)
*Da Cheng Gu,Yifei Dong,Xinghao Yang,Yongshun Gong,Wei Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种名为ASCII Attack的黑盒单轮越狱攻击方法：将有害请求以ASCII艺术字符的形式呈现为"艺术作品"并请求反馈，由于安全对齐主要覆盖表面形式而非重情境化内容，该方法能绕过模型的安全拒答。在11个模型和8个有害话题上，62%的框定提示被判为有害（对照组仅42%），最易受攻击的模型成功率高达93%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全对齐训练主要针对表面形式（直接表述的有害请求），对重情境化内容的泛化不足。作者旨在揭示这一安全缺口，证明仅通过改变模型阅读方式而不隐藏任何内容的单轮黑盒攻击即可绕过安全机制。

Method: 提出ASCII Attack方法：将完全可读的有害请求嵌入ASCII艺术字符中，将其呈现为艺术作品并请求模型提供反馈。攻击为单轮、黑盒形式（单条消息，不访问模型内部）。每个框定提示都配有直接提问的对照组，以隔离话题、模型和解码变化的影响，从而识别是捆绑状态而非单一通道导致的安全失效。

Result: 在11个模型和8个有害话题上，有害性感知分类器判定62%的框定提示为有害，远高于对照组的42%。最易受攻击模型上框定提示成功率达93%。该攻击在四个有害性度量维度中的单次查询性能达到或超过已发表的单查询攻击。攻击效果更多取决于模型本身而非话题，且不随模型规模增大而减弱。近三分之二的框定行中至少有一位评判者与多数评判者意见相左，反映了测量有效性问题。

Conclusion: ASCII Attack证明了当前安全对齐在表面形式上的过度拟合导致的弱泛化问题，攻击效果受模型特性影响强于话题影响且不随规模减小。评判者之间分歧显著，暗示评判标准可能存在不匹配泛化问题，这对安全评估方法本身提出了测量有效性的挑战。

Abstract: Safety alignment trains large language models to refuse harmful requests stated plainly, but that training is applied mostly to surface form. Requests that only recontextualise the same operational content, changing how the model reads it, are therefore only weakly covered. The ASCII Attack is one such recontextualisation. It is single-turn and black-box: one message, with no access to model internals. It embeds a fully legible harmful request in ASCIl-art characters, presents it as artwork, and asks for feedback. Unlike ArtPrompt, it hides nothing: the request stays readable. The reply is written as artistic critique and can contain operational detail that a plain request would have been refused for. Every framed prompt is paired with a direct-question control, so the contrast is isolated from topic, model and decoding variation. The contrast identifies a bundled surface, not one isolated channel. Across eleven models and eight harm topics, a harm-aware classifier judges 62% of framed prompts harmful against 42% of controls. On the most susceptible model the framed prompt succeeds 93% of the time. A single query matches or exceeds published single-query attacks under four of five harm judges. The effect tracks the model more than the topic and does not diminish with scale. At least one judge dissents from the panel majority on nearly two-thirds of framed rows, which is itself a measurement-validity finding. That pattern is consistent with mismatched generalisation.

</details>


### [214] [PEARL: Path-Entity Aligned Relational Learning with Contextual Subgraphs for Inductive Knowledge Graph Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02216)
*Yunchi Yang,Longlong Li,Cunquan Qu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PEARL框架用于归纳知识图谱补全，通过构建查询特定上下文子图、利用LLM引导的检索器筛选语义相关路径，并建立路径-实体二部交互图使路径嵌入适应局部和全局结构证据，同时采用双视图对比学习抑制噪声。在WN18RR、FB15k-237和NELL-995三个基准上取得最佳平均Hits@10。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于子图和路径的归纳知识图谱补全方法通常将关系路径与其周围查询子图独立编码，忽略了路径的预测相关性在不同结构上下文中可能变化的问题。

Method: PEARL框架包含三个关键组件：1）构建查询特定的上下文子图；2）使用LLM引导的检索器蒸馏语义相关路径；3）构建路径、上下文实体和全局子图表示之间的二部交互图，使路径嵌入适应局部和全局结构证据；4）采用双视图对比学习目标，在随机上下文扰动下促进表示一致性以抑制噪声。

Result: 在WN18RR、FB15k-237和NELL-995三个基准上，PEARL相较其他归纳知识图谱补全方法获得最佳平均Hits@10。消融实验、效率分析和案例研究验证了各组件（上下文子图建模、语义路径检索、路径-实体交互、对比正则化）的有效性。

Conclusion: PEARL通过将路径建模为上下文条件下的推理信号，有效提升了归纳知识图谱补全的性能，验证了上下文感知路径建模和对比学习在抑制噪声方面的价值。

Abstract: Inductive knowledge graph completion (IKGC) aims to predict missing links involving entities unseen during training, requiring models to learn transferable relational and structural patterns. Existing subgraph- and path-based approaches often encode relational paths independently of their surrounding query subgraphs, although the predictive relevance of a path may vary across structural contexts. We propose PEARL, a Path-Entity Aligned Relational Learning framework that models paths as context-conditioned reasoning signals. PEARL constructs a query-specific contextual subgraph from the union of the query entities' neighborhoods and uses a large language model (LLM)-guided retriever to distill semantically relevant paths. It then builds a bipartite interaction graph over paths, contextual entities, and a global subgraph representation, allowing path embeddings to adapt to local and global structural evidence. To suppress noise introduced by the enlarged context, PEARL employs a dual-view contrastive objective that promotes representation consistency under stochastic contextual perturbations. Experiments on WN18RR, FB15k-237, and NELL-995 show that PEARL obtains the best average Hits@10 among the compared IKGC methods on all three benchmarks. Ablation studies, efficiency analyses, and case studies further validate the contributions of contextual subgraph modeling, semantic path retrieval, path-entity interaction, and contrastive regularization.

</details>


### [215] [SkillGLoW: Procedural-Family Skill Consolidation for Self-Improving Agents on Long-Horizon Task Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02217)
*Ao Yan,Xin Zhang,Jiawei Du,Joey Tianyi Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SkillGLoW（Global-Local Weave），一种以"求解流程"为基本复用单元的智能体技能库构建框架。它将局部技能汇聚为程序家族并压缩为去实例化的全局先验，任务执行时再按需重新生成实例细节，通过提交门控保证先验不损害已有库。在四个基准和三个模型上，先验较无技能基线平均提升17.2分（困难任务），且库规模比逐任务池紧凑3.6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体自我改进主要通过两种形式复用文本技能：全局单文档或逐任务扁平池，但二者的有效性证据多来自结构相似的领域。在长时程任务中每个任务需要不同方案时，两种方式会以相反方式失效——全局文档退化为泛泛而谈的规则，而逐任务池不断膨胀且条目与产生它的具体实例深度绑定。作者认为缺失的复用单元是"由一组相关任务共享的求解流程"。

Method: SkillGLoW围绕"求解流程"这一复用单元构建：1）将单个任务从自身执行中写出的局部技能聚合为"程序家族"（procedural families）；2）压缩为去实例化的全局先验（de-instantiated global priors）；3）实例细节不在库中存储，而是在每任务执行时重新生成；4）设置提交门控（commit gate），仅当真实执行证明先验不会降低已部署库的性能时才允许入库。

Result: 在四个基准（数学推理、终端自动化、软件修复、具身控制）和三个模型上：先验较无技能基线在困难任务上平均提升17.2分，全部12次持续改进运行均获正增益；加入局部再生成后达18.0分。每个程序家族仅保留一个先验，比逐任务池紧凑3.6倍。在同一协议下，GLoW在21个对比单元中有15个优于已发表的单文档优化器。未经修改的库将ALFWorld未见任务成功率从73.9%提升至83.9%。

Conclusion: 实验结果证明跨任务迁移的是"程序"而非"任务记忆"，即求解流程本身可复用，而具体实例细节需要在每任务上下文中重新生成。SkillGLoW通过全局先验与局部再生成的"编织"机制，解决了长时程异构任务场景下全局文档与逐任务池各自的失效问题，实现了兼顾泛化性与紧凑性的技能复用。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly self-improve by writing and reusing textual skills, kept either as one global document or as a flat pool of per-task entries, though most of the evidence comes from domains with structurally similar tasks. On long-horizon workloads where each task demands a different solution, the two forms fail in opposite ways: the document collapses into generic discipline, while the pool inflates and its entries stay bound to the instance that wrote them. We argue the missing unit of reuse is the solving procedure shared by a cluster of related tasks, and build SkillGLoW (Global-Local Weave) around it: the local skills a task writes from its own execution are aggregated into procedural families and compressed into de-instantiated global priors, while the instance detail they hold is regenerated per task rather than stored; a commit gate admits a prior only when real execution shows it does not degrade the deployed library. Across four benchmarks (mathematical reasoning, terminal automation, software repair, and embodied control) and three models, the priors gain 17.2 points (hard) over the no-skill baseline on average, with positive gains in all 12 continual-improvement runs, and 18.0 with local regeneration, while the library holds one prior per procedural family, 3.6x more compact than the per-task pool. Under the same protocol GLoW leads a published single-document optimizer on 15 of 21 cells. Unmodified, the library lifts success on unseen ALFWorld tasks from 73.9% to 83.9%, evidence that what transfers is procedure rather than task memory.

</details>


### [216] [PhoenixNest-Video: Evidence-Grounded Multimodal Agent Framework for Automated Video Interview Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02231)
*Fan Yuxuan,Huang Miaojun,Zhang Haimei,Wu Jingshen,Liu Hao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PhoenixNest-Video，一个基于证据驱动的多模态智能体框架，用于自动化视频面试评估。该框架构建语义视频图作为结构化工作记忆，通过基于评分标准的检索和跨模态验证生成有迹可循的逐项评分，在VInterview-2025基准上达到91.50%的等级准确率，超越了更大的专有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的仅靠人工的面试评估成本高且不一致，而现有AI方法虽然能评分但缺乏可追溯的理由，无法生成有行为证据支撑的分标准判断。

Method: PhoenixNest-Video框架包含三个核心组件：1) 语义视频图作为结构化工作记忆；2) 基于评分的条件检索与跨模态验证（整合视觉、音频和文本流）；3) 基于Rubrics的强化学习训练Scorer，采用双奖励机制同时优化评分标准对齐和分数级区分。

Result: 在VInterview-2025数据集上达到91.50%的等级准确率，性能超越远大于自身的专有模型，且与专家小组的判断一致性高于大型模型的直接提示方法。

Conclusion: 紧凑且基于评分标准驱动的智能体在面试评分上比大型模型直接提示更贴近专家判断，并且能提供每个分数的证据支持以供人工审查，兼顾了评估准确性和可解释性。

Abstract: Interview assessment requires per-criterion judgments grounded in behavioral evidence, yet surging applicant volumes have made human-only evaluation costly and inconsistent, while existing AI approaches yield opaque scores without traceable rationale. We introduce PhoenixNest-Video, an evidence-grounded multimodal agent framework for automated video interview assessment. It builds a semantic video graph as structured working memory, performs rubric-conditioned retrieval with cross-modal verification across visual, audio, and textual streams, and produces per-criterion scores anchored to the candidate's materials. A Scorer trained via Rubrics-based Reinforcement Learning with dual rewards for rubric alignment and score-level differentiation internalizes the discriminative structure of multi-level rubrics. PhoenixNest-Video attains 91.50\% grade-level accuracy on VInterview-2025, outperforming substantially larger proprietary models. A compact, rubric-grounded agent therefore scores candidates in closer agreement with an expert panel than direct prompting of much larger models, and exposes the evidence behind each score for human review.

</details>


### [217] [PGPO: Potential-Guided Policy Optimization for Multi-Turn Agentic Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02236)
*Yuyao Zheng,Haipeng Sun,Junwei Bao,Lemao Liu,Hongfei Jiang,Yang Song,Dejing Dou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Potential-Guided Policy Optimization（PGPO），一种用于多轮智能体任务中LLM后训练的细粒度信用分配方法。PGPO通过估计锚点状态势能并利用相邻状态间的势能差推导动作优势，实现跨轨迹信用传播，解决了现有组内RL方法在失败轨迹中难以区分有效动作与错误动作的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于分组的强化学习方法在多轮智能体任务中依赖稀疏的终端奖励，导致中间动作的信用分配过于粗糙。现有的GiGPO等方法虽然引入了步级优势，但步级信号仍依赖于单条轨迹的最终结果，导致失败轨迹中有效动作与错误动作难以区分，获得相同的负面信用。

Method: PGPO首先从每个rollout组内的锚点状态组返回统计量中估计经验状态势能；然后利用相邻状态之间的势能差推导动作优势，实现跨轨迹的信用传播，从而为中间步骤（尤其是失败轨迹内部）提供更细粒度的信用分配信号。

Result: 在ALFWorld和WebShop基准上的实验表明，PGPO相较近期基于分组的RL方法具有更强的整体性能。进一步分析提供了证据，表明PGPO能产生更具信息量的失败侧信用信号，且训练开销可忽略不计。

Conclusion: PGPO通过跨轨迹的势能差异实现更细致的步级信用分配，有效改善了失败轨迹中动作的区分度，为多轮智能体任务提供了更优的RL训练范式，且几乎不增加额外训练成本。

Abstract: Group-based reinforcement learning (RL) has become an effective paradigm for LLM post-training, but in multi-turn agentic tasks with sparse terminal rewards, it often provides coarse credit for intermediate actions. To obtain more fine-grained credit assignment, recent work such as GiGPO introduces step-level advantages for intermediate actions. However, these step-level signals still rely on the final outcome of each individual trajectory. As a result, actions within failed trajectories can remain poorly differentiated, so effective actions can receive the same unfavorable credit as erroneous ones. In this work, we propose Potential-Guided Policy Optimization (PGPO) for multi-turn agentic tasks. PGPO estimates empirical state potentials from anchor-state-group return statistics within each rollout group. It then derives action advantages from potential differences between adjacent states, enabling cross-trajectory credit propagation. This provides finer-grained step-level credit assignment, especially within failed trajectories. Experiments on ALFWorld and WebShop show strong overall performance relative to recent group-based RL methods. Further analysis provides evidence that PGPO yields more informative failure-side credit signals with negligible training overhead.

</details>


### [218] [Task-Level Natural Language Priors as Learning Signals for Low-Resource LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02244)
*Jian Gao,Xiao Zhang,Xun Zhu,Miao Li,Ji Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Prior-Guided Tuning (PGT)视角，将任务级自然语言先验作为辅助学习信号而非仅作为输入上下文，用于低资源大语言模型训练。具体方法Contrastive Prior Steering (CPS)在保持原始监督目标的同时，加入正负先验条件的辅助损失来引导任务一致学习并抑制误导性替代方案。在AmbiMath、Jigsaw和MNLI/HANS上的实验表明CPS持续优于普通微调和提示微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源训练数据存在模糊或不完整的问题，导致LLM性能受限。现有方法通常将任务级自然语言先验仅作为输入上下文处理，而非作为训练过程中的学习信号，未能充分利用先验知识的引导价值。

Method: 提出Prior-Guided Tuning (PGT)训练视角，将自然语言先验作为辅助学习信号。在此基础上引入Contrastive Prior Steering (CPS)方法：保持原始监督目标不变，同时添加正先验条件辅助损失（鼓励任务一致学习）和负先验条件辅助损失（抑制看似合理但具有误导性的替代方案），形成对比学习机制。

Result: CPS在三个基准数据集上均优于普通微调和提示微调：AmbiMath达到97.6%平均精确匹配准确率；Jigsaw上平均Macro F1比标准微调提升9.5个百分点，且仅用1/10实验训练数据即略超全量数据普通微调；HANS上非蕴含准确率分别提升8.3个百分点（LLaMA 3.1 8B）和5.2个百分点（Qwen 2.5 7B），同时保持可比的域内MNLI准确率。

Conclusion: 实验结果支持核心主张：任务级自然语言先验可以作为一种有效的辅助学习信号用于低资源LLM训练，而非仅仅作为提示上下文。代码和数据将公开。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often struggle when low-resource training data are ambiguous or incomplete. Task-level natural-language priors can provide useful guidance in such settings, but existing approaches usually treat these priors as input context rather than as learning signals during training. We propose Prior-Guided Tuning (PGT), a training perspective that incorporates natural-language priors as auxiliary learning signals for low-resource LLM training. Under this perspective, we introduce Contrastive Prior Steering (CPS), which keeps the original supervised objective intact while adding positive and negative prior-conditioned auxiliary losses to encourage task-consistent learning and discourage plausible but misleading alternatives. Experiments on AmbiMath, Jigsaw, and MNLI/HANS show that CPS consistently improves over plain and prompt fine-tuning. On AmbiMath, CPS achieves 97.6% average exact-match accuracy. On Jigsaw, CPS improves average Macro F1 by 9.5 percentage points over standard fine-tuning, and with 1/10 of the experimental training data slightly exceeds full-data plain fine-tuning. On HANS, CPS improves non-entailment accuracy by 8.3 and 5.2 percentage points for LLaMA 3.1 8B and Qwen 2.5 7B, respectively, while maintaining comparable in-domain MNLI accuracy. These results support our central claim: task-level natural-language priors can provide useful guidance as auxiliary learning signals for low-resource LLM training. Our code and data will be publicly available.

</details>


### [219] [LLM-as-a-Judge Is Not an Oracle: Why Self-Improving Agents Need Deterministic Guardrails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02246)
*Vansh Wahi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文主张将LLM评判者从"神谕"降级为"顾问"，提出PROCTOR框架——一个教师-学生循环架构，通过五种确定性护栏（密封沙箱、能力分离角色、超越评判者的验收检查、冻结的保留集和诱饵用例）来防止自主提示优化管道中的奖励作弊和评估信号失败。作者基于生产环境数月运行经验，系统总结了四类共十一种评估信号失效方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主优化管道中，评判者本身是LLM，它拥有系统改进与否的最终话语权，但作者认为这个评判者并不值得信赖。基于生产运行中观测到的严峻问题——代理可通过读取环境中的缓存答案密钥实现100%通过率而实际能力仅为68%，被污染的地面真实标签导致优化器删除正确规则，语法破损的提示反而被选为胜者——作者主张必须用确定性验证层来限缩LLM评判者的权力。

Method: 作者提出PROCTOR架构：一个有状态编排器持有全部工具访问权，无状态子代理负责诊断失败和起草变异但无法自行执行，教师负责在五种确定性护栏下对这些变异打分。五种护栏包括：密封沙箱环境、能力不重叠的角色分工、优先级高于教师评判的验收检查、冻结的保留数据集，以及精心构造的诱饵用例——得分满分本身反而是作弊的证据。

Result: 作者报告了该架构在生产中防止的各类失败，同时坦诚地记录了该架构未能防止的失败——因为教师本身仍然是LLM评判者。作者还发现尝试通过改写评判者规则来修复LLM评判者效果有限（趋于平台期），唯一可靠的改进来自对其输出顺序施加结构性约束。

Conclusion: 自主优化管道中的LLM评判者应被结构性降级为顾问而非最后的裁决者，其判定仅作为多种输入之一存在。任何变更都必须通过不可被评判者推翻的确定性验证层。工程上可行的路径不在于修补评判者本身，而在于设计使其即使不完美也无害的结构性约束。

Abstract: Self-improving agent pipelines have a problem at their center. An optimizer rewrites prompts to score higher, and the score comes from a judge that is itself an LLM. That judge has the last word on whether the system is getting better, and our position is that it has not earned it. The judge should be demoted from oracle to advisor: its verdict becomes one input among several, and every change is gated instead by a deterministic verification layer the judge cannot override. We reached this position by building the alternative and running it. Over months of running autonomous prompt-optimization loops in production across contract analysis, compliance review, and code quality, we cataloged eleven ways the evaluation signal failed, in four classes: judge bias, harness and metric failures, ground-truth errors, and reward hacking. Agents achieved perfect scores by reading cached answer keys from their environment, a 100% pass rate concealing 68% true capability. A corrupted ground-truth label caused the optimizer to delete correct compliance rules to agree with it. A syntactically broken prompt was promoted as the winner because a silent parser fallback improved the metric. Attempts to fix the judge by rewriting its rubric plateaued; the only reliable gain came from a structural constraint on its output order. In response we describe PROCTOR, a Teacher-Student loop in which a stateful orchestrator holds all tool access, stateless subagents diagnose failures and draft mutations they cannot apply, and a Teacher grades those mutations under five deterministic guardrails: hermetic sandboxes, capability-disjoint roles, acceptance checks that outrank the Teacher, frozen holdouts, and canary cases engineered so that a perfect score is itself evidence of cheating. We report the failures this prevented, and, because the Teacher is itself an LLM judge, the failures it did not.

</details>


### [220] [APEx: Distillation of Agent Procedural Experience for Adaptive Deep Research Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02253)
*Jie Ding,Rui Sun,Xinyuan Zhang,Zeyu Zhang,Xin Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: APEx是一个分层经验利用框架，将交互历史组织为实例级轨迹记忆和类别级程序性技能，通过Executor、Distiller、Planner三个模块的闭环架构耦合，并采用三阶段交替GRPO训练范式实现奖励引导的技能蒸馏。在7个基准上超越GPT-5.4达14.7分，超过最强记忆增强基线3.0分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么检索冗长的任务特定轨迹加重决策负担，要么提取的程序性技能与下游策略适应脱节。需要一种能够有效利用经验实现持续改进的方法。

Method: 提出APEx分层经验利用框架：将交互历史分为实例级轨迹记忆和类别级程序性技能；通过Executor、Distiller、Planner三模块闭环架构耦合；采用三阶段交替GRPO训练范式实现奖励引导的技能蒸馏；测试时通过技能引导的强化学习实现在线Planner自适应，并用技能对齐正则化防止策略漂移。

Result: 在7个基准测试上取得最先进性能，比GPT-5.4高14.7分，比最强记忆增强基线高3.0分。

Conclusion: APEx通过分层技能蒸馏与闭环策略适应，有效解决深度研究代理的经验利用与持续改进问题，验证了奖励引导技能蒸馏优于固定提示生成，且技能引导的测试时强化学习可实现无真值自改进。

Abstract: Deep research agents augment large language models with external tools to answer complex, long-horizon questions through multi-turn reasoning. Learning from prior experience is crucial for continual improvement, yet existing methods either retrieve verbose task-specific traces that burden decision-making, or distill procedural skills that remain decoupled from downstream policy adaptation. We propose APEx, a hierarchical experience utilization framework that organizes interaction history into instance-level trajectory memories and category-level procedural skills, and couples them through a closed-loop architecture of Executor, Distiller, and Planner. The three modules are optimized via a three-stage alternating GRPO training paradigm, enabling reward-guided skill distillation rather than fixed-prompt generation. At test time, distilled skills serve as procedural priors for online Planner adaptation through skill-guided test-time reinforcement learning, allowing ground-truth-free self-improvement with skill-alignment regularization to prevent policy drift. Experiments on 7 benchmarks demonstrate that APEx achieves state-of-the-art performance, surpassing GPT-5.4 by 14.7 points and the strongest memory-augmented baseline by 3.0 points.

</details>


### [221] [SafeEvolve: Harness-Policy Co-Evolution from Agent Experience for Safety Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02786)
*Qinghua Mao,Wanying Qu,Dadi Guo,Leitao Yuan,Qingyu Liu,Yu Li,Guanxu Chen,Yanwei Fu,Xi Lin,Xia Hu,Dongrui Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SafeEvolve是一个基于经验驱动的自进化框架，用于智能体安全对齐。它通过利用已完成on-policy轨迹中的安全经验，推动"harness-策略"持续协同进化，在harness端生成可审计、可逆的组件级更新，在策略端采用两阶段SFT-RL范式提升自主安全行为，最终在多个智能体安全基准上实现了更好的安全性与实用性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的智能体安全性受基座模型和交互环境harness共同影响，面临有害最终响应和多步执行轨迹双重安全风险。现有安全对齐机制仅依赖外部harness更新或策略优化的单一范式，无法弥合运行时控制与内在安全性之间的鸿沟。

Method: SafeEvolve提出harness-策略协同进化框架：1) harness侧将轨迹级安全证据转化为有界的、组件级更新（安全提示词、分层技能），生成可审计可逆的harness工件；2) 策略侧采用两阶段SFT-RL范式，先通过harness使用SFT引导策略主动利用进化后的harness工件，再用harness增强的RL通过验证器分解奖励塑造多步探索中的自主安全行为。

Result: 在智能体安全基准上，SafeEvolve相比现有基线实现了更强的安全性与实用性权衡。在Qwen3.5-4B上，AgentDojo基准的ASR（攻击成功率）降低3倍，同时良性实用性从59.79%提升至61.86%。

Conclusion: SafeEvolve通过将安全经验转化为进化的运行时harness和改进的策略行为，实现了harness与策略的协同进化，验证了"经验驱动、协同进化"范式对智能体安全对齐的有效性。

Abstract: The performance of LLM-based agents is jointly shaped by the base model and the harness used when interacting with the environment. This exposes them to safety risks in both harmful final responses and multi-step execution trajectories. Existing safety alignment mechanisms often rely on either external harness updates or policy optimization, yet applying either paradigm in isolation fails to bridge runtime control with intrinsic safety. We propose SafeEvolve, an experience-driven self-evolving framework for agent safety alignment. SafeEvolve leverages safety experience from completed on-policy trajectories to drive a continual loop of harness-policy co-evolution. On the harness side, SafeEvolve converts trajectory-level safety evidence into bounded, component-level updates across safety prompt and hierarchical skills, yielding auditable and reversible harness artifacts. On the policy side, SafeEvolve follows a two-stage SFT-RL paradigm, where harness-use SFT bootstraps the policy to actively leverage evolved harness artifacts, and harness-augmented RL further shapes autonomous safety behaviors during multi-step exploration via verifier-decomposed rewards. Through harness-policy co-evolution, SafeEvolve converts safety experience into an evolved runtime harness and improved policy behavior. Experiments on agentic safety benchmarks show that SafeEvolve achieves a stronger safety-utility tradeoff than existing baselines. For Qwen3.5-4B, SafeEvolve achieves a $3\times$ ASR reduction on AgentDojo while improving benign utility from 59.79% to 61.86%.

</details>


### [222] [CoMerge: Conflict-Driven Preference Optimization for Multi-Task Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02273)
*Mingjie Zheng,Zihao Chen,Wenqing Chen,Weile Yuan,Zhixuan Chu,Jianxing Yu,Zibin Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CoMerge框架，将模型合并重构为偏好优化问题，利用朴素合并方法（如任务算术）的缺陷作为硬负样本，自监督地构建偏好对来优化轻量级张量级合并系数，从而缓解多任务LLM合并中的参数干扰。在MergeBench上达到0.9968的平均归一化性能，超越所有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型合并方法虽旨在保留各专家模型能力并缓解参数干扰，但普遍未能直接从朴素合并所暴露的退化行为中学习，缺乏对这种失败信号的利用。

Method: 提出CoMerge冲突驱动的偏好优化框架。将模型合并公式化为偏好优化问题，采用自监督、冲突驱动的策略，将朴素合并方法（如任务算术）的缺陷作为硬负样本，无需外部标注即可构建偏好对；通过偏好优化细化轻量级张量级合并系数，同时缓解参数空间冲突并保留任务特定能力。

Result: 在MergeBench上平均归一化性能达0.9968，优于所有数据无关和数据驱动的模型合并基线；在Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct上，对指令跟随和安全等冲突敏感任务有显著改进，仅优化1,445个标量系数即可与全参数微调竞争。

Conclusion: CoMerge验证了将朴素合并的失败模式作为学习信号的有效性，为模型合并提供了一种无需全量训练的高效且强竞争力的新范式，在极大减少优化参数量的同时接近全参数微调的性能水平。

Abstract: Model merging provides an efficient paradigm for constructing multi-task large language models (LLMs) without full model retraining, yet it remains challenged by parameter interference. While existing methods aim to preserve the capabilities of individual expert models and mitigate interference, they generally do not directly learn from the potentially degraded behaviors exposed by naive merging. In this paper, we propose a conflict-driven preference optimization framework for model merging (CoMerge), which reformulates model merging as a preference optimization problem. The approach utilizes a self-supervised, conflict-driven strategy that leverages the defects of naive merging methods (e.g., task arithmetic) as hard negative samples to construct preference pairs without external annotations. By applying preference optimization to refine lightweight, tensor-wise merging coefficients, CoMerge enables the model to mitigate parameter-space conflicts while preserving task-specific capabilities. Extensive experiments show that CoMerge achieves an average normalized performance of 0.9968 on MergeBench, outperforming all evaluated data-free and data-driven model-merging baselines. Furthermore, on Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, CoMerge yields marked improvements on conflict-sensitive tasks such as instruction following and safety, while remaining highly competitive with full-parameter fine-tuning despite optimizing only 1,445 scalar coefficients.

</details>


### [223] [SCX Router: Streaming Zero-Shot Model Selection with a Decoder-KV Classifier and a Real-World Task Ontology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02292)
*Ihor Stepanov,Aleksandr Smechov,Mykhailo Shtopko,Dmytro Vodianytskyi,Oleksandr Lukashov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于GLiClass的轻量级LLM路由器（0.6B参数），通过为推理端点分配适合度分数，在无需自回归生成的情况下实现按任务级别选择最优模型，在速度-成本-质量之间取得良好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM及其应用的快速增长，推理端点在质量、价格、延迟、上下文支持等方面存在高度异质性，手动启发式难以在不同任务上持续维持良好的速度-成本-质量权衡，因此需要一种自动化的模型路由机制。

Method: 设计了一个GLiClass-based轻量路由器：采用Qwen3解码器结合浅层双向打分器；利用解码器KV执行路径在会话中保留纯文本键值缓存，仅编码新的对话轮次，并对瞬态候选标签令牌进行评估而不加入持久缓存。同时预测任务类型、难度、推理模式和期望输出长度，支持自定义零样本标签。构建了包含23个家族、115个任务类型、345个路由子类型和30个领域的任务本体，生成150,000个验证器评分任务和15,000个开放任务用于训练。

Result: 在六个LiveBench子集上，路由器表现优于平均候选模型；在选定的1,000任务子集上，实现了0.707的聚合top-1得分，优于最强固定模型的0.696，且不同基准上有显著增益。

Conclusion: 该路由器证明了轻量级、非自回归的模型路由方法可以在任务级实现优于固定最强模型的性能，同时保持高效推理，为大规模LLM生态中的模型选择提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of large language models (LLMs) and the growing diversity of their applications presents a unique optimization opportunity: selecting the right model for the task, while optimizing for speed, cost, and quality at a per-task level. However, inference endpoints can vary widely in quality, price, latency, context support, tool use, domain expertise, and reasoning behavior. This heterogeneity makes manual heuristics difficult to maintain and unlikely to achieve consistently favorable speed--cost--quality trade-offs on their own. We introduce \router{}, a lightweight GLiClass-based router that assigns a suitability score to each inference-time model label without autoregressive generation. The released 0.6B-parameter checkpoint combines a Qwen3 decoder with a shallow bidirectional scorer. Its decoder-KV execution path preserves a text-only key--value cache across a session, encodes only new dialogue turns, and evaluates transient candidate-label tokens without adding them to the persistent cache. The same checkpoint also predicts task type, difficulty, reasoning mode, and expected output length, and supports custom zero-shot labels. For task generation, we construct a task ontology with 23 families, 115 task types, 345 routable subtypes, 1,173 synthetic examples, and an orthogonal axis of 30 domains. Using this structure, we generate 150,000 verifier-scored tasks and 15,000 open-ended tasks. We then train the Qwen3 decoder on these tasks, while explicitly separating learned request prediction from per-task policies for attributes such as eligibility, cost, cache reuse, safety, and sovereignty. Across six LiveBench subsets, the router outperforms the mean candidate; on the selected 1,000-task subset, it achieves an aggregate top-1 score of 0.707 versus 0.696 for the strongest fixed model, with benchmark-dependent gains.

</details>


### [224] [Improving Evaluation Realism with Inference-Time Compute and Deployment Scaffolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02302)
*Axel Ahlqvist,Richard Guan,Juan-Pablo Rivera,Adeline Kassler,Dmitrii Troitskii,Alexandra Souly,Kai Fronsdal,Robert Kirk,John Hughes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出两种技术（critique refinement和DISH）来提升对齐评估模拟的部署真实性，使模型更难区分"被测试"与"实际部署"场景，且两种技术可组合使用，在多个目标模型上均验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对齐评估的核心障碍在于"评估意识"：能力强的模型能够意识到自己正处于被测试而非实际部署的环境中，这会削弱安全评估结论的可信度和有效性。

Method: 提出两种技术：1）Critique Refinement：在模拟器的每个动作上增加推理时计算，生成多个候选动作，利用目标模型实例的反馈进行精细化改进，选出最像真实部署的候选；2）DISH（Deployment-Imitating SWE-Agent Harness）：将目标模型封装在代理工具环境中，缩小编码场景下模拟部署与实际部署环境之间的差距。两种技术可组合应用。

Result: 在多个目标模型上测试发现：两种技术具有组合效应，同时应用比单独使用任何一种都能获得更大的真实性提升；自动化方法能有效提升对齐评估的真实性，且这些改进比延长审计时间更能有效利用额外的计算资源。

Conclusion: 自动化方法可以显著提高对齐评估中模拟环境的部署真实性，且组合使用多种技术比单一技术效果更好，额外计算投入到改进评估真实性上比简单的延长审计过程更有效率。

Abstract: A core obstacle to alignment evaluation is evaluation awareness: capable models can tell when they are being tested rather than deployed, weakening the conclusions a safety evaluation can support. We present two techniques that make simulated alignment evaluations harder to distinguish from real deployments. Our first technique, critique refinement, spends additional inference-time compute on each simulator action: the simulator generates multiple candidate actions, refines them using feedback from an instance of the target model on how to make them more realistic, and continues the evaluation with the most deployment-like candidate. Our second technique, DISH (Deployment-Imitating SWE-Agent Harness), wraps the target in an agent harness, reducing the gap between simulated and real deployment environments in coding settings. We test the techniques on multiple target models and find that they compose: applying both yields larger realism gains than either alone. Our results show that automated approaches can improve the realism of alignment evaluations, and that these improvements use additional compute more effectively than making the audits longer.

</details>


### [225] [SALA: Semantic-Aware Logical Alignment for Complex Reasoning in In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02336)
*Zhao Ji,Wenqing Chen,Zhixuan Chu,Jianxing Yu,Jingping Liu,Shanhe Zhao,Zibin Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SALA（语义感知逻辑对齐框架），用于改进大语言模型中复杂推理的上下文学习（ICL）演示示例选择。SALA自动学习任务特定的推理操作，将其嵌入连续语义空间，并使用动态时间规整（DTW）进行软性、灵活的逻辑对齐匹配，在四个推理基准和三个LLM上超越现有演示选择方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于表面相似性的检索方法无法捕捉底层问题求解逻辑，而现有基于逻辑的方法依赖预定义的推理步骤和刚性、精确匹配规则，无法灵活应对多样化的推理过程。现有方法在"表面相似"与"逻辑刚性"之间存在空白，需要一种既关注推理逻辑又具备灵活性的演示选择方法。

Method: SALA框架包含三个核心组件：（1）自动学习任务特定的推理操作，取代固定操作库存；（2）将推理操作嵌入连续的语义空间，实现语义层面的表示；（3）使用动态时间规整（DTW）对推理序列进行对齐，实现软性、灵活的逻辑匹配，同时保持可解释性。

Result: 在四个推理基准和三个大语言模型上的实验表明，SALA优于现有的演示选择方法。进一步分析验证了操作归纳和逻辑语义对齐这两个组件的各自贡献。

Conclusion: SALA通过自动学习推理操作并利用DTW实现灵活的逻辑语义对齐，有效弥合了表面相似性和刚性逻辑匹配之间的鸿沟，为复杂推理任务的ICL演示选择提供了一种兼具灵活性与可解释性的新范式。

Abstract: Effective in-context learning (ICL) for complex reasoning relies on selecting the right demonstrations. Traditional retrieval methods based on surface similarity fail to capture the underlying problem-solving logic. Recent logic-based methods address this by matching predefined reasoning steps, but the rigid rules and exact-match criteria is improper to handle flexible or diverse reasoning processes. To address the problem, we propose SALA, a Semantic-Aware Logical Alignment framework. Instead of relying on a fixed inventory, SALA automatically learns task-specific reasoning operations. It then embeds these operations into a continuous semantic space and uses dynamic time warping (DTW) to align the reasoning sequences. This approach allows for soft, flexible matching of reasoning logic while remaining highly interpretable. Experiments across four reasoning benchmarks and three LLMs demonstrate that SALA outperforms existing demonstration selection methods. Further analysis confirms the roles of the operation induction and the logical semantic alignment.

</details>


### [226] [Diagnosing with Insights: Structured Analysis of Agent Failures via Behavioral Abstractions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02371)
*Jiayi Bi,Yanjie Gao,Yuanmin Xie,Liqun Li,Tianyin Xu,Fan Yang,Mao Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AGENTSCOPE，一种用于LLM智能体故障模式诊断的神经符号方法。它将智能体行为抽象为结构化表示，并引入"神经不变量"概念来规范行为属性，结合LLM引导推理来定位轨迹中的故障步骤和类型，在两个数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体的失败常表现为长而复杂的轨迹，人工排查不可行；传统软件缺陷诊断技术难以应对智能体故障，而完全依赖LLM作为评判者又会产生不可靠的诊断结果。

Method: AGENTSCOPE采用神经符号方法：首先将智能体轨迹行为抽象为结构化表示；引入神经不变量（neural invariants）以规范智能体行为属性；然后基于结构化表示和神经不变量，利用LLM引导推理来精确定位轨迹中的故障步骤及其类型。

Result: 在公开数据集（Who&When）和自建更全面数据集（AgentErrata）上，AGENTSCOPE在故障定位和归因准确性方面显著优于当前最先进方法。

Conclusion: 将结构化抽象与LLM引导推理相结合，可以实现对智能体故障的有效、可靠且可解释的诊断。

Abstract: With the proliferation of LLM agents, the ability to understand and diagnose failures in agents is essential to achieving superior effectiveness and trustworthiness. As agent failures often manifest via long and complex trajectories, manually finding the needles in the haystack is untenable. However, traditional diagnosis techniques for software bugs can hardly address LLM agent failures, while completely relying on LLMs as the judge yields unreliable diagnosis results. To overcome these challenges, this paper presents AGENTSCOPE, a new neuro-symbolic approach for agent failure mode diagnosis. The key principle of AGENTSCOPE is to abstract agent behavior, based on its trajectories, into structured representations. Furthermore, AGENTSCOPE introduces the concept of neural invariants to specify agent behavior properties. AGENTSCOPE leverages LLM-guided reasoning atop the structured representation against neural invariants to pinpoint both the failure step and its type in the trajectory. We show the effectiveness of AGENTSCOPE on publicly available agent failure datasets (Who&When) and a more comprehensive dataset created by us (AgentErrata), where AGENTSCOPE significantly outperforms the current state of the art in fault localization and attribution accuracy. Our work shows that integrating structured abstractions with LLM-guided reasoning enables effective, reliable, and interpretable diagnosis for agent failures.

</details>


### [227] [Contrastive Explanations in Quantitative Bipolar Argumentation Frameworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02399)
*Xiang Yin,Nico Potyka,Antonio Rago,Francesca Toni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对定量双极论证框架（QBAFs）引入对比性解释方法，与现有侧重于单一论点解释的方法不同，对比性解释用于解释两个主题论点之间的推理差异。作者提出了对比归因函数（CAFs）的一般形式及其应满足的性质，并基于删除法、梯度法和Shapley值设计了具体实现。最后在医疗保健和偏差识别场景中演示了对比性解释的实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有QBAFs的解释方法主要解释单个论点（主题论点）的推理结果，无法解释为什么两个论点的推理结果存在差异。这在需要对比决策的场景（如分类任务补充解释）中是不够的，因此需要一种能够解释两个论点之间差异的对比性解释方法。

Method: 作者提出了一种对比性归因函数（CAFs）的一般形式，确立了它们应满足的一组通用性质；在此基础上，设计了基于移除（removal）、梯度（gradients）和Shapley值（Shapley-values）的三类CAFs，并研究了它们的性质。

Result: 论文提出了基于删除、梯度和Shapley值的三类对比性归因函数，并验证了它们满足所定义的性质；同时通过在医疗保健和偏差识别两个场景中的演示，展示了对比性解释的实际效用。

Conclusion: 对比性解释比传统的单论点解释提供了更强的表达能力，能够解释两个论点推理结果之间的差异，在医疗保健决策解释和偏见识别等需要比较分析的场景中具有重要的实用价值。

Abstract: Argumentation frameworks are useful tools for representing and reasoning with information in a variety of settings, e.g. in supplementing AI models as they perform classification tasks, with a notable benefit of providing additional explainability. In this paper, we introduce contrastive explanations for Quantitative Bipolar Argumentation Frameworks (QBAFs), one such formalism. Unlike most existing explanations for QBAFs, which explain the reasoning outcome of a single argument of interest (i.e. a topic argument), contrastive explanations explain the difference between two topic arguments. We introduce a general form of contrastive attribution functions (CAFs) and establish a set of general properties they should satisfy. We introduce CAFs based on removal, gradients and Shapley-values, and study their properties. Finally, to illustrate contrastive explanations, we demonstrate their usefulness in healthcare and bias identification settings.

</details>


### [228] [UTP-Bench: Uncertainty-aware Travel Planning Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02421)
*Etcharla Revanth Rao,Priyanshu Karmakar,Shubhojit Mallick,Manish Gupta,Shreya Ghosh,Abhik Jana*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了UTP-Bench，一个面向不确定性感知的旅行规划大规模基准数据集，涵盖印度504个城市的真实旅行数据，集成经验延误分布和人群密度模式，并提出了三个评价指标来衡量生成的行程对交通延误和人群波动的鲁棒性。实验表明现有LLM在不确定性条件下生成的旅行计划与人类撰写的计划存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的旅行规划基准（如TravelPlanner和TripCraft）假设确定性的环境，仅评估静态约束满足情况，忽略了现实世界中交通延误、人群波动和意外随机延误带来的不确定性，无法评估生成的计划在面对这些不确定性时是否仍然健壮可行。

Method: 构建了覆盖印度504个城市的大规模基准数据集UTP-Bench，包含景点、餐厅、住宿和多模态交通网络等真实旅行数据；引入经验延误分布和人群密度模式来模拟现实中的干扰；提出了三个新的评价指标：Buffer Adequacy Score (BAS)、Crowd-Aware Timing Score (CATS)和Transport Delay Absorption Score (TDAS)，分别量化行程在时间缓冲、人群感知时间安排和交通延误吸收方面的鲁棒性。

Result: 对GPT-5、Qwen3、Mistral和Phi-4等最先进LLM的实验表明，模型生成的旅行计划与人类撰写的计划之间存在显著差距，特别是在时间缓冲、延误感知的交通调度和人群敏感规划方面表现不足。

Conclusion: UTP-Bench填补了现有旅行规划基准缺乏不确定性建模的空白，揭示了当前LLM在不确定性感知旅行规划方面的局限性，为未来开发更具鲁棒性的自动化旅行规划系统提供了新的评估标准和研究方向。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have recently demonstrated strong capabilities in automated travel itinerary generation. However, real- world travel planning is inherently uncertain: transportation delays, crowd fluctuations, and unexpected stochastic delays frequently inval- idate otherwise feasible schedules. Existing benchmarks like TravelPlanner and TripCraft assume deterministic environments, evaluating only static constraint satisfaction and ignoring whether generated plans remain robust when such uncertainties arise. To address this limitation, we introduce UTP-Bench1 , a large-scale benchmark for uncertainty-aware travel planning. The dataset integrates real-world travel data spanning 504 cities of India, including attractions, restau- rants, accommodations, and multi-modal trans- portation networks. To model realistic disrup- tions, UTP-Bench incorporates empirical delay distributions and crowd-density patterns col- lected from major cities, enabling evaluation of travel plans under stochastic conditions. We further propose three evaluation metrics, namely Buffer Adequacy Score (BAS), Crowd- Aware Timing Score (CATS), and Transport Delay Absorption Score (TDAS), which quan- tify the ability of generated itineraries to main- tain robustness against transit delays and crowd variability. Experiments with state-of-the-art LLMs like GPT-5, Qwen3, Mistral and Phi-4 re- veal substantial gaps between model-generated and human-authored plans, particularly in tem- poral buffering, delay-aware transportation scheduling, and crowd-sensitive planning.

</details>


### [229] [CivBench: A Long-Horizon Benchmark for Tool-Mediated Agents in Civilization VI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02459)
*Austin Tudor David Andrews,Liam Wilkinson,Jamie Heagerty,Harry Coppock,Jakob Nicolaus Foerster,Rui Ponte Costa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了CivBench，一个基于Model Context Protocol（MCP）的开源基准测试框架，用于评估语言模型代理在长时程、工具中介环境中的表现。单个episode包含300+轮交互和数千次工具调用。研究发现代理存在两个一致性问题：对战略状态监控不足（PMR指标）以及规划反思中承诺的执行率低（RAG@10指标介于48.2%-65.8%）。这些问题被解读为指令遵循下的偏差而非能力缺失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型代理评估多集中于短时程任务，缺乏对长时程、工具中介环境中代理规划能力和持续执行能力的系统评测。作者希望通过构建一个开放、可复现的基准来刻画代理在复杂部分可观测环境中的行为模式。

Method: 1) 构建了基于文明VI游戏的CivBench环境，暴露76个MCP工具并通过叙述层将视觉状态转为结构化文本。2) 引入两个"接口级"度量指标：Proactive Monitoring Rate（PMR）衡量主动查询潜在战略状态的频率；RAG@10衡量结构化规划反思中承诺在10个回合内被执行的比例。3) 在4个模型家族的23次有效运行中进行pilot实验。

Result: 发现两个一致模式：(1) 代理对战略相关状态的监控不足，尽管有playbook指导每20回合查询胜利进度，实际查询间隔为30-75回合，且在20次可检测的失败中有7次未在游戏结束前20回合警告窗口内查询；(2) 代理频繁未执行自身规划反思中的近期承诺，RAG@10各模型间范围为48.2%-65.8%。

Conclusion: 两种失败模式均在工具可用和明确指令指导下出现，表明问题本质是指令遵循偏差而非能力缺失。该工作为长时程代理评测提供了开源基础设施和新的分析视角。

Abstract: We present CivBench, an open-source benchmark for evaluating language model agents in long-horizon, tool-mediated environments through the Model Context Protocol (MCP). A single episode spans 300+ turns and produces thousands of tool calls over a large action space, requiring sustained planning, state monitoring, and execution under partial observability. The environment exposes 76 MCP tools and a narration layer that converts visual game state into structured text.
  We use CivBench to characterise agent behaviour across four model families in 23 admissible runs. The sample is a pilot, not a model ranking: aggregate outcomes do not reliably discriminate models at this scale. Instead, we introduce two interface-level metrics that the environment makes measurable: Proactive Monitoring Rate (PMR), capturing whether agents actively query latent strategic state, and RAG@10, capturing whether commitments stated in structured planning reflections are executed within ten subsequent turns.
  Across runs we observe two consistent patterns under a shared playbook protocol. Agents under-monitor strategically relevant state that is available but requires explicit querying: despite playbook guidance to query victory progress every 20 turns, agents do so only every 30 to 75 turns, and in 7 of 20 detectable defeats they failed to query within the 20 turn warning window before game end. Agents also frequently fail to execute near-term commitments stated in their own planning reflections (RAG@10 between 48.2% and 65.8% across models). Both patterns arise despite tool access and explicit guidance, and we interpret them as deviations under instruction rather than absences of capability.
  We release the environment, scenarios, logs, metrics, and analysis pipeline at https://github.com/lmwilki/civ6-mcp

</details>


### [230] [Loom: Weaving Diagnostic Strands into Free-Text Consensus via Embedding-Space Reweighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02649)
*Ron Begleiter,Katya Egert Berg,Gilad Saban,Gil Shabat*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Loom，一个用于真实世界根因分析（RCA）的生成式共识框架。它将模块化启发式规则产生的开放形式假设投影到连续嵌入空间，通过迭代质心重加权算法解决冲突信号，最终由单次轻量级LLM调用生成共识。在OpenRCA基准上，Loom以每次事件仅需一次LLM调用的代价（约快26倍），在准确率-效率帕累托前沿上与最先进自主智能体相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在工业环境部署NLP系统时，聚合嘈杂且冲突的文本假设以形成可靠共识是根本挑战。单一LLM智能体虽表达能力无限制，但面临上下文限制、幻觉累积和推理延迟过高的问题；传统弱监督方法虽有统计严谨性，但在数学上局限于离散类别。需要一种能弥合这两种范式的方案。

Method: Loom框架包含三个步骤：（1）模块化启发式规则（诊断模板动态填充特定事件实体、时间和指标）产生开放形式假设；（2）将这些假设投影到连续嵌入空间，用迭代质心重加权算法解决冲突信号；（3）生成的共识权重支撑单次轻量级LLM摘要合成步骤。

Result: 在OpenRCA基准上，Loom在Bank和Market-2数据集上匹配最先进自主智能体，在Market-1和Telecom上略逊色，但每个事件仅需一次LLM调用（约26倍加速；用8B参数合成器可达约33倍加速）。

Conclusion: Loom验证了确定性共识机制在RCA中的有效性：在准确率相近的前提下大幅降低推理成本，同时确定性共识增强了领域专家（SMEs）对系统的信任。论文还分享了部署经验，包括智能体深度与推理延迟的权衡、冗余检测的负面结果等教训。

Abstract: Aggregating noisy, conflicting textual hypotheses into a reliable consensus is a fundamental challenge when deploying NLP systems in real-world industrial settings. While monolithic Large Language Model (LLM) agents offer unbounded expressivity for tasks like Root Cause Analysis (RCA), they suffer from context limits, compounding hallucinations, and prohibitive inference latency. Traditional weak supervision offers statistical rigor but is mathematically restricted to discrete classes. We present Loom, a generative consensus framework deployed for real-world RCA that bridges these paradigms. Loom aggregates open-form hypotheses emitted by modular heuristics (diagnostic templates dynamically populated with episode-specific entities, times, and metrics) by projecting them into a continuous embedding space, and resolves conflicting signals with an iterative centroid-based reweighting algorithm. The resulting consensus weights ground a single lightweight LLM synthesis step. Evaluated on the OpenRCA benchmark, Loom occupies the accuracy--efficiency Pareto frontier: it matches a state-of-the-art autonomous agent on Bank and Market-2 and trails on Market-1 and Telecom, while using a single LLM call per incident on all four datasets ($\sim$26$\times$ faster; $\sim$33$\times$ with an 8B-parameter synthesizer). We discuss our deployment experience, highlighting lessons learned regarding the trade-offs between agentic depth and inference latency, negative results in redundancy detection, and how deterministic consensus fosters trust among Subject Matter Experts~(SMEs).

</details>


### [231] [Door-in-the-Face Requests and Refusal Behaviour in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02707)
*Til Jordan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文测试了"门面技术"（door-in-the-face，即先提出大请求被拒后再提小请求以提高成功率）是否对9个大语言模型有效。结果因模型而异：Anthropic的旗舰模型上有效（Opus 5从29.3%提升至65.8%），但OpenAI和Google的旗舰模型以及Haiku 4.5上适得其反（合规率下降15.5-23.0个百分点）。对照实验表明让步本身在所有模型上都有影响，但对"刚被拒绝"的反应因模型家族而异。从公开基准中提取的拒绝案例不适用该技术，效果取决于请求类型（改写为解释类请求后263例中263例去除了拒绝）。人类影响技巧只能逐模型家族地迁移到语言模型上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究人类的心理说服技巧"门面技术"是否能够迁移到AI语言模型上，并理解不同模型家族对该技巧的反应差异及其背后的机制。

Method: 对来自3个提供商的9个生产模型进行实验：先让模型拒绝一个较大的请求，随后提出同一请求的较小版本，与直接提出小请求的对照组比较合规率。同时设置无关主题的控制实验以分离"让步"和"刚被拒绝"两种效应，并通过改写请求类型验证影响机制。

Result: 门面技术在Anthropic的Opus 5上显著有效（合规率从29.3%升至65.8%），但在OpenAI、Google旗舰模型及Haiku 4.5上会降低合规率15.5-23.0个百分点。控制实验显示让步本身在所有模型上都起作用，但对拒绝的反应因模型家族而异。该技术不适用于公开基准中的拒绝案例，改写请求为解释类请求可消除99.2%的拒绝。

Conclusion: 人类影响技巧向语言模型的迁移不是普遍适用的，而是逐模型家族地呈现差异性。效果取决于模型架构/训练差异以及请求的具体内容类型，未来需要针对不同模型家族分别评估社会工程攻击的有效性。

Abstract: Does the door-in-the-face technique work on language models? In humans, a large request that is refused makes a smaller follow-up request more likely to be granted. We test this on nine production models from three providers: each model refuses a large request, then receives a smaller version of the same request, and we compare its compliance with asking directly. The answer depends on the model. On Anthropic's frontier models the technique works: Opus 5 answers the smaller request 65.8% of the time after refusing the larger one, against 29.3% when asked directly. On the frontier models of OpenAI and Google, and on Haiku 4.5, it backfires, lowering compliance by 15.5 to 23.0 points. A control locates the effect: a refused large request on an unrelated topic does less than the related one on all nine models, so the concession itself matters everywhere, while the reaction to having just refused something differs by model family. The technique does not transfer to refusals drawn from public benchmarks. What decides whether a retreat can work is what the request asks for: rewriting 265 refused requests for usable instructions into requests for explanations of the same topic removed the refusal in 263 cases. Human influence techniques port to language models one model family at a time.

</details>


### [232] [Repo-To-Skill: Distilling GitHub Repositories Into AI4AI Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02749)
*Jianlyu Chen,Yuyang Hu,Hongjin Qian,Jiawei Liu,Wenqing Wei,Xiaolong Chen,Defu Lian,Zhicheng Dou,Chaozhuo Li,Qiwei Ye,Zheng Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DisCo 是一个基于技能的科研智能体，通过任务无关和任务导向两种知识蒸馏形式，将开源 ML 仓库和任务特定知识转化为可复用、可验证的技能，在多个基准测试上显著提升了科研性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主智能体虽然在端到端执行 ML 研究中取得进展，但其架构缺少领域特定的操作知识（operational knowledge），即从"知道方法"到"让方法起作用"之间所需的实践经验。这类知识分散在论文和代码仓库中，以人类可读且体积庞大的形式存在，无法在任务执行期间实时加载。作者认为需要将其蒸馏为紧凑、可验证的技能以便跨任务复用。

Method: 提出 DisCo 框架，包含两种互补的知识蒸馏形式：(1) 任务无关蒸馏——对开源生态中广泛使用的 ML 仓库进行浓缩，生成可复用技能；(2) 任务导向蒸馏——针对具体任务需求生成所需技能。任务无关蒸馏产出了包含 5,000+ 已验证技能的 AREX-Skill Library，覆盖 20 个领域和 178 个能力族。

Result: 在固定模型骨干（GPT-5.5）、研究框架和下游执行预算的条件下，配备技能的科研智能体相对于无技能版本在 MLE-bench 上提升 134.3%，在 PaperBench 上提升 34.4%，在 FrontierCS 上提升 9.2%，在 PassNet 上提升 14.0%。

Conclusion: DisCo 证明了将领域操作知识蒸馏为可复用技能，是提升自主 ML 研究智能体性能的关键途径。这些性能增益完全源于在固定设置下添加蒸馏后的操作上下文，而非改进模型或框架本身。该工作指出了技能库作为智能体必备知识层的价值。

Abstract: Autonomous agents are beginning to carry out machine-learning (ML) research end to end. These agents combine a model backbone with a harness for planning, execution, memory, and verification, but this architecture still leaves domain-specific know-how outside the agent. We call this missing layer operational knowledge, the know-how that separates knowing a method from making it work. That knowledge is not absent from the field. It appears in repositories and papers, but in forms written for human readers and too large to load during a task. Once distilled into compact, verified skills, this knowledge can be reused across tasks rather than rediscovered during each run.
  We present DisCo, a skill-powered research agent that creates skills and uses them during research. Its distillation runs in two complementary forms: task-agnostic, condensing the field's widely used repositories into reusable skills, and task-oriented, producing the skills a concrete task calls for. The former, applied across the open ecosystem, yields the AREX-Skill Library, with 5,000+ verified skills distilled from 1,000 widely used ML repositories and organized into 20 areas and 178 capability families. With the GPT-5.5 backbone, research harness, and downstream execution budget held fixed, the skill-equipped research agent scores 134.3% higher on MLE-bench, 34.4% higher on PaperBench, 9.2% higher on FrontierCS, and 14.0% higher on PassNet than the same agent without skills. These gains come from adding distilled operating context under that fixed setup.

</details>


### [233] [Bilevel Coordinated Reflection: A Game-Theoretic Approach to Multi-Agent LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02750)
*Yihang Chen,Yuxiang Chen,Yuxuan Huang,Meng Fang,Weilin Luo,Jun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的理论框架来分析多智能体LLM系统中的协调、反思与外部验证问题：将orchestrator-worker交互建模为双层协调博弈，将自由文本反思建模为语义记忆上的随机运动，并证明了信息论层面的"门控"不可能性结果，进而提出了具有环境接地评估的随机反思记忆上升算法（SRMA）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统（如orchestrator-workers模式）虽有强实证结果，但缺乏统一的数学框架来解释协调机制、记忆改进以及外部验证的作用，亟需理论层面的系统性分析。

Method: 通过三类工具构建理论：1）双层协调博弈建模（在bounded coupling下证明workers局部更新博弈为近似势博弈）；2）将反思建模为语义记忆状态上的随机运动并推导有限时间上下界；3）信息论门控分析（证明仅观察转录的gate无法在文本不可区分环境中统一改进，而环境接地的gate可行），据此设计SRMA算法并分析其收敛率。

Result: 推导出自由反思的有限时间上界并证明最坏情况紧性；证明SRMA在标定和非退化修正质量下设几何或多项式精确收敛且阶紧；实验上在500个SWE-bench实例中，完整Kimi系统达到72.2%的解决率（对照公开mini-SWE-agent为70.8%）。

Conclusion: 该工作为多智能体LLM系统的协调动力学、反思记忆更新和环境接地验证提供了统一的理论基础，其不可能性结果揭示仅依靠内部文本反思存在固有局限，环境接地的评估门控是系统持续改进的必要条件。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems commonly use an orchestrator to decompose a task for a team of workers and then improve through textual reflection. Despite strong empirical results, these systems lack a unified account of coordination, memory improvement, and the role of external verification. We model orchestrator-worker interaction as a bilevel coordination game: under bounded coupling, the workers' local-update game is an approximate potential game whose equilibrium slack is controlled by decomposition quality. We then analyse reflection as stochastic movement over semantic memory states. For free-form reflection, we derive a finite-time upper bound, prove worst-case tightness, and give a positive lower bound under a falsifiable persistent-harm condition. We further prove an information-theoretic impossibility result: no gate that observes only the generated transcript can improve uniformly over text-indistinguishable environments, whereas an environment-grounded gate can. Motivated by this separation, we introduce Stochastic Reflective Memory Ascent (SRMA), which accepts a candidate memory only after a grounded evaluation risk strictly decreases. Under calibration and non-degenerate corrective mass, SRMA converges exactly, geometrically or polynomially; matching constructions show that both rate regimes are order-tight. We also provide confidence gating for stochastic evaluation and re-anchoring guarantees for piecewise-stationary environments. Experiments instantiate these objects with environment-grounded metrics and test the predicted coordination and drift laws. On 500 SWE-bench instances, the complete Kimi-based system resolves 72.2% versus a 70.8% public mini-SWE-agent reference. Code: https://github.com/YihangChen9/Bilevel-Coordinated-Reflection

</details>


### [234] [Measurement-Driven Sub-Network Selection for On-Premise Retrieval-Augmented Factory Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02760)
*Vasileios Rizeakos,Georgios Paisios,Alexandros Machairas,Michael Birbas,Athanasios Bachoumis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了部署在工厂车间硬件上的对话式助手模型，通过结构压缩和检索增强适配，证明压缩后模型大小不再是判断答案质量的可靠预测指标，并提出基于子网络选择和权重共享超网络的高效部署方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工厂车间需要本地（on-premise）助手为工人提供设备文档的对话式访问，但具备相应能力的模型往往无法适配车间硬件资源限制（如内存、功耗）。现有方法通常仅优化单一指标，无法兼顾能力与部署约束。

Method: 采用结构压缩与检索增强适配相结合的流程，提出将部署问题视为后适配选择问题：为每台设备选择合适的子网络，基于判断质量与实测吞吐量进行优化，并可设定通用能力下限与内存预算；通过带三明治式就地蒸馏的权重共享超网络降低选择成本。

Result: 在制造手册案例研究中，压缩后模型判断质量损失为未剪枝模型的13.7%，通过检索增强蒸馏恢复到4.6%以内，挽回三分之二的损失；同一助手可在三个异构边缘设备层运行，待机功耗仅1.3至5瓦。

Conclusion: 模型大小不再能可靠预测压缩模型的检索增强答案质量，仅优化大小、速度或质量中任一单一规则都会牺牲能力或吞吐量，因此按设备进行子网络选择的部署策略能更好平衡多目标约束。

Abstract: On-premise assistants can give factory workers conversational access to machine documentation, but models capable of the task rarely fit shop-floor hardware. We show that after structural compression and retrieval-grounded adaptation, model size is no longer a reliable predictor of adapted answer quality: general capability falls almost linearly with parameter count, while judged retrieval-augmented answer quality does not. We therefore treat deployment as a post-adaptation selection problem, committing one sub-network per device on judged answer quality and measured on-device throughput under a configurable general-capability floor and memory budget; rules that optimize size, speed, or quality alone each give up capability or throughput. A weight-shared supernetwork trained with sandwich-style in-place distillation keeps this selection inexpensive. In a manufacturing-manual case study, extraction costs 13.7 percent of the unpruned model's judged quality and retrieval-grounded distillation returns it to within 4.6 percent, recovering two thirds of the loss, and the same assistant runs across three heterogeneous edge tiers at 1.3 to 5 watts standby.

</details>


### [235] [Large Language Models (LLMs) for Telecom Root Cause Analysis (RCA): A Structured Reasoning Framework for Evidence-Grounded Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02805)
*Hao Zhou,Mandar Kulkarni,Hao Chen,Yan Xin,Charlie,Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向LLM的电信网络根因分析结构化推理框架，通过将异构网络遥测组织为标准上下文、强制决策路径推理并生成基于证据的解释，在TeleLogs和TelecomTS两个5G数据集上显著提升了诊断准确率和决策一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 5G和6G网络中跨层依赖复杂，传统RCA方法难以应对；直接使用vanilla LLM进行电信RCA会产生幻觉、推理不稳定以及与结构化网络证据对齐不佳的问题，亟需设计对齐电信领域知识的结构化推理框架。

Method: 首先回顾电信RCA从规则/ML方法到LLM技术的演进，概述结构化推理、检索增强知识锚定、智能体编排和可验证推理等新兴范式；在此基础上提出结构化推理框架，具体包括三个步骤：(1) 将异构网络遥测组织为规范上下文；(2) 在诊断过程中执行决策路径推理；(3) 生成基于证据的解释以支持可靠的故障识别。

Result: 在两个5G RCA数据集（TeleLogs和TelecomTS）上的实验表明，所提框架相比基线方法在诊断准确性和决策一致性两方面均取得了一致的提升。

Conclusion: 跨数据集结果强调了结构化推理设计对于下一代电信网络中实用LLM-based RCA系统的重要性，验证了将诊断推理与电信特定证据和领域知识对齐的有效性。

Abstract: Root cause analysis (RCA) is a critical task in telecom network operations, but diagnosing performance degradations in modern 5G and emerging 6G networks remains challenging due to complex cross-layer dependencies. While large language models (LLMs) offer promising capabilities for reasoning and knowledge integration, directly applying vanilla LLMs to telecom RCA often leads to hallucination, unstable reasoning, and poor alignment with structured network evidence. This work first reviews the evolution of telecom RCA from rule-based and machine learning (ML) approaches to emerging LLM-enabled techniques, and provides an overview of recent paradigms, including structured reasoning, retrieval-augmented knowledge grounding, agentic orchestration, and verifiable reasoning. Building upon these insights, we propose a structured reasoning framework for LLM-enabled telecom RCA that aligns diagnostic reasoning with telecom-specific evidence and domain knowledge. The proposed approach first organizes heterogeneous network telemetry into canonical contexts, and then enforces decision-path reasoning during diagnosis, and finally generates evidence-grounded explanations for reliable fault identification. Experimental results on two 5G RCA datasets, TeleLogs and TelecomTS, demonstrate that the proposed framework consistently improves diagnostic accuracy and decision consistency compared with baseline techniques. These cross-dataset results highlight the importance of structured reasoning design for practical LLM-based RCA systems in next-generation telecom networks.

</details>


### [236] [AI Contextual Measurement for Recovering Individual and Group-Level Effects: Validation Against Survey Measures and an Occupational Application](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02821)
*Wenxin Jiang,Xuyang Wang,Yuxiao Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AICOME（AI情境测量）框架，用于评估AI生成的受访者级测量能否在情境模型中恢复个体和群体层面的效应，并通过2022年中国家庭追踪调查（CFPS）数据验证该框架的有效性及边界条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者越来越多地使用AI来构建现有调查中缺失的社会、组织和职业特征测量，但缺乏一个系统的框架来评估这些AI测量是否能在情境模型中准确恢复个体和群体层面的效应，而非仅仅作为响应预测工具。

Method: 提出AICOME框架，核心思路是将AI在受访者层面构建的测量用于推导群体层面聚合值和个体偏差值，从而同时估计组间和组内关联。使用2022年CFPS数据进行验证，以职业作为经验分组结构，并选取计算机使用、外语使用、每周工作时长和管理职责四个变量作为验证基准，进行响应级、模型级、情境和边界条件四个层次的验证。

Result: AI情境测量在丰富的受访者和职业特征条件下能够恢复调查变量中包含的大部分情境模型信息。每周工作时长是最强的验证案例，AI测量能够复现CFPS中观察到的工作时长与满意度之间的负向组间和组内关联。框架也明确了边界条件：当信息仅限于职业和基本人口统计特征时性能下降，当多个相关概念同时视为未观测时恢复效果较弱。

Conclusion: AICOME框架最适合用于从丰富的现有数据集中恢复少量理论重要构念，研究者应关注AI测量在情境建模中的价值而非单纯将其视为响应预测问题。

Abstract: Researchers increasingly use artificial intelligence to construct measures of social, organizational, and occupational characteristics that are absent from conventional surveys. We propose AICOME, AI COntextual MEasurement, a framework for evaluating whether AI-derived respondent-level measures can recover individual and group-level effects in contextual models. The key idea is that an AI measure constructed at the respondent level can be used to derive its group-level aggregate and its individual deviation, allowing researchers to estimate both between-group and within-group associations rather than treating AI measurement as response prediction alone.
  We validate the framework using the 2022 China Family Panel Studies (CFPS), where occupations provide the empirical grouping structure and several job-related survey variables provide validation benchmarks. For computer use, foreign-language use, weekly hours, and management responsibilities, we compare survey measures with AI-derived measures in response-level, model-level, contextual, and boundary-condition validations. The results show that AI contextual measurement can recover much of the contextual-model information contained in observed survey variables when rich respondent and job characteristics are available. Weekly hours provides the strongest validation case, with AI-derived measures reproducing the large negative between- and within-occupation associations with satisfaction observed in CFPS. The framework also identifies clear boundary conditions: performance deteriorates when information is restricted to occupation and basic demographics, and recovery is weaker when several related concepts are treated as simultaneously unobserved. The findings suggest that AICOME is most useful for recovering a limited number of theoretically important constructs from rich existing datasets.

</details>


### [237] [Discriminative World Models for Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02885)
*Kelvin Li,Dhruv Pendharkar,Anish Pahilajani,Chuyi Shang,Leon Oks,Leonid Karlinsky,Rogerio Feris,Trevor Darrell,Roei Herzig*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"预测状态匹配"（predicted-state matching）训练目标来训练网络智能体的世界模型，使预测的网页状态能够区分真实结果状态与备选动作到达的状态，从而解决监督式下一状态预测与下游排序器目标不一致的问题，在多个基准上取得改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网络智能体使用世界模型进行测试时动作选择时，通常通过监督式下一状态预测来训练生成HTML或AXTree等固定表示。但这种训练目标与下游排序器不匹配——排序器依赖预测状态在候选动作之间具有区分度，而监督式预测只追求单步状态准确性，忽略了状态表示对候选动作的判别能力。

Method: 提出预测状态匹配训练目标，要求预测的表示不仅能正确预测真实结果状态，还必须区别于备选动作所到达的状态。利用从WebArena Go-Browse轨迹派生的分支网络智能体数据集进行训练，其中每个决策点包含多个备选动作及其对应的结果状态。

Result: 在预测状态匹配基准上优于使用监督式下一状态预测训练的世界模型；在WebPRMBench上改进PRM风格的动作排序，优于仅用动作的PRM和监督式下一状态世界模型增强的PRM；在WebArena-Lite上使用该方法的世界模型进行测试时动作选择能提升端到端任务成功率。

Conclusion: 预测状态匹配作为训练目标能有效对齐世界模型的预测表示与下游排序器的需求，从而显著提升网络智能体动作选择的质量和端到端任务性能。

Abstract: Recent web agents use world models for test-time action selection by sampling candidate actions, predicting the resulting web states, and ranking them with a ranker model or a Process Reward Model (PRM). These world models are typically trained via supervised next-state prediction to generate fixed representations like HTML or AXTree snapshots. However, this objective is misaligned with the downstream ranker, which relies on predicted states being discriminative across candidates to accurately score them. To address this, we introduce predicted-state matching, a training objective where the predicted representation must distinguish the true resulting state from those reached by alternative actions. We train these models using a branching web-agent dataset derived from WebArena Go-Browse trajectories, where every decision point contains multiple alternative actions and their resulting states. Experiments on our held-out predicted-state matching benchmark show that our approach outperforms world models trained with supervised next-state prediction. We further show that our approach improves PRM-style action ranking on WebPRMBench compared with action-only PRMs and PRMs augmented with supervised-next-state world models. Finally, on WebArena-Lite, using our world model for test-time action selection improves end-to-end task success. Our project page is available at: https://dhruvpendharkar.github.io/dwm/.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [238] [Context Inference Attacks Without Jailbreaks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01663)
*Prince Jha,Samuele Poppi,Nils Lukas*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了agentic AI系统在推理时处理敏感数据（如医疗记录、财务文档）时存在的隐私泄露风险。作者提出了"上下文推理攻击"（context-inference attacks）这一新攻击范式，与传统的越狱攻击不同，该攻击通过agent自身工具调用组装上下文时探测隐藏信息。实验表明，无论采用指令约束、logit抑制还是上下文稀释等防御措施，agent仍存在可利用的泄露信号。攻击在三种场景（已知上下文、未知上下文、agent自身检索获取上下文）中均保持有效，展现了较高的攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐私风险研究主要聚焦于越狱攻击（jailbreaking），即诱导模型直接泄露敏感内容，但忽略了agentic场景下由agent自身工具调用组装上下文的隐私风险。随着agentic AI系统被广泛应用于处理敏感数据的推理场景，需要系统性地研究这一被忽视的隐私攻击面。

Method: 作者形式化定义了"上下文推理攻击"，通过安全博弈（security game）框架建模。评估了三种攻击者知识递减、上下文传递日益间接的场景：已知上下文、未知上下文、以及agent通过自身工具调用检索到的上下文。区分了灰盒设置（用目标模型评分观测）和黑盒设置（用攻击者控制的替代模型评分）。测试了多种防御措施：指令禁止泄露、logit抑制和上下文稀释。

Result: 单个攻击无需修改即可贯穿三种场景。在已知上下文中，小候选集上达到100% ASR，1024个候选时达到63%；模板和周围记录未知时为78.9 AUROC；14B替代模型评分32B目标模型时为92.5 AUROC；记录通过agent检索返回时为81.8 AUROC（对照基线分别为1/|Z|和50的随机水平）。

Conclusion: 即便施加了指令约束、logit抑制和上下文稀释等多种防御措施，agentic AI系统仍然存在严重的隐藏上下文泄露漏洞。网络浏览型agent在回答良性用户查询时，仍会携带关于静默加载到其上下文中记录的可利用信号，表明当前的防御机制不足以应对agentic场景下的上下文推理攻击。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems are increasingly deployed to process sensitive data at inference time, such as healthcare records or financial documents assembled into a hidden \emph{context} before the system answers. Prior work has studied privacy risks primarily through \emph{jailbreaking} attacks that induce models to directly disclose sensitive content, but has largely overlooked the agentic setting where the context is assembled by the agent's own tool calls. We show that the agents we evaluate remain vulnerable to hidden-context leakage despite the controls we test against them, namely an instruction not to disclose the context, logit suppression, and context dilution. For instance, a web-browsing agent answering benign user queries still carries exploitable signals about records silently loaded into its context. We introduce and formalize \emph{context-inference attacks} through a security game and evaluate three settings under decreasing attacker knowledge and increasingly indirect delivery of the context: a known context, an unknown context, and a context the agent retrieves through its own tool calls. We distinguish a grey-box setting, in which the target model is used to score observations, from black-box settings in which the attacker scores with a surrogate it controls. We further characterize how leakage varies with query budget, context size, and target-model size. A single attack carries through all three settings without modification, reaching $100\%$ ASR on small candidate sets and $63\%$ at $1024$ candidates against a known context, $78.9$ AUROC when the template and surrounding records are unknown, $92.5$ AUROC when a 14B surrogate scores a 32B target, and $81.8$ AUROC when the records arrive as an agent's retrieval returns, against chance rates of $1/|\mathcal{Z}|$ and $50$ respectively.

</details>


### [239] [Private Computation Space: Experience with Trusted Multi-Cluster Federated Learning for Agriculture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01667)
*Shuangyu Lei,Muhammad Salman Abid,Jacob Belding,Sam Mosher,Manushi B. Trivedi,Shivranjani Baruah,Liam Wickes-Do,Andrew Anderson,Braulio Dumba,Alyssa Whitcraft,Ritvik Sahajpal,Sijin Li,Kelly Robbins,Michael Gore,Margaret Frank,Steven Wolf,Liz Jones,Abraham Stroock,Kaitlin Gold,Hakim Weatherspoon*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个用于农业场景的私有计算空间（PCS）系统，它结合联邦学习、差分隐私和可信执行环境，以解决农民数据隐私顾虑，并在实际部署的两个农业工作负载中验证了其有效性与隐私保护能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管AI能改善农业实践，但69%的美国农民对数据共享存在隐私顾虑，这限制了AI在农业中的广泛采用。需要构建一个既能保护农民数据和身份、又不损害模型效用的系统，同时还需在商品化硬件上运行并适应脆弱的农村基础设施。

Method: 提出了私有计算空间（PCS）系统，这是一个开源机器学习系统。系统采用多集群编排保障农村地区的可靠性，结合异步联邦学习、差分隐私（DP）和可信执行环境（TEEs），使农场能够在保持数据隐私的同时参与模型训练框架。

Result: 系统在两个实际部署的工作负载上进行了评估：纽约州六个月的活体植物传感器氮监测（DSC达0.71）和加州十个月的天气站蒸散量预测（R²精度达0.84）。与最差单站点模型相比，精度分别提升了22.4%和9.1%，同时保证了隐私。

Conclusion: PCS系统成功在真实农业场景中实现了隐私保护的机器学习部署，在模型效用和隐私保障之间取得了良好平衡，证明了该框架在解决农业数据隐私问题上的可行性和有效性。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence has shown to help improve agricultural practices, yet adoption remains limited: 69% of U.S. farmers have privacy concerns with sharing their data, and these concerns must be addressed before adoption is widespread. While Federated Learning has been demonstrated to protect privacy at scale for other sectors, deploying a system for agriculture comes with its own set of challenges; the problem necessitates a system that can protect farmer data and identities while preserving model utility, runs on commodity hardware, and is resilient to fragile rural infrastructure. To address these concerns, we introduce the Private Computation Space (PCS), a deployed, open-source Machine Learning system to provision and process farmer data securely. We design a system tailored to an agricultural setting, with multi-cluster orchestration for reliability in rural areas with asynchronous Federated Learning (FL), Differential Privacy (DP), and Trusted Execution Environments (TEEs), to allow farms to participate in the framework while keeping their data private. We evaluate the system on two deployed workloads: monitoring nitrogen with living plant sensors in NY for six months and predicting evapotranspiration from weather stations in CA for ten months. Our evaluation finds a Dice Similarity Coefficient (DSC) of 0.71 and $R^2$ accuracy of 0.84 for the respective workloads, improving the worst single-site model accuracy by 22.4% and 9.1%, respectively, while preserving privacy.

</details>


### [240] [Ranked by the Matcher: A Reproducibility Audit of Knowledge Graph Extraction from Threat Reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01671)
*Safayat Bin Hakim,Houbing Herbert Song*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了威胁情报知识图谱抽取工具评估中的一个关键缺陷：基于triple-F1分数的系统排名高度依赖于三元组匹配规则的选择。作者对12个系统中仅5个能复现其匹配规则，在不同匹配协议下重打分导致45对排序中11对反转，单个预测集的F1分数范围横跨0.16到0.70。同时构建了CTIForge框架，通过保持抽取内容字节一致、仅变化确定性验证层来分离组件效应，发现验证层对在线后端提升精度、对离线后端降低精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全团队和研究人员依据发表的triple-F1分数选择威胁报告的知识图谱抽取工具，但这些分数高度依赖预测三元组与金标准标注的匹配规则，而大多数系统的匹配规则无法复现，导致评估结果可能误导工具选型决策。

Method: 作者（1）检查12个系统的匹配规则可复现性，仅5个可重新实现；（2）在共享文档上以8种匹配协议对10个系统输出重新打分；（3）在GRID的378项外部校准集上比较机械匹配器（词法、嵌入、蕴含）与多评审者裁决及LLM评判的一致性；（4）构建CTIForge，允许在抽取内容字节一致的前提下仅变化确定性验证层，测试7种部署配置。

Result: 匹配协议变化导致45对排序中11对反转；固定预测集的F1在0.16-0.70间波动。机械匹配器与人工裁决一致性不超过71%，LLM评判达86%。CTIForge实验中，验证层提升全部4个在线后端精度但降低全部3个离线后端精度，同时争论实体类型的操作增加了约2.8倍。作者指出该差异为描述性而非因果性的服务效应，与手写规则编码提取器自身约定有关。

Conclusion: 现有知识图谱抽取工具的比较评估因匹配规则不透明和一致性低而不可靠，需要统一的、可复现的评估协议；验证层的效果与后端类型相关，发布管道、协议套件和逐三元组审计记录以支持可复现的评估和改进。

Abstract: Security teams and researchers choose knowledge-graph extraction tooling for threat reports on the strength of published triple-F1 scores, yet those scores depend on how predicted triples are matched to gold annotations. We could reimplement the stated matching rule for only five of twelve inspected systems. Re-scoring ten system outputs on shared documents under eight protocols reverses eleven of forty-five pairwise orderings; one fixed prediction set spans 0.16-0.70 F1. On GRID's external 378-item calibration set, no mechanical matcher (lexical, embedding, or entailment) agrees with multi-reviewer adjudication above 71%, whereas an LLM judge reaches 86%. To separate component effects from matcher rewards, we build CTIForge, whose deterministic validation layer can vary while extraction is held byte-identical. Across seven tested deployment configurations, validation raises precision for all four hosted backbones and lowers it for all three offline backbones. Because backbone, decoding, and backend-specific prompting covary, this is a descriptive split rather than an isolated serving effect. It coincides with a roughly 2.8-fold increase in actions explicitly disputing entity type, consistent with hand-written rules encoding the conventions of the extractor against which they were developed. We release the pipeline, protocol suite, and per-triple audit records.

</details>


### [241] [Skill-as-API: Confidential Multi-Agent Coordination for Agentic Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01677)
*Ziwei Zhao,Yu Gu,Haojun Liang,Chen Zhang,Xizhi Ding*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Skill-as-API协议，一种将AI编码智能体技能封装为API的协调协议，限制公开视图仅为技能的名称、描述、输入输出模式和信任层级，技能主体通过闭包捕获保留在所有者进程中，永不跨网络传输。通过四层架构结构性地缩小提示注入攻击面，并提供基于XMTP的开源代码实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI编码智能体从独立工具演变为协作同伴，但在协调过程中会泄露技能的知识产权。MCP和A2A等协议虽然实现于服务端，仍会将技能描述和类型模式公开给所有对等节点，无法隐藏技能存在性，也不能保证包装的系统提示词不会离开网络。应用层隐私过滤器只是在模型决定输出敏感文本之后才起作用，无法从根本上解决问题。

Method: Skill-as-API采用互补的协议层路线：将技能公开视图限制为名称、描述、类型化输入/输出模式和信任层级；技能主体通过闭包捕获保留在所有者进程中且永不跨线传输。四层架构增加访问控制并结构性缩小提示注入攻击面，而非通过内容过滤来实现安全。

Result: 提供了基于XMTP的开源Python实现，跨大洲热重连延迟为1.8-2.9秒；在软件工程案例研究中，三个智能体协调完成拉取请求审查，同时每个智能体保留了其专有分析提示词的所有权。

Conclusion: Skill-as-API通过协议层的设计从根本上解决智能体协作中的知识产权泄露问题，在不牺牲协作效率的前提下实现专有技能的安全共享，为AI智能体安全协调提供了新的范式。

Abstract: AI coding agents are evolving from solitary tools into collaborative teammates that discover and invoke one another's specialized skills. But the coordination channel itself can leak a skill's intellectual property. Protocols such as MCP and A2A run implementations server-side, yet they still publish each skill's description and typed schemas to every peer, offer no way to hide a skill's existence, and cannot guarantee that a wrapped system prompt stays off the wire. Application-layer privacy filters help, but act only after the model has decided to emit sensitive text. We take a complementary, protocol-layer route: Skill-as-API, a coordination protocol whose public view of a skill is limited to its name, description, typed input/output schemas, and trust tier.
