### [273] [PRISM: An Agentic Multi-Model Architecture for Proactive Safety in Autonomous Transportation Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01623)
*Joyjit Roy,Samaresh Kumar Singh,Sushanta Das*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出PRISM，一种基于智能体的多模型安全架构，将传统ADAS从反应式碰撞规避转变为主动式持续风险管理。该系统通过逆碰撞概率建模将二分类碰撞检测转换为动态可解释的安全评分，结合三个专业模型（轨迹运动学、环境风险、VRU交互）并由包含强化学习、上下文记忆和特征级归因的推理层协调，提供四层分级安全干预机制。在1,296个自然驾驶场景中验证（无需数据集特定重训练），平均安全得分68/100，77.6%场景被分类为咨询级别，3.8%近碰率，11%场景升级至干预或紧急响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ADAS主要采用反应式机制，仅在危险出现后才触发，而美国VRU（行人、骑行者）伤亡率持续上升，暴露了当前安全系统的关键局限性。亟需从反应式碰撞规避向主动式、持续化的风险管理范式转变。

Method: PRISM采用agentic多模型安全架构：1) 逆碰撞概率建模将二分类碰撞分类器转换为动态可解释安全分数；2) 三个并行专业模型分别处理轨迹运动学、环境风险和VRU交互；3) 推理层集成强化学习、上下文记忆和特征级归因进行协调；4) 提供四层分级安全干预（从静默监控到紧急告警），并动态调整安全参数而非静态阈值。

Result: 在三个自然驾驶数据集的1,296个场景中验证，无数据集特定重训练情况下：平均安全得分68/100，77.6%场景分类为咨询级，近碰率3.8%，11%场景升级至干预或应急响应。特征归因一致识别出轨迹风险和VRU邻近度是首要安全因素。

Conclusion: PRISM提供了一个统一、可解释的主动式交通安全框架，强调在密集城区中降低VRU风险，证明无需特定数据集重训练即可实现跨场景泛化的动态安全风险管理能力。

Abstract: Autonomous and intelligent transportation systems operate in complex urban environments where safety depends on interactions among vehicle behavior, environmental conditions, and vulnerable road users (VRUs) such as pedestrians and cyclists. Most advanced driver assistance systems (ADAS) employ reactive mechanisms that activate only after hazards have emerged, a critical limitation underscored by rising VRU fatalities in the United States.
  This study introduces PRISM (Proactive Risk Intelligence and Safety Management), an agentic multi-model safety architecture that transitions from reactive crash avoidance to proactive, continuous risk management. PRISM employs inverse crash-probability modeling to convert binary crash classifiers into dynamic, interpretable safety scores. Three specialized models addressing trajectory kinematics, environmental risk, and VRU interaction operate concurrently, coordinated by a reasoning layer incorporating reinforcement learning, contextual memory, and feature-level attribution. The system provides graduated safety interventions across four tiers, from silent monitoring to emergency alerts.
  Unlike rule-based systems with static thresholds, PRISM dynamically adjusts safety parameters in real time. Validated across 1,296 scenarios from three naturalistic driving datasets without dataset-specific retraining, the system yielded a mean safety score of 68 out of 100, classified 77.6% of scenarios as advisory, and flagged a near-miss rate of 3.8%, with 11% of scenarios escalating to intervention or emergency response. Feature attribution consistently identified trajectory risk and VRU proximity as primary safety factors. PRISM provides a unified, interpretable framework for proactive transportation safety with emphasis on VRU risk reduction in dense urban environments.

</details>


### [274] [Differential Games for Compositional Handling of Competing Control Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01838)
*Joshua Shay Kricheli*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于微分博弈的分而治之控制设计方法，为多目标单智能体动态系统建立非合作微分博弈框架，通过纳什均衡合成复合控制器，在倒立摆和四旋翼基准测试中优于经典LQR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模单智能体系统中，多个动态冲突的控制目标使得全局权重优化难以处理，传统方法（如LQR）在处理多目标权衡时缺乏直观性和模块化，需要一种新的控制设计方法论来解决这一问题。

Method: 将每个控制目标关联一个虚拟输入，在代表玩家之间建立非合作有限/无限时域微分博弈；每个玩家针对自身目标优化独立的虚拟代价函数，同时考虑其他玩家的最优策略；通过求解纳什均衡合成复合控制器；针对连续时间和离散时间动态系统提供形式化数学推导；开发了开源Python包用于求解无限时域博弈中的耦合代数Riccati方程。

Result: 在倒立摆-小车系统和非线性分层控制的四旋翼两个基准案例上进行评估，与经典LQR在瞬态和稳态控制指标上进行比较，显示出更优的轨迹跟踪性能和鲁棒的多目标调节能力。

Conclusion: 该方法为控制工程师提供了一种直观且模块化的参数重新调整框架，能够在竞争性控制目标之间实现稳定平衡，在多目标控制方面优于传统LQR方法。

Abstract: We introduce a novel Divide and Conquer control design methodology leveraging differential games in single-agent, multi-objective dynamical systems. The proposed framework associates each control objective with a virtual input and establishes a non-cooperative, finite or infinite horizon differential game among representative players. Each player optimizes a distinct virtual cost function tailored to its specific goal, the full system state, and the other virtual inputs, while accounting for the remaining players' optimal policies. By establishing a Nash Equilibrium for this game, we synthesize a composite controller that achieves a stable balance across competing objectives, providing control engineers with an intuitive and modular framework for parameter re-tuning throughout the design cycle. We provide formal mathematical derivations for both continuous-time and discrete-time dynamical systems, targeting large-scale single-agent applications where complex, dynamically conflicting control objectives make global weighting intractable. To demonstrate the methodology, we developed an open-source Python package implementing a novel numerical algorithm for solving Coupled Algebraic Riccati Equations arising in infinite-horizon differential games. We evaluate the approach on two benchmark case studies: an inverted pendulum on a cart and a non-linear hierarchically controlled quadrotor. The resulting closed-loop performance is compared against the classical Linear Quadratic Regulator (LQR) across various transient and steady-state control metrics, demonstrating superior trajectory tracking and robust multi-objective regulation.

</details>


### [275] [ArcticSwarm: Deferring Early Consensus in Long-Horizon Multi-Agent Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01870)
*Soyoung Yoon,Boyi Liu,Yite Wang,Ruofan Wu,Canwen Xu,Nikki Lijing Kuang,Seung-won Hwang,Yuxiong He,Zhewei Yao*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出ArcticSwarm，一种分离"证据收集"与"证据整合"的多智能体研究架构。子智能体将发现发布到共享公告板，通过门控隔离防止过早共识，并在三个承诺边界实施结构化审查，显著提升了长时程多智能体深度研究的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多智能体系统在编程等有可靠验证器的领域表现优异，但在缺乏验证器的开放式、长时程研究任务中难以泛化。多数投票或自一致性作为代理验证器时，并行智能体会重复探索相同证据，且过早接触同伴的部分发现会导致搜索过早收敛到早期候选方案，限制了备选方案的空间探索。

Method: ArcticSwarm将研究过程分离为两个阶段：子智能体将发现发布到共享公告板（证据收集），同时采用门控隔离机制让选定的搜索任务保留各自的先验知识，防止早期共识。在三个承诺边界（commitment boundaries）处实施结构化审查，确保只有高置信度的候选方案才被传播到后续阶段。

Result: ArcticSwarm在完整BrowseComp-Plus数据集上使用Qwen 3.5-27B模型达到82.6%的准确率，相比无门控隔离的78.8%和无结构化审查的74.5%均有提升，超过MiroFlow基线（70.6%）。在实时网页BrowseComp上使用GPT-5达到73.6%，远超提供商系统（54.9%）和MiroFlow（63.4%）。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，在证据收集阶段限制同伴阅读、并在假设共享前加强承诺边界，能够拓宽搜索空间并改善长时程多智能体深度研究的性能。这一发现为缺乏可靠验证器的开放式研究任务中的多智能体协作提供了新的设计原则。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems have shown strong performance in domains with reliable verifiers such as coding, where multi-parallel candidate generation selected by a verifier is effective. However, such pipelines would not generalize to open-ended, long-horizon research tasks without a verifier. While majority voting or self-consistency is often used to reach consensus as a proxy verifier, parallel agents repeatedly explore the same evidence, while access to peers' partial findings cause search to converge on an early candidate before alternatives are tested. We present ArcticSwarm, a multi-agent research architecture that separates evidence gathering from evidence integration. Subagents publish findings to a shared bulletin board, while gated isolation lets selected search tasks maintain their own prior, preventing early consensus. Structured review at three commitment boundaries enforce only confident candidates to be propagated. As a result, ArcticSwarm reaches 82.6% on the full BrowseComp-Plus set with the open-weight Qwen 3.5-27B model, compared with 78.8% without gated isolation and 74.5% additionally with structured review disabled, outperforming aligned baseline MiroFlow runs (70.6%). Extending to live-web BrowseComp, ArcticSwarm reaches 73.6% with GPT-5, which is well above the reported provider system (54.9%) and MiroFlow (63.4%). Overall, the results show that restricting peer reads during evidence gathering and strengthening commitment boundaries before a hypothesis is shared can broaden search and improve long-horizon multi-agent deep research.

</details>


### [276] [RideSkill: A Hierarchical Algorithm for Generalized Ride Sharing with LLM-Driven Automatic Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02250)
*Zijian Zhao,Sen Li,Xialiang Tong,Mingxuan Yuan*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出RideSkill，一种利用LLM辅助自动算法设计的层次化拼车调度方法，通过技能库、组合器和重定位器三个组件解决拼车系统中多智能体强化学习面临泛化性差、迁移性低和扩展性差等问题，且训练完成后无需LLM在线调用即可实现高效实时部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MARL方法在拼车问题中面临泛化性差（难以适应不同场景）、迁移性低（难以适应不同平台目标）和大规模系统中的训练困难（维度灾难）等挑战。虽然LLM已被引入网约车系统，但目前的方法既不支持车辆共享（导致状态和动作空间指数级扩展），又需要频繁的LLM推理调用，无法实时部署。

Method: RideSkill采用层次化架构，包含三个核心组件：1）组合器（combiner），从可学习的技能库中为每辆车分配合适的技能，实现不同场景和目标下的自适应调度；2）重定位器（repositioner），顺序地将空闲车辆重定位到新兴需求区域，避免车辆间冲突；3）技能库、组合器与重定位器均由基于LLM的自动进化方法训练，部署时无需LLM调用。

Result: 论文提出的RideSkill通过LLM辅助的自动算法设计，在不需推理时LLM调用的情况下实现了对拼车系统的优化调度，解决了现有方法在实时部署和车辆共享支持方面的不足。

Conclusion: RideSkill为拼车调度提供了一种结合LLM自动化设计优势与实时部署可行性的新方案，通过将LLM用于离线训练而非在线决策，兼顾了算法适应性和系统实时性。

Abstract: Ride-sharing, which allows multiple passengers with different origin-destination (OD) pairs to share a single vehicle, is a challenging operational problem, as it requires orders with different OD pairs to be efficiently bundled and assigned to vehicles under uncertain and varying scenarios. Although multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) solutions have achieved promising performance, they suffer from limited generalization (adapting to different environmental scenarios), low transferability (adapting to different platform objectives), and training difficulties in large-scale systems, such as the curse of dimensionality. Recently, motivated by the scaling of large language models (LLMs), several works have incorporated LLMs into ride-hailing systems, either by employing LLMs directly as decision-making agents or using them for automatic algorithm design. However, none of these approaches support vehicle sharing, which complicates the problem by expanding both the state and action spaces exponentially. Moreover, most of them require frequent LLM calls at inference time, making them infeasible for real-time deployment. To address these issues, we propose RideSkill, a hierarchical method for ride-sharing that leverages LLM-assisted automatic algorithmic design. RideSkill consists of a combiner that assigns appropriate skills to each vehicle from a learned skill repository, enabling adaptive dispatch under varying scenarios and objectives, and a repositioner that sequentially relocates idle vehicles to emerging regions, avoiding conflicts among vehicles. Crucially, the skill repository, combiner, and repositioner are all trained by an LLM-based automatic evolutionary method, eliminating the need for LLM calls during deployment and thus ensuring high real-time performance.

</details>


### [277] [Competitive Market Behavior of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02580)
*Pawel Struski,Jakub Swistak,Inez Okulska,Przemyslaw Biecek*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文通过复现经典经济学实验，将人类被试替换为LLM智能体，在双向拍卖市场中检验LLM代理是否适合参与为人类设计的市场机制。研究发现，由LLM智能体构成的市场向均衡收敛的速度更慢甚至不收敛，资源配置效率低于人类市场；同时分析了不同模型家族和角色之间的异质性，并通过链式思维（CoT）的词汇分析揭示了交易决策背后的认知转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地被部署为经济代理，但目前缺乏证据表明LLM代理是否适合参与为人类设计的市场机制，以及这些机制在面对LLM代理时能否产生预期结果。这是一个全新的对齐维度检验。

Method: 复现经典经济学实验，将人类被试替换为LLM代理，将其置于广泛应用的双向拍卖市场机制中；分析个体交易决策的异质性；对代理生成的Chain-of-Thought（CoT）轨迹进行词汇分析，并公开发布测试框架。

Result: LLM代理构成的市场向均衡的收敛速度较慢或完全不收敛，资源配置效率低于人类市场；代理个体决策在不同模型家族和市场角色间存在显著异质性；CoT分析发现，执行交易而非逐步调整价格的决定与从策略考虑转向紧迫感的转变相关。

Conclusion: LLM代理与基础市场机制之间存在兼容性问题，需要进一步研究和改进LLM代理在市场经济场景中的表现；研究框架已公开，可用于未来评估。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as economic agents, yet there is little evidence whether LLM agents are suited for participating in market mechanisms designed for humans, and whether these mechanisms deliver desired outcomes when faced with LLM agents. We address this question by replicating seminal economic experiments, replacing human subjects with LLM agents. We place agents in a double auction environment, which is a widely-used market mechanism. We check whether such a market is able to deliver an efficient allocation of resources, thereby testing a novel dimension of alignment of LLM agents -- their compatibility with a fundamental market mechanism. We find that markets populated by LLM agents exhibit slower or no convergence towards market equilibrium, thus providing less efficient allocations than markets populated by humans. We then analyze agents' individual trading decisions and find substantial heterogeneity both across model families and market roles. We also run a lexical analysis of Chain-of-Thought (CoT) traces generated by the agents. We find that the decision to execute a trade rather than continue incrementally adjusting prices is associated with a shift from strategic considerations toward urgency. We publicly release our testing framework, which can be used for future evaluations.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [278] [Barriers to Using Static Application Security Testing (SAST) Tools: A Literature Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01669)
*Zachary Wadhams,Clemente Izurieta,Ann Marie Reinhold*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文调查了近期文献，旨在揭示开发者对静态应用安全测试（SAST）工具心存顾虑的原因及使用中遇到的具体问题。研究发现SAST存在多种可用性问题，既有工具固有限制（需要开发者投入），也有工具本身的缺陷（需SAST工具开发者解决）。作者认为，推动SAST广泛采用需要开发者接受必要的投入，同时SAST工具创建者需简化工具使用相关环节，兼顾技术与人为因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件漏洞是导致安全事件的主要原因，而SAST工具虽能帮助开发者识别和修复安全漏洞，但许多开发团队并未将其集成到开发环境中。作者希望通过文献调研找出开发者对SAST工具产生顾虑的具体原因，以推动SAST的广泛采用。

Method: 对近期相关文献进行系统综述（survey），梳理并分类开发者在使用SAST工具时遇到的各类可用性问题，区分工具固有限制与工具自身缺陷两类问题。

Result: 研究发现开发者面临多种SAST可用性问题：一部分源于工具固有属性，需要开发者投入一定的学习与使用成本；另一部分则属于SAST工具自身的设计缺陷，需要工具研发方改进解决。

Conclusion: 要实现SAST的广泛采用和持续使用，开发者需要接受一定的投入成本，同时SAST工具创建者应简化工具使用体验。克服SAST采纳的主要障碍必须综合考虑技术因素和人为因素两个方面。

Abstract: Developers face a challenging problem with no clear solution. Modern software breaches can wreak havoc on businesses and individuals alike. With code vulnerabilities being a leading cause, securing applications must be a priority for developers. Static Application Security Testing (SAST) has the potential to harden applications by assisting in the identification and resolution of security vulnerabilities. Despite this, many development teams have not adopted SAST tools into their environment. In this paper, we survey the recent literature to uncover why some developers are apprehensive towards SAST and identify what specific problems they encounter when using it. We found a variety of usability problems developers face when using SAST. Some are inherent of the tool and ultimately require some level of developer investment while others are tool shortcomings that SAST tool creators must address. Ultimately, we argue that in order to drive widespread adoption and consistent SAST usage, developers will need to embrace that some investment is required. Simultaneously, developers will be more likely to integrate SAST tools into their workflows if the creators of SAST tools simplify many aspects related to tool usage. Surmounting the primary obstacles preventing the adoption of SAST requires full consideration of both the technical and human factors.

</details>


### [279] [RosettaBitcoin: An Artifact-Backed Experience Report on Verification Infrastructure for Agent-Assisted Consensus Validators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01702)
*Donavon Guyot*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文档报告了RosettaBitcoin项目在2026年6月17日不可变快照上的审计结果。该项目是一个单人开发者使用Agent辅助构建的、包含12个独立实现的Bitcoin测试网4共识验证器。分析发现所有端口均通过端口自有的45/45脚本语料证明和严格的5,000区块基线，9个端口具备规范的50,000/100,000/后100,000区块验证通道，但无一满足二进制全节点门禁，也不存在空状态到顶点的完整证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Agent辅助软件项目的报告多依赖演示或聚合基准，掩盖了正确性声明是如何被验证的。本文通过审阅一个保留完整证据数据库的特定项目快照，探究Agent辅助系统在真实开发场景中的可审计性与证据透明度问题。

Method: 对RosettaBitcoin项目的不可变软件快照（DOI 10.5281/zenodo.20738249）进行静态取证分析，检查其SQLite证据数据库、策划工件索引、一致性测试夹具、验证脚本、阻断记录和版本历史，并对诊断补充物（纯Mojo密码学后端）的证据进行独立评估。

Result: 12个端口均通过端口自有的45/45脚本语料证明和5,000区块基线；9个具有规范的多阶段验证通道；Java产生了一个19.86秒的近顶维护工件。没有端口拥有空状态到顶点证明或满足二进制全节点门禁。Zig脚手架到50,000区块的记录间隔（3小时17分57秒）被判定为非等价任务且无法估计生产力。纯Mojo后端证据被归类为非规范、不可比且类别受限。

Conclusion: 显式的失败记录、测试夹具、端口自有证明和验证导入机制能够提升Agent辅助系统的可审计性，但需要受控消融实验和外部复制研究来确认这类基础设施是否对开发结果产生因果性改进。

Abstract: Agent-assisted software projects are often reported through demonstrations or aggregate benchmarks that conceal how correctness claims were admitted. This experience report studies RosettaBitcoin, a single-developer project that built twelve separately implemented Bitcoin testnet4 consensus validators, through its immutable 17 June 2026 software snapshot (DOI 10.5281/zenodo.20738249). We analyze the snapshot's tracked SQLite evidence database, curated artifact index, conformance fixtures, validation scripts, blocker records, and version history. At the snapshot, all twelve ports had port-owned 45/45 script-corpus proofs and strict 5,000-block baselines. Nine had canonical clean 50,000-block, 100,000-block, and post-100,000 validation lanes. Java had one 19.86-second near-tip maintenance artifact. No port had an empty-state-to-tip proof, and no port satisfied the project's binary full-node gate; Docker and live-node capability gaps remained.
  The artifact history also records a 3 h 17 min 57 s Zig scaffold-to-50,000 span, but the observed intervals describe non-equivalent tasks and cannot estimate effort, productivity, or causality. A separate diagnostic supplement preserves evidence that a pure-Mojo cryptographic backend validated fresh state to height 100,000 and resumed to 140,234, agreed on a 45-case shadow comparison, rejected six crafted invalid classes, and was killed by three targeted mutations. That evidence is noncanonical, noncomparable, and class-bounded. The case suggests that explicit failure records, fixtures, port-owned proofs, and validating imports can make agent-assisted systems more auditable. Controlled ablations and external replications are needed to test whether such infrastructure causally improves development outcomes.

</details>


### [280] [Harness Engineering in LLM Tool Use via Agent-Native Reusable Tool Primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01736)
*Haibo Jin,Suijin Wang,Xucheng Yu,Haojing Luo,Haohan Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Tool Primitives（以自然语言替代API schema作为工具调用接口）和ToolFace（包含25,519个函数的大型工具仓库），并在此基础上构建HEART框架（包含Planner、Router和Verifier），解决LLM工具调用中的多步推理脆弱性和大规模工具目录性能下降问题。实验显示HEART在五个基准上平均超越SFT模型10%，超越GPT-5.4等前沿商业模型6%，API成本降低85%，在50个真实任务上完成率达84%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM工具增强方法面临两大关键挑战：一是多步多轮推理脆弱，源于工具输出类型和API schema不兼容；二是在大规模工具目录下性能严重退化。这阻碍了LLM在复杂真实任务中的应用。

Method: 提出三个核心组件：(1) Tool Primitives——用自然语言作为工具调用接口，每个工具内置LLM接口处理schema解析和执行，支持嵌套和多轮工具调用；(2) ToolFace——集中式工具仓库（25,519个函数），LLM在推理时动态检索相关工具而非在上下文中枚举全部API；(3) HEART编排框架——包含Planner（规划）、Router（路由）和Verifier（验证）三模块，支持动态工具调用规划、多步执行和基于反馈的恢复机制。

Result: 在五个基准测试上，HEART平均超越SFT模型10%，并分别超越GPT-5.4、Claude-4.6-Sonnet和Gemini-3.1-Pro平均6%，API成本降低最高达85%。在50个真实世界任务上，HEART实现84%任务完成率，是三个前沿商业模型平均值（22%）的3.8倍。

Conclusion: 通过自然语言接口替代刚性API schema调用、结合动态工具检索与三模块编排框架，HEART有效解决了多步推理脆弱性和大规模工具目录下的性能退化问题，显著提升了LLM工具调用的可靠性、效率和复杂任务完成能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) augmented with external tools have demonstrated remarkable capability in solving complex real-world tasks. However, existing approaches suffer from two key challenges: brittle multi-step and multi-turn reasoning caused by incompatible tool output types and API schemas, and performance degradation under large tool catalogues. To address these, we introduce \textbf{Tool Primitives}, a design that replaces rigid API schema-based invocation with natural language as the interface for tool calling, where each tool is wrapped with an LLM interface that handles schema resolution and execution internally, enabling natural inter-tool communication for nested and multi-turn tool calling. Building on Tool Primitives, we host \textbf{ToolFace}, a centralized repository of 25,519 functions from which LLMs dynamically retrieve only the relevant tools at inference time, eliminating the need to enumerate raw API schemas in context. To orchestrate Tool Primitives and ToolFace reliably in complex settings, we further propose \textbf{HEART}, a \textbf{H}arness \textbf{E}ngineering framework via \textbf{A}gent-native, \textbf{R}eusable \textbf{T}ool Primitives, comprising a Planner, Router, and Verifier that jointly support dynamic tool invocation planning, multi-step execution, and feedback-driven recovery.
  Experiments on five benchmarks demonstrate that HEART outperforms SFT-based models by $10\%$ on average and surpasses GPT-5.4, Claude-4.6-Sonnet, and Gemini-3.1-Pro by $6\%$ on average while reducing API cost by up to $85\%$. On 50 real-world tasks, HEART achieves $84\%$ task completion, $3.8\times$ the average of three frontier commercial models ($22\%$).

</details>


### [281] [From Silicon to Boot Code: Extending Automated Program Repair to Firmware-Layer Security Workarounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01769)
*Maisha Mastora,Dean Sullivan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了将自动化程序修复（APR）方法从RTL层面扩展到固件（UEFI/EDK II）层面的可行性。通过自动化commit聚类挖掘器在EDK II仓库全量历史中发现重复修复模板，构建了四种独立定位器（覆盖Spectre v1、解压缩库CVE、Spectre v2等），全部达到100%召回率，并将C语言家族的误报根因归因于两个过程内原因（占77-90%）。该研究主张建立统一的硬件到固件正确性生命周期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有APR研究局限于设计阶段（RTL/HLS），一旦芯片流片后出现硬件漏洞，补丁合成仍需人工完成——现有自动化只解决补丁部署而非补丁合成。作者希望验证能否将最初为RTL修复开发的"定位-合成-验证"APR方法扩展应用到固件层，以弥补硅后漏洞修复的自动化空白。

Method: 1) 构建自动化commit聚类挖掘器，在EDK II（UEFI）固件仓库全量commit历史中发现重复修复模板（不依赖已知CVE标识）；2) 基于挖掘出的真实修复证据，构建四个独立定位器：C语言中缺失推测屏障（CVE-2017-5753/Spectre v1）、C语言中数组写入前缺失边界检查（解压库CVE）、x86汇编中缺失RSB填充（CVE-2017-5715）、Hand-Off Block创建代码中缺失整数溢出保护；3) 对C语言家族误报进行根因分析，区分过程内与跨过程别名分析缺陷的贡献；4) 通过留出测试集、新增字典条目（CVE-2018-3630）和朴素语法基线对比验证方法的泛化性。

Result: 四个定位器均达到100%召回率，精度在C语言家族为2.1-15.5%，汇编和HOB家族为100%。C语言家族误报的77-90%归因于两个过程内原因，跨过程别名分析缺口被实测为15-20%。留出测试确认Spectre v1在未见文件上保持100%召回；第五个独立字典条目（CVE-2018-3630）证明方法可低成本扩展到新漏洞签名；朴素语法基线最多只能召回14%，远低于定位器的100%。

Conclusion: 字典引导的定位-合成-验证APR方法论能够从RTL层面有效扩展到固件层面。该方法发现了全部三个已知CVE修复活动并额外挖掘出两个候选bug家族，证明了其发现能力。作者主张以此为基础建立统一的硬件到固件正确性生命周期研究议程。

Abstract: Automated program repair (APR) research has been constrained to design time. Current techniques localize and fix bugs in RTL or HLS designs before a chip reaches production. Once a hardware vulnerability surfaces post-silicon, the patch must be manually generated: existing automation addresses patch deployment but not patch synthesis. We study the feasibility of extending a dictionary-guided, localize-synthesize-validate APR methodology originally developed for RTL repair to this firmware layer. An automated commit-clustering miner surfaces recurring fix templates across the EDK II (UEFI) firmware repository's full commit history without depending on known CVE identifiers, recovering all three known CVE-fix campaigns and surfacing two additional candidate bug families. Grounded in real fix evidence, we build four independent localizers: missing speculation barriers in C (CVE-2017-5753, Spectre v1), missing bounds checks before array writes in C (a decompression library CVE), missing Return Stack Buffer stuffing in x86 assembly (CVE-2017-5715), and missing integer-overflow guards in Hand-Off Block creation code (surfaced by the miner itself). All four achieve 100% recall; precision ranges from 2.1-15.5% on the C families to 100% on the assembly and HOB families. Root-cause analysis of the C-family false positives attributes 77-90% to two intra-procedural causes, isolating the inter-procedural alias-analysis gap as a measured 15-20% rather than an estimate. A held-out test confirms Spectre v1 localization holds at 100% recall on unseen files; a fifth, independently built dictionary entry (CVE-2018-3630) shows the methodology extends to a new bug signature at low cost; and a naive syntactic baseline recalls at most 14% where our detector recalls 100%. We frame these results within a broader research agenda for a unified hardware-to-firmware correctness lifecycle.

</details>


### [282] [Modelstamp: Pre-Deserialization Verification of Machine-Learning Artifacts and Runtime Environment State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01781)
*Anagha Dhekne*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Modelstamp是一个轻量级Python持久化库，在反序列化之前验证ML模型的工件完整性和运行环境状态，通过SHA-256摘要和JSON manifest检查来解决环境漂移中的验证问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型在加载时其软件环境可能已发生变化，即使模型工件字节完全一致也无法通过工件完整性检查发现环境漂移带来的风险，需要一种在反序列化前验证表示环境状态的方法。

Method: 在持久化时将序列化工件与包含SHA-256摘要、运行时元数据和已安装包版本信息的sidecar JSON manifest关联；记录一个模型相关子集用于漂移比较；可选HMAC认证支持共享密钥场景；在验证时检查工件和当前环境后再反序列化模型。

Result: 在14个受控环境漂移场景、8个受控信任边界场景及10MiB到1GiB的工件规模基准中评估。漂移实验按预期工作；验证时间从10MiB的0.032s增加到1GiB的3.334s，吞吐量约307-312MiB/s。

Conclusion: Modelstamp被定位为反序列化前的参考状态验证控制手段，是对依赖管理系统、恶意模型检测、安全反序列化等现有方法的补充而非替代。

Abstract: Persisted machine-learning models can remain byte-identical while the software environments in which they are loaded evolve, creating a verification problem that artifact integrity checks alone cannot expose. This paper presents Modelstamp, a lightweight Python persistence library for verifying artifact integrity and represented runtime-environment state before deserialization. At persistence time, Modelstamp associates a serialized artifact with a sidecar JSON manifest containing a SHA-256 digest, runtime metadata, and installed versions from a bounded tracked-package set; a separately recorded model-relevant subset determines which package versions participate in drift comparison. Optional HMAC authentication supports workflows in which the producer and verifier share a secret key. At verification time, the artifact and represented current environment are checked against this recorded evidence before the model is deserialized. Modelstamp is evaluated using 14 controlled environment-drift scenarios, eight controlled trust-boundary scenarios, and an artifact-size scaling benchmark from 10 MiB to 1 GiB. The controlled drift experiments behaved as specified across relevant dependency changes, unchanged environments, and unrelated environmental changes, including broader noise controls. The trust-boundary experiments similarly confirmed both intended detections and expected limitations, including shared-key forgery and replay. Median verification time increased from 0.032 s at 10 MiB to 3.334 s at 1 GiB, with measured throughput of approximately 307-312 MiB/s in the benchmark environment. These results characterize Modelstamp as a complementary pre-deserialization reference-state verification control rather than as a replacement for dependency-management systems, malicious-model detection, safe deserialization, or public publisher authentication.

</details>


### [283] [ExecRetrieval: Measuring the Functional-Correctness Gap in Code-Embedding Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01865)
*Aaryan Kapoor,Md Abdullah Al Hafiz Khan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出ExecRetrieval基准——939个Python任务，每个任务配有一个经过执行验证的正确规范实现和最多四个执行验证的有缺陷近克隆干扰项。评测23种密集嵌入配置及BM25后发现，在近克隆反事实存在时，系统exec@1仅达0.331，导致排名第一的漏检中91.5-99.4%是配对的错误变体，说明现有嵌入无法在检索层面对正确代码与近克隆但错误的代码进行功能区分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码检索基准未在搜索池中植入受控的、经执行验证的单编辑变体（即有缺陷的近克隆实现），导致无法回答"嵌入能否在功能上区分正确代码与近似克隆但不正确的代码"这一关键问题。检索正确代码比检索词法相似代码更重要，需要专门基准来测试检索器的功能判别能力。

Method: 构建ExecRetrieval基准：939个Python任务，每个任务配一个执行验证的正确规范实现，并通过机械突变生成最多四个执行验证的有缺陷干扰项（每个仅做单一目标编辑）。使用23种密集嵌入配置和BM25进行提供方原生调用评测，辅以配对McNemar检验和查询级自助法区间。

Result: 在搜索池包含近克隆反事实的情况下，顶尖托管系统exec@10=1.00但exec@1仅0.331；排名第一的漏检中91.5-99.4%是配对的错误变体（四大领先系统）；领先系统在67-78%的查询中，规范实现的得分低于其至少一个配对干扰项。

Conclusion: 即便在较高的top-10召回率下，现有嵌入模型在top-1精确检索中无法稳定区分功能正确的代码与其近克隆但有缺陷的变体，说明功能感知的代码嵌入与检索仍是一个未解决的关键挑战。数据集、执行预言机、嵌入矩阵等资源已公开发布。

Abstract: Embedding-based code retrieval is a core component of coding agents and retrieval-augmented code generation, where retrieving correct code matters more than retrieving lexically similar code. Existing code-retrieval benchmarks do not plant controlled, execution-verified single-edit variants of each query's canonical implementation in the search pool, leaving the question of whether embeddings can functionally discriminate correct from near-clone-but-incorrect code unanswered in a retrieval setting. Resolving this requires a benchmark whose search pool itself contains the relevant counterfactuals -- execution-verified buggy variants near-identical to each canonical -- so that a retriever's rank ordering can be directly tested for functional discrimination rather than topical or identity overlap. We introduce ExecRetrieval, 939 Python tasks each paired with one execution-verified canonical implementation and up to four execution-verified buggy distractors, each generated by a mechanical mutation making a single targeted edit, and evaluate 23 dense embedding configurations plus BM25 under provider-native invocation with paired McNemar tests and query-level bootstrap intervals. With near-clone counterfactuals in the pool, the top hosted system reaches exec@10 = 1.00 but only exec@1 = 0.331; rank-1 misses are paired buggy variants 91.5-99.4% of the time across the four leading systems, and the canonical scores below at least one of its four paired distractors in 67-78% of queries on the leading systems. The full dataset, execution oracle, embedding matrices, environment snapshot, and pairwise statistical tests are released at the URL in Appendix D.

</details>


### [284] [From Prompting to Engineering: A Research Agenda for Prompt Engineering in Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02248)
*Vincenzo De Martino,Giovanna Broccia,Fabiano Pecorelli,Jennifer Horkoff,Riccardo Coppola,Antonino Ferraro,Quim Motger,Emma McKenzie,Shahbaz Siddeeq*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文基于PROMPT-SE研讨会（EASE 2026）的社区讨论，总结了提示工程在软件工程中的应用现状与挑战，将讨论成果归纳为五个关键领域，并提出研究议程以推动提示工程从临时性实践向系统化、可维护、可评估、可追溯和可治理的SE实践转型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提示工程虽已广泛应用于软件工程各活动（如需求分析、编码、测试、文档等），但提示及相关指令工件通常以任务特定和非正式的方式创建和演进，缺乏系统的评估、管理、可追溯性和治理支持。作者希望通过社区讨论探索SE如何促进这些实践的成熟化。

Method: 在首届国际软件工程经验提示工程研讨会（PROMPT-SE，与EASE 2026联办）上组织了结构化社区讨论，收集参与者对当前提示实践、采用与评估挑战以及未来方向的看法，并对讨论结果进行综合归纳。

Result: 将讨论综合为五个领域：（1）提示工件与标准化；（2）评估与基准测试；（3）生命周期集成；（4）人机协作与技能；（5）治理、隐私与技术债务。基于这些领域提出了研究议程。

Conclusion: 提示工程需要从以临时交互为主的实践向更系统化、可维护、可评估、可追溯和可治理的软件工程实践演进，本文提出的研究议程为这一转型提供了方向指引。

Abstract: Prompt engineering is increasingly used across Software Engineering (SE) activities, including requirements analysis, coding, testing, documentation, repository analysis, and planning. Yet prompts and related instruction artifacts are often created and evolved through task-specific and informal practices, with limited support for their systematic evaluation, management, traceability, and governance. To examine how SE can contribute to the maturation of these practices, we organized a structured community discussion at the First International Workshop on Empirical Prompt Engineering for Software Engineering (PROMPT-SE), co-located with EASE 2026. Participants discussed current prompting practices, challenges to their adoption and evaluation, and future directions for integrating prompt engineering into software development. We synthesized these discussions into five areas: prompt artifacts and standardization; evaluation and benchmarking; lifecycle integration; human-AI collaboration and skills; and governance, privacy, and technical debt. Based on these areas, we outline a research agenda to move prompt engineering from predominantly ad hoc interactions toward more systematic, maintainable, evaluable, traceable, and governable SE practices.

</details>


### [285] [AgOSS: A Dataset and Multi-Layer Characterization of Open-Source Agricultural Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02591)
*Vatsal Dudhaiya,Mikhail Golovenchits,Aryan Banerjee,James C. Davis*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出AgOSS数据集，包含66个农业开源软件仓库，评估其供应链安全态势并与非农业对照软件比较，发现农业领域的治理水平与依赖风险无显著关联，且安全分数差距主要由项目规模与成熟度造成而非农业领域特殊性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业大量依赖开源软件形成领域特定的软件供应链，但该生态系统的供应链安全状况鲜有实证研究关注。作者希望探究农业开源生态的安全态势是否与非农业软件存在差异，以及这种差异是否源于农业领域本身的特性，还是仅仅反映项目规模和成熟度的不同。

Method: 构建AgOSS数据集（66个仓库，覆盖6种架构类别），采用OpenSSF Scorecard进行供应链安全评分，结合治理指标、基于SBOM的依赖分析以及KEV（已知可利用漏洞）匹配来综合评估；同时选取匹配的非农业对照项目进行比较，并使用匹配与回归方法控制混杂因素。

Result: 发现两点：第一，治理水平与继承的依赖风险基本无关，Scorecard反映社区活跃度，但与漏洞数量、密度或已知被利用漏洞数量无关联，回归中暴露信号落在架构类别而非领域上；第二，农业项目在原始Scorecard上得分显著较低，但规模和成熟度混淆了这一差距，在匹配和回归后残差失去显著性。

Conclusion: 保障农业开源软件生态的安全应着重投入于贡献者能力建设和依赖卫生管理，而非开发农业特有的安全控制措施。

Abstract: Much of agriculture depends on open-source software spanning farm management platforms, cloud services, edge gateways, embedded systems, and field-deployed sensors, forming a domain-specific software supply chain that has drawn little empirical security attention. It is unknown whether this ecosystem's supply chain security posture differs from that of comparable non-agricultural software, and if it does, whether the difference reflects the agricultural domain or the size and maturity of the projects within it.
  As a step towards securing agricultural open-source software, we present AgOSS, a dataset of 66 repositories across six architectural categories. We assess supply chain security within the dataset via OpenSSF Scorecard, governance metrics, SBOM-based dependency analysis, and KEV matching, and compare against matched non-agricultural controls. We report two findings. First, governance is largely independent of inherited dependency risk. Scorecard tracks community activity, but we detect no association with vulnerability count, density, or known-exploited count, and in regression the exposure signal loads on architectural category rather than domain. Second, agricultural projects score far lower on raw Scorecard, but size and maturity confound the gap: the residual loses significance under matching and regression. Securing this ecosystem means investing in contributor capacity and dependency hygiene, not agriculture-specific controls.

</details>


### [286] [Automated Vulnerability Injection in Smart Contracts Using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02624)
*Luca Migliaccio,Roberto Natella,Naghmeh Ivaki,Nuno Laranjeiro,Marco Vieira*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出利用大型语言模型（LLM）自动向Solidity智能合约注入漏洞以构建基准数据集的方法。通过多步骤验证管道处理近千个候选变体，最终获得32个覆盖25种漏洞类型的已验证漏洞合约（存活率16.58%），并用这些合约评估了三个静态分析器。结果表明此方法可行，但存在可扩展性和多样性方面的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估智能合约漏洞检测工具需要具有已知真实标签的数据集，然而此类数据集稀缺且手工构建困难，亟需自动化方法。

Method: 利用LLM自动向Solidity合约注入漏洞，针对OpenSCV中的49种漏洞类型进行案例研究；通过编译检查、执行验证、业务逻辑验证和漏洞存在性验证的多步骤管道对注入合约进行筛选；在SmartBugs真实合约数据上进行验证，并使用获得的合约评估三个静态分析器。

Result: LLM生成近1000个候选变体，经去重和验证后，32个覆盖25种漏洞类型的已验证漏洞合约得以保留（存活率16.58%）；存活合约集中在结构较简单的目标和具有局部化语法模式的漏洞类型；评估三个静态分析器发现其覆盖范围互补且不完整。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的漏洞注入方法在智能合约漏洞数据集构建方面具有可行性，但需解决LLM非确定性和合约语义保持等挑战，并在可扩展性与多样性方面仍有改进空间。

Abstract: Assessing vulnerability detection tools for smart contracts requires datasets with known ground truth, yet such datasets are scarce and difficult to build by hand. We propose an approach that uses Large Language Models (LLMs) to automatically inject vulnerabilities into Solidity smart contracts, and demonstrate it in a case study targeting 49 vulnerability types from OpenSCV. Injected contracts are validated through a multi-step pipeline checking compilation, execution, business logic, and the presence of the intended vulnerability. Applied to real-world contracts from SmartBugs, LLMs generate nearly 1,000 candidate variants; after deduplication and validation, 32 confirmed vulnerable contracts spanning 25 vulnerability types survive (a 16.58% survival rate). Surviving contracts concentrate in structurally simpler targets and vulnerability types with localized syntactic patterns. We report practical challenges including LLMs' non-determinism and the difficulty of preserving contract semantics. We then use the validated contracts to assess three static analyzers, revealing complementary and incomplete coverage profiles. Results show that LLM-based vulnerability injection is feasible, while exposing key limitations in scalability and diversity.

</details>


### [287] [The Import Tax: A Longitudinal Measurement of Startup Cost in the Python Ecosystem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02753)
*Trinath Sai Subhash Reddy Pittala*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对PyPI上500个最常下载的Python包进行了系统性的导入成本测量，覆盖5年（季度采样）、6个CPython版本、2个平台共63,431项测量，并实测了Python 3.15的全局惰性导入模式。研究发现导入成本高度偏斜（中位数<6ms但P99达354ms），首次导入因字节码编译贵3-22倍，子模块导入比顶层导入贵至多294倍；成本随时间增长集中在重尾（均值年增11-13%），新解释器在macOS上慢1.16倍但Linux上无差异，单点版本号变动可造成1.34x差异；全局惰性模式使导入几乎零成本，但破坏了414个顶级包中的8个。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Python 3.15新增显式惰性导入（PEP 810）主要基于轶事证据，生态系统的导入成本缺乏系统性测量。作者希望提供首个大规模、跨版本、跨平台的导入成本实证数据，为PEP 810等优化决策提供依据。

Method: 选取PyPI最常下载的500个包，按季度采样五年内的版本，在6个CPython版本（3.9-3.14）和2个平台（Apple M5/macOS、Intel Xeon/Linux）上进行测量，共63,431次测量；并直接测量Python 3.15的全局惰性导入模式的性能与兼容性影响。

Result: 导入成本严重偏斜：中位数<6ms但P99为354ms；首次导入比后续导入贵3-22倍；子模块导入成本最高比顶层导入贵294倍。成本逐年增长集中重尾（均值+11-13%/年，中位数仅+1.6-2.4%/年）。macOS上新解释器慢1.16x但Linux不受影响；3.11.5vs3.11.16单点版本差1.34x。全局惰性模式使导入近乎免费，但破坏414个顶级包中的8个。

Conclusion: 导入成本是Python生态中被严重低估的性能问题，尤其影响CLI工具、测试worker和服务端无服务器冷启动。PEP 810的惰性导入方案有明确的数据支撑但非零风险（兼容性破坏约2%的顶级包），需要权衡布署。

Abstract: Python programs pay for their imports at every process start, a cost that is invisible in steady-state benchmarks but dominant for command-line tools, test workers, and serverless cold starts. Python 3.15 adds explicit lazy imports (PEP 810) largely on anecdotal evidence; no systematic measurement of the ecosystem's import cost exists. We present one: the 500 most-downloaded PyPI packages, sampled quarterly over five years of releases, measured under six CPython versions (3.9-3.14) on two platforms (Apple M5/macOS and Intel Xeon/Linux), for 63,431 measurements in total, plus direct measurement of 3.15's global lazy-import mode. Import cost is heavily skewed: half of packages import in under 6 ms, but the 99th percentile is 354 ms, the first import after installation costs 3-22x more (bytecode compilation), and importing a package's submodules costs up to 294x more than the top-level import that benchmarks report. The median package's cost grows only +1.6-2.4%/year, but the mean grows +11-13%/year: growth is concentrated in a heavy tail. Newer interpreters import the same code 1.16x slower on macOS, but not on Linux, and a single point release (3.11.5 vs. 3.11.16) swings cost by 1.34x. Global lazy mode makes import statements essentially free, yet breaks 8 of 414 top packages. Harness and dataset are available on request.

</details>


### [288] [Type Hints in Python Libraries and Frameworks: An Empirical Analysis of Adoption and Maintenance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02782)
*Thiago Roberto Magalhães,Fabio Petrillo,João Eduardo Montandon*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过分析1,000个热门GitHub仓库，研究了Python库和框架中类型提示的采用、使用、维护及动机，发现91%的库至少使用一次类型提示，但采用不一致；系统使用时主要服务于API契约而非实现细节，注释倾向于向更具表现力的类型演进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然Python类型提示已被广泛使用，但关于库和框架中类型提示的采用和维护方式知之甚少，缺乏系统性的实证研究。

Method: 分析了1,000个热门GitHub仓库，识别出库和框架并提取其类型注解，检查了注解覆盖率、位置和来源、随git历史的演进，以及开发者注解与Pyright推断类型之间的关系。

Result: 91%的库至少使用一次类型提示，但采用不一致；系统使用的库中函数参数和返回类型覆盖率中位数分别为45.8%和35.9%，主要使用内建类型(73.0%)；修改时注解倾向于迁移到更具表现力的类型；开发者即使在Pyright能推断类型时也会注解成员，且这些注解通常简化了推断类型。

Conclusion: Python库和框架中的类型提示主要作为API契约而非实现细节的全面描述，这为优先关注公共接口、识别有意义的注解变更以及支持维护者演进类型信息的工具提供了机会。

Abstract: Context: In Python, type hints allow developers to annotate variables and functions with explicit type information, improving code clarity and reliability. Although type hints are widely available, little is known about how they are adopted and maintained in libraries and frameworks. Objective: We investigate the adoption, usage, maintenance, and rationale of type hints in Python libraries and frameworks. Method: We analyzed 1,000 popular GitHub repositories, identifying libraries and frameworks and extracting their type annotations. We examined annotation coverage, the locations and origins of annotations, their evolution across git histories, and the relationship between developer annotations and types inferred by Pyright. Results: Of the analyzed repositories, 91% of libraries use type hints at least once, although adoption is inconsistent. Among libraries with systematic usage, maintainers prioritize function parameters and return types, with median coverage of 45.8% and 35.9%, respectively, and mainly use built-in types (73.0%). When modified, annotations tend to migrate to more expressive types. Developers annotate members even when Pyright can infer their types, and these annotations often simplify the inferred type. Conclusion: Type hints in Python libraries and frameworks primarily serve as API contracts rather than comprehensive descriptions of implementation details. The findings suggest opportunities for tooling that prioritizes public interfaces, identifies meaningful annotation changes, and supports maintainers in evolving type information.

</details>


### [289] [ShikumiMiner: Mining Recurring Implementation Patterns in AI Codebases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02789)
*Afsana Tasnim,Sheikh Motahar Naim*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出ShikumiMiner，一个结合抽象语法树（AST）和控制流图（CFG）特征的静态分析框架，用于检测和比较C++本地LLM代码库中重复出现的实现模式，并通过多标签随机森林模型对十个GitHub开源仓库中的函数进行分类分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前开源社区中存在成千上万个LLM工程项目，但这些项目是否具有底层（可复用的）模式仍是一个未解问题。探索这些底层模式能够为致力于开发LLM项目的开发者提供新的维度与洞察。

Method: 提出ShikumiMiner静态分析框架，该框架将抽象语法树（AST）和控制流图（CFG）特征相结合，用于检测并比较C++本地LLM代码库中的重复实现模式。分析了十个GitHub开源仓库，并使用多标签随机森林（Random Forest）模型将函数分类为七个按研究需求定义的类别。

Result: 通过对十个开源仓库的分析，成功利用多标签随机森林模型完成了函数的七类别分类，识别出了LLM代码库中存在的重复性实现模式。

Conclusion: 研究这些模式可以为致力于设计LLM应用程序的开发者提供有价值的洞见，帮助理解LLM项目的结构特征并指导后续开发实践。

Abstract: Large language models are paving the way towards innovation by understanding, analyzing, summarizing and generating content in the modern world. Currently there are thousands of LLM projects developed by engineers in open-source repositories. However, whether these LLM projects have underlying patterns or not remains a question. Exploring these underlying patterns will give new dimensions to the developers who aim to develop these LLM projects. In this paper, we propose ShikumiMiner, a static-analysis framework that combines Abstract Syntax Tree (AST) and Control Flow Graph (CFG) features to detect and compare recurring implementation patterns in C++ local LLM codebases. We analyze ten GitHub open-source repositories and classify functions into seven study-specific categories using a multi-label Random Forest model. Studying these patterns can provide useful insights for developers aiming to design LLM applications.

</details>
